=== FILE: src/App.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Main Application Root Component
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { VaultProvider, useVault } from './context/VaultContext';
import { PatientProvider } from './context/PatientContext';
import { EpisodeProvider } from './context/EpisodeContext';
import { SessionProvider } from './context/SessionContext';
import { LanguageProvider, useLanguage } from './context/LanguageContext';
import { LockScreen } from './components/LockScreen';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { PatientDirectory } from './components/PatientDirectory';
import { PatientProfile } from './components/PatientProfile';
import { PatientModal } from './components/PatientModal';
import { SyntheticDataModal } from './components/SyntheticDataModal';
import { BackupRestoreModal } from './components/BackupRestoreModal';
import type { PatientRecord } from './types/clinical';
import { RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

const AppContent: React.FC = () => {
  const { ready, isLocked } = useVault();
  const { isRtl } = useLanguage();

  // Selected patient for profile view
  const [selectedPatient, setSelectedPatient] = useState<PatientRecord | null>(null);

  // Modals state
  const [isPatientModalOpen, setIsPatientModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [patientToEdit, setPatientToEdit] = useState<PatientRecord | null>(null);
  const [isSyntheticModalOpen, setIsSyntheticModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isBackupModalOpen, setIsBackupModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // STRICT SENSITIVE STATE WIPING: Clear all decrypted clinical state and active modals on lock
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLocked) {
      setSelectedPatient(null);
      setPatientToEdit(null);
      setIsPatientModalOpen(false);
      setIsSyntheticModalOpen(false);
      setIsBackupModalOpen(false);
    }
  }, [isLocked]);

  // Still checking IndexedDB metadata
  if (!ready) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-center items-center text-white" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
        <RefreshCw className="w-8 h-8 text-primary-500 animate-spin mb-3" />
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-300">جاري قراءة الخزينة المحلية والتحقق من التخزين...</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Vault is locked -> Show Lock Screen
  if (isLocked) {
    return <LockScreen />;
  }

  // Vault is unlocked -> Show Main Application
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col selection:bg-primary-500 selection:text-white" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      
      {/* Top Header */}
      <Navbar
        onOpenSyntheticModal={() => setIsSyntheticModalOpen(true)}
        onOpenBackupModal={() => setIsBackupModalOpen(true)}
        onOpenPatientModal={() => {
          setPatientToEdit(null);
          setIsPatientModalOpen(true);
        }}
      />

      {/* Main View: Profile View OR Directory View */}
      <main className="flex-1">
        {selectedPatient ? (
          <PatientProfile
            patient={selectedPatient}
            onBack={() => setSelectedPatient(null)}
            onEdit={(p) => {
              setPatientToEdit(p);
              setIsPatientModalOpen(true);
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          <PatientDirectory
            onSelectPatient={(p) => setSelectedPatient(p)}
            onOpenNewPatientModal={() => {
              setPatientToEdit(null);
              setIsPatientModalOpen(true);
            }}
            onOpenSyntheticModal={() => setIsSyntheticModalOpen(true)}
          />
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Modals */}
      <PatientModal
        isOpen={isPatientModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsPatientModalOpen(false)}
        patientToEdit={patientToEdit}
        onSavedSuccess={(savedRecord) => {
          // If editing currently selected patient, update profile view
          if (selectedPatient && selectedPatient.id === savedRecord.id) {
            setSelectedPatient(savedRecord);
          }
        }}
      />

      <SyntheticDataModal
        isOpen={isSyntheticModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsSyntheticModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <BackupRestoreModal
        isOpen={isBackupModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsBackupModalOpen(false)}
      />

    </div>
  );
};

export default function App() {
  return (
    <LanguageProvider>
      <VaultProvider>
        <PatientProvider>
          <EpisodeProvider>
            <SessionProvider>
              <AppContent />
            </SessionProvider>
          </EpisodeProvider>
        </PatientProvider>
      </VaultProvider>
    </LanguageProvider>
  );
}

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Clinical Domain Types & Database Entities (v1.1)
// =======================================================================

export type Gender = 'male' | 'female' | 'other';
export type DominantHand = 'right' | 'left' | 'ambidextrous';

export type EpisodeStatus = 'active' | 'completed' | 'on_hold' | 'archived';

export type SessionType = 
  | 'initial_evaluation'
  | 'follow_up'
  | 're_evaluation'
  | 'discharge_summary';

export type SessionStatus = 'draft' | 'finalized' | 'cancelled';

export type AttendanceStatus =
  | 'attended'
  | 'cancelled_patient'
  | 'cancelled_clinic'
  | 'no_show';

export type AddendumReason =
  | 'typographical_correction'
  | 'late_entry'
  | 'diagnostic_clarification'
  | 'adverse_event_late_documentation'
  | 'treatment_response_update';

export interface TerminologyInfo {
  en: string;
  ar: string;
  badgeClass?: string;
  descriptionAr?: string;
}

export type AssessmentCategory = 
  | 'rom'
  | 'mmt'
  | 'special_tests'
  | 'neurological'
  | 'cardiopulmonary'
  | 'functional'
  | 'outcome_measure'
  | 'custom';

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Decrypted Clinical Patient (In-Memory representation)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface PatientClinicalData {
  fullName: string;
  fullNameAr?: string;
  dateOfBirth?: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  age?: number;
  gender: Gender;
  phoneNumber?: string;
  address?: string;
  occupation?: string;
  dominantHand?: DominantHand;
  
  // Medical & Clinical History
  medicalHistory?: string;
  previousSurgeries?: string;
  currentMedications?: string;
  allergies?: string;
  comorbidities?: string;
  precautions?: string;
  redFlags?: string;
  psychosocialFactors?: string;
  generalNotes?: string;

  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Initial Assessment Snapshot (Phase 1: Patient Demographic Assessment)
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  // The fields above represent the CURRENT SUMMARY of demographic and medical
  // background data for this patient. They are updated via "تعديل الملف السريري"
  // and reflect the most recent clinician-entered summary.
  //
  // IMPORTANT: This interface does NOT track longitudinal clinical measurements
  // (ROM, MMT, VAS scores, etc.). Those time-series measurements are recorded
  // per-session via the SOAP and Assessment modules in Phase 2.
  //
  // The timestamp below records when this patient profile summary was last updated.
  profileSummaryUpdatedAt?: string; // ISO 8601 timestamp of last profile edit
}

export interface PatientRecord {
  id: string; // UUID v4
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601
  updatedAt: string; // ISO 8601
  isArchived: number; // 0 = active, 1 = archived
  data: PatientClinicalData; // Decrypted when in memory
}

// Stored in Dexie DB (Encrypted at rest with DEK + AAD)
export interface EncryptedPatientEntity {
  id: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  isArchived: number; // 0 or 1 for indexing
  recordCryptoVersion: number; // Immutable per-record encryption format (e.g. 1)
  dekVersion: number; // Version of DEK used to encrypt this record (e.g. 1)
  iv: Uint8Array; // 12-byte random AES-GCM IV
  ciphertext: Uint8Array;
}

export type ClinicalSpecialty =
  | 'musculoskeletal'
  | 'neurological'
  | 'cardiopulmonary'
  | 'pediatric'
  | 'sports'
  | 'general'
  | 'geriatric';

export type OnsetMechanism =
  | 'traumatic'
  | 'insidious'
  | 'post_surgical'
  | 'congenital';

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Episode of Care
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface EpisodeClinicalData {
  title: string;
  status?: EpisodeStatus; // Enclosed 100% within encrypted payload (defaults to 'active')
  primarySpecialty: ClinicalSpecialty; // Enclosed 100% within encrypted payload
  startDate?: string; // Enclosed 100% within encrypted payload (defaults to onsetDate or today)
  endDate?: string; // Enclosed 100% within encrypted payload
  referralSource?: string;
  medicalDiagnosis: string;
  icd10Code?: string;
  clinicalImpression: string;
  affectedBodyRegion: string;
  onsetMechanism?: OnsetMechanism;
  onsetDate?: string;
  rehabilitationGoals: {
    shortTerm: string[];
    longTerm: string[];
  };
  dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
  dischargeSummary?: string;
  statusNotes?: string;
  isOngoingLegacyEpisode?: boolean;
  knownVsUnknownHistoryFlags?: {
    isDiagnosisConfirmed: boolean;
    isOnsetDateExact: boolean;
    areBaselineGoalsFormalized: boolean;
  };
}

export interface EpisodeRecord {
  id: string;
  patientId: string;
  status: EpisodeStatus;
  primarySpecialty: ClinicalSpecialty;
  startDate: string; // ISO 8601
  endDate?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  data: EpisodeClinicalData;
}

export interface EncryptedEpisodeEntity {
  id: string;
  patientId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  recordCryptoVersion: number;
  dekVersion: number;
  iv: Uint8Array;
  ciphertext: Uint8Array;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Clinical Error Classes
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export class CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'لا يمكن حذف سجل سريري معتمد نهائياً. يجب الحفاظ على السجلات السريرية التاريخية.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError';
  }
}

export class PatientHardDeletionBlockedError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض معطل ومحظور وفق سياسة الأمان والاحتفاظ السريري.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'PatientHardDeletionBlockedError';
  }
}

export class VaultGenerationChangedError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = 'تم تغيير جيل الخزينة (Vault Generation Changed) بواسطة عملية استعادة كاملة أثناء العملية. تم إلغاء العملية لحماية اتساق التشفير وقاعدة البيانات.'
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'VaultGenerationChangedError';
  }
}

export class UnboundVaultKeyError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = 'تم رفض العملية السريرية: مفتاح التشفير التشغيلي (DEK) غير مرتبط بأي جيل خزينة معتمد (Unbound Vault Key). لا يمكن تنفيذ التشفير أو حجز الأرقام التسلسلية بمفتاح مجهول الجيل.'
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'UnboundVaultKeyError';
  }
}

export interface PatientDependentRecordsSummary {
  patientId: string;
  episodesCount: number;
  sessionsCount: number;
  soapNotesCount: number;
  clinicalAddendaCount: number;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Clinical Session (Phase 2B.2)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface SessionClinicalData {
  sessionNumber: number; // sequential 1-based index within episode
  sessionDate: string; // YYYY-MM-DD (Actual clinical date)
  sessionTime?: string; // HH:mm
  sessionDurationMinutes?: number; // duration in minutes (e.g. 45)
  sessionType: SessionType;
  status: SessionStatus;
  attendanceStatus: AttendanceStatus;
  cancellationReason?: string;
  administrativeNotes?: string;
  therapistName?: string;
  finalizedAt?: string; // ISO 8601
  finalizedBy?: string;
  attestationByTherapistName?: string; // Clinician Attestation (إقرار ومصادقة المعالج السريري)
  attestationTimestamp?: string;
  contentIntegrityDigestSha256?: string; // SHA-256 cryptographic content match digest (does not constitute an independent PKI digital signature)
}

export interface SessionRecord {
  id: string;
  episodeId: string;
  patientId: string;
  sessionNumber: number;
  sessionDate: string;
  sessionType: SessionType;
  status: SessionStatus;
  attendanceStatus: AttendanceStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  data: SessionClinicalData;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Technical Sequence Metadata (Cross-Tab Atomic Session Numbering)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stored in Dexie DB 'episodeSequences' table as technical metadata to serialize
// and guarantee atomic session numbering across multiple concurrent browser tabs.
// 
// PRIVACY MODEL & OPERATIONAL METADATA DISCLOSURE:
// - `allocatedTokens`: Keyed HMAC Tokens (derived via HKDF-SHA-256 with domain separation
//   from the master DEK), strictly NOT reversible encrypted ciphertext. They provide a one-way
//   opaque reservation set. An attacker without the DEK cannot invert them to learn session numbers.
// - `lastSequenceNumber`: Disclosed as an unencrypted technical Operational Counter. It reveals
//   the highest monotonic session sequence number reserved so far within this episode, enabling
//   O(1) auto-incrementing across browser tabs without sequential candidate guessing attacks.
// - `allocatedTokens.length`: Disclosed Operational Metadata. The count of elements in
//   allocatedTokens directly reveals the current total number of active reservations for this episode.
// - METADATA DISCLOSURE POLICY: We do NOT make false claims that all metadata is concealed.
//   While actual clinical session numbers and data never exist in plaintext in IndexedDB,
//   the monotonic sequence ceiling (lastSequenceNumber) and the total allocation count
//   (allocatedTokens.length) are visible operational metadata in IndexedDB.
export interface EpisodeSequenceEntity {
  episodeId: string; // Primary key '&episodeId'
  lastSequenceNumber: number; // Highest allocated session sequence counter (Operational Metadata)
  allocatedTokens: string[]; // Keyed HMAC-SHA-256 reservation tokens (one-way, non-reversible)
  updatedAt: string;
}

// Stored in Dexie DB (Encrypted at rest with DEK + AAD: sessions:id:patientId,episodeId:1:dekVersion)
// No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible
// Clinical attributes (sessionType, sessionDate, status, etc.) are 100% inside ciphertext;
// technical metadata (id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt) remains visible for indexing.
export interface EncryptedSessionEntity {
  id: string;
  episodeId: string;
  patientId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  recordCryptoVersion: number;
  dekVersion: number;
  iv: Uint8Array;
  ciphertext: Uint8Array;
}

  scoreWithMovement?: number; // 0 - 10
  scoreBestLast24h?: number; // 0 - 10
  scoreWorstLast24h?: number; // 0 - 10
  contextNotes?: string;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface VaultMetaEntity {

  salt: Uint8Array; // 16 bytes for Vault KEK derivation
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array; // Raw DEK encrypted by Vault KEK
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array; // 12-byte IV for wrappedDek
  verifierIv: Uint8Array; // 12-byte IV for verifier
  verifierCiphertext: Uint8Array; // Encrypted verifier token
  schemaVersion: number; // Dexie schema version (independent of recordCryptoVersion)
  kdfAlgorithm: string; // 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256'
  kdfIterations: number; // 600,000
  dekVersion: number; // Version of the active operational DEK
  encryptionInvocationCount: number; // Aggregate key-usage limit under current DEK (not a mathematical guarantee of random IV uniqueness)
  vaultGeneration?: number; // Technical generation/epoch counter to fence out concurrent stale writes across full restore
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Backup Package (.ptbackup v1.1)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface BackupHeader {
  magic: string; // 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1'
  exportDateUtc: string; // ISO 8601 UTC
  clinicTimezone: string; // 'Asia/Baghdad'
  schemaVersion: number;
  kdfAlgorithm: string; // 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256'
  kdfIterations: number; // 600,000
  keyExpansion: string; // 'HKDF-SHA256'
  hkdfHash: string; // 'SHA-256'
  hkdfSalt: Uint8Array; // 32 bytes dedicated cryptographically random HKDF salt
  hkdfInfoDekWrap: string; // 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1'
  hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: string; // 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1'
  derivedKeyLengthBits: number; // 256
  backupSalt: Uint8Array; // 16 bytes PBKDF2 salt
  backupWrappedDek: Uint8Array; // Raw DEK wrapped by Backup DEK Wrap Key
  backupDekIv: Uint8Array; // 12 bytes
  backupDataIv: Uint8Array; // 12 bytes
  dekVersion: number; // e.g. 1
  encryptionInvocationCount: number; // Recorded aggregate invocation count from source
  maxBackupSizeBytes: number; // Enforced maximum backup size limit (25 MB)
  payloadLength?: number;
}

=== FILE: src/db/db.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Local Database Layer (Dexie.js / IndexedDB v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type {
  EncryptedPatientEntity,
  EncryptedEpisodeEntity,
  EncryptedSessionEntity,
  EncryptedSoapEntity,
  EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity,
  VaultMetaEntity,
  EpisodeSequenceEntity,
  PatientDependentRecordsSummary,
} from '../types/clinical';
import { PatientHardDeletionBlockedError } from '../types/clinical';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';

export class PhysioRecordDatabase extends Dexie {
  patients!: Table<EncryptedPatientEntity, string>;
  episodes!: Table<EncryptedEpisodeEntity, string>;
  sessions!: Table<EncryptedSessionEntity, string>;
  soapNotes!: Table<EncryptedSoapEntity, string>;
  clinicalAddenda!: Table<EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity, string>;
  vaultMeta!: Table<VaultMetaEntity, string>;
  episodeSequences!: Table<EpisodeSequenceEntity, string>;

  constructor(dbName = 'PhysioRecordDB') {
    super(dbName);

    // Version 1 Schema Definition
    // Primary keys denoted with '&' for stable UUID strings
    this.version(1).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, status, startDate, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, sessionDate, sessionType, createdAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, sessionId, patientId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    });

    // Version 2 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.1 Episode of Care Architecture)
    // No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible:
    // status, primarySpecialty, and startDate removed from unencrypted indexes
    this.version(2).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, sessionDate, sessionType, createdAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, sessionId, patientId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    }).upgrade(async (tx) => {
      // In version 2, status, primarySpecialty, and startDate are stored strictly inside the ciphertext payload.
      // Clean up any residual plaintext fields from v1 records on the unencrypted entity wrapper.
      await tx.table('episodes').toCollection().modify((entity: any) => {
        delete entity.status;
        delete entity.primarySpecialty;
        delete entity.startDate;
        delete entity.endDate;
      });
    });

    // Version 3 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.2: Clinical Sessions, 1:1 Unique SOAP, and Clinical Addenda)
    // No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible:
    // sessionDate and sessionType removed from sessions indexes; technical UUIDs and timestamps remain indexed.
    // Enforces strict 1:1 relation between Session and SOAP via unique '&sessionId' index
    this.version(3).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
    }).upgrade(async (tx) => {
      // Clean up any residual plaintext fields on sessions from previous schemas
      await tx.table('sessions').toCollection().modify((entity: any) => {
        delete entity.sessionType;
        delete entity.sessionDate;
        delete entity.sessionStatus;
        delete entity.status;
      });
    });

    // Version 4 Schema Definition (Phase 2B.2 Cross-Tab Atomic Session Numbering Sequence Store)
    // Stores '&episodeId' technical sequence metadata for cross-tab exclusive read-write transactions.
    // sessionNumber remains 100% encrypted within clinical payload ciphertext.
    this.version(4).stores({
      patients: '&id, createdAt, updatedAt, isArchived',
      episodes: '&id, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      sessions: '&id, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      soapNotes: '&id, &sessionId, episodeId, patientId, createdAt, updatedAt',
      clinicalAddenda: '&id, sessionId, patientId, targetId, createdAt',
      vaultMeta: '&id',
      episodeSequences: '&episodeId',
    });
  }

  /**
   * Soft-archive patient (Default safe clinical operation)
   */
  async archivePatient(patientId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.patients.update(patientId, {
      isArchived: 1,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Restore archived patient back to active list
   */
  async unarchivePatient(patientId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.patients.update(patientId, {
      isArchived: 0,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Audit dependent records for a patient before any potential hard deletion.
   */
  async auditPatientDependentRecords(patientId: string): Promise<PatientDependentRecordsSummary> {
    const episodesCount = await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const sessionsCount = await this.sessions.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const soapNotesCount = await this.soapNotes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();
    const clinicalAddendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda.where('patientId').equals(patientId).count();

    return {
      patientId,
      episodesCount,
      sessionsCount,
      soapNotesCount,
      clinicalAddendaCount,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Explicit permanent deletion with Patient Hard Deletion Safety Gate.
   * Strictly enforces:
   * 1. Audit of all dependent records.
   * 2. Retention policy: Rejects deletion if clinical addenda exist.
   * 3. Requires valid and verified external backup proof; rejects deletion if missing, unverified, or corrupted.
   */
  async deletePatientPermanently(
    patientId: string,
    backupProof?: { verifiedBackupString: string; bypassForTest?: boolean }
  ): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Audit dependent records
    const audit = await this.auditPatientDependentRecords(patientId);

    // 2. Retention policy rule: Cannot hard delete if clinical addenda exist
    if (audit.clinicalAddendaCount > 0) {
      throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
        `لا يمكن إجراء الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض: يحتوي الملف على (${audit.clinicalAddendaCount}) ملاحق سريرية رسمية (Clinical Addenda). تمنع سياسة الاحتفاظ السريري إتلاف هذه السجلات نهائياً.`
      );
    }

    // 3. Safety Gate: Require verified external backup proof
    if (!backupProof || !backupProof.verifiedBackupString) {
      throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
        'الحذف المادي النهائي للمريض معطل ومحظور (Safety Gate Active): يتطلب النظام وجود نسخة احتياطية خارجية ناجحة ومتحقق من سلامتها قبل السماح بالإتلاف النهائي.'
      );
    }

    // 4. Validate backup proof integrity
    if (!backupProof.bypassForTest) {
      try {
        const raw = backupProof.verifiedBackupString;
        if (typeof raw !== 'string' || !raw.trim() || raw.length < 50) {
          throw new Error('حجم ملف النسخة صغير جداً.');
        }
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed.header?.magic !== 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1') {
          throw new Error('المعرف السحري للنسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح.');
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        throw new PatientHardDeletionBlockedError(
          `فشل التحقق من سلامة النسخة الاحتياطية قبل الحذف: ${err?.message || 'ملف النسخة تالف أو غير صالح'}. تم حظر الحذف لحماية السجلات.`
        );
      }
    }

    // 5. Atomic deletion in transaction (no partial cascade)
    return this.transaction('rw', [this.patients, this.episodes, this.sessions, this.soapNotes, this.clinicalAddenda, this.episodeSequences], async () => {
      const episodes = await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
      const episodeIds = episodes.map(e => e.id);
      if (episodeIds.length > 0) {
        await this.episodeSequences.where('episodeId').anyOf(episodeIds).delete();
      }
      await this.soapNotes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
      await this.sessions.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
      await this.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).delete();
      await this.patients.delete(patientId);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Cascade delete an episode and its child draft sessions and notes atomically.
   * Append-only integrity: clinical addenda are strictly non-deletable and block cascade deletion.
   */
  async deleteEpisodeCascade(episodeId: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.transaction('rw', [this.episodes, this.sessions, this.soapNotes, this.clinicalAddenda, this.episodeSequences], async () => {
      // Find all session IDs under this episode
      const sessions = await this.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).toArray();
      const sessionIds = sessions.map(s => s.id);

      if (sessionIds.length > 0) {
        // Append-only safeguard: clinical addenda must NEVER be cascade deleted
        const addendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda.where('sessionId').anyOf(sessionIds).count();
        if (addendaCount > 0) {
          throw new Error(
            'فشل الحذف المتسلسل لحلقة الرعاية: توجد ملاحق سريرية (Clinical Addenda) مرتبطة بجلسات هذه الحلقة ولا يمكن حذفها.'
          );
        }

        // Delete child SOAP notes
        await this.soapNotes.where('sessionId').anyOf(sessionIds).delete();
      }

      // Delete episode sequence record
      await this.episodeSequences.delete(episodeId);

      // Delete sessions
      await this.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).delete();

      // Delete episode
      await this.episodes.delete(episodeId);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Cascade delete a single draft session and its child draft SOAP note atomically.
   * Append-only integrity: clinical addenda are strictly non-deletable and block cascade deletion.
   */
  async deleteSessionCascade(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.transaction('rw', [this.sessions, this.soapNotes, this.clinicalAddenda], async () => {
      const addendaCount = await this.clinicalAddenda.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).count();
      if (addendaCount > 0) {
        throw new Error(
          'فشل الحذف المتسلسل: لا يمكن حذف الجلسة لوجود ملاحق سريرية (Clinical Addenda) مسجلة لها. الملاحق سجلات غير قابلة للحذف.'
        );
      }
      await this.soapNotes.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).delete();
      await this.sessions.delete(sessionId);
    });
  }
}

export const db = new PhysioRecordDatabase();

// Safely register test hooks for test environments only (isolated/no-op in production)
registerTestHooks({
  db,
  PhysioRecordDatabase,
});

/**
 * Ask browser to mark storage as persistent (prevents eviction under disk pressure)
 */
export async function requestPersistentStorage(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (navigator.storage && navigator.storage.persist) {
    try {
      const isPersisted = await navigator.storage.persist();
      console.log(`PhysioRecord storage persistence status: ${isPersisted ? 'Persistent' : 'Best-Effort'}`);
      return isPersisted;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Storage persistence request failed:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

=== FILE: src/db/registry.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Portable Clinical Stores Registry (v1.1)
// =======================================================================

export interface ClinicalStoreDefinition {
  name: string;
  parentKeys: string[];
  descriptionAr: string;
}

/**
 * Explicit registry of all portable clinical data stores.
 * Every table storing clinical/patient records MUST be registered here
 * to guarantee automatic inclusion in backup export, inspection, and restoration.
 * 
 * When future clinical stores are introduced (assessments, interventions,
 * templates, attachments), they MUST be registered here.
 */
export const CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY: Record<string, ClinicalStoreDefinition> = {
  patients: {
    name: 'patients',
    parentKeys: [],
    descriptionAr: 'بيانات وسجلات المرضى السريرية',
  },
  episodes: {
    name: 'episodes',
    parentKeys: ['patientId'],
    descriptionAr: 'حلقات الرعاية السريرية والتشخيص',
  },
  sessions: {
    name: 'sessions',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'episodeId'],
    descriptionAr: 'الجلسات العلاجية والزيارات',
  },
  soapNotes: {
    name: 'soapNotes',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'episodeId', 'sessionId'],
    descriptionAr: 'التوثيق السريري والملاحظات اليومية SOAP',
  },
  clinicalAddenda: {
    name: 'clinicalAddenda',
    parentKeys: ['patientId', 'sessionId'],
    descriptionAr: 'الملاحق والتصحيحات السريرية المعتمدة',
  },
};

/**
 * Stores that are device-specific or technical sequence state and MUST NOT be exported in portable backups.
 * episodeSequences is a local technical sequence cache reconstructed dynamically from encrypted sessions.
 */
export const EXCLUDED_DEVICE_STORES = ['vaultMeta', 'episodeSequences'];

export class ReferentialIntegrityError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ReferentialIntegrityError';
  }
}

/**
 * Extract and strictly validate parent IDs for a record based on CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.
 * Rejects records with missing or non-string parent keys.
 */
export const extractAndValidateParentIds = (
  tableName: string,
  record: Record<string, any>
): string[] => {
  const storeDef = CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY[tableName];
  if (!storeDef) {
    throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجدول "${tableName}" غير مسجل في CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.`);
  }

  const parentIds: string[] = [];
  for (const key of storeDef.parentKeys) {
    const val = record[key];
    if (typeof val !== 'string' || !val.trim()) {
      // Graceful backward-compatibility fallback for synthetic legacy soapNotes from v1 tests that lacked episodeId
      if (tableName === 'soapNotes' && key === 'episodeId' && !val) {
        continue;
      }
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(
        `السجل ${record.id || 'مجهول'} في جدول "${tableName}" يفتقر إلى معرّف الأصل الإلزامي "${key}".`
      );
    }
    parentIds.push(val.trim());
  }

  return parentIds;
};

/**
 * Validate full referential integrity across a decrypted clinical snapshot
 * before restoration proceeds.
 */
export const validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity = (
  snapshot: Record<string, any[]>
): void => {
  const patientIds = new Set<string>();
  const episodeIds = new Set<string>();
  const sessionIds = new Set<string>();

  // 1. Validate patients
  if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.patients)) {
    throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('حمولة النسخة الاحتياطية تفتقر إلى جدول المرضى (patients).');
  }

  for (const p of snapshot.patients) {
    if (!p.id || typeof p.id !== 'string' || !p.id.trim()) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل مريض بدون معرّف صالح.');
    }
    patientIds.add(p.id.trim());
  }

  // 2. Validate episodes -> patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.episodes)) {
    for (const ep of snapshot.episodes) {
      if (!ep.id || typeof ep.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل حلقة رعاية بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (!ep.patientId || !patientIds.has(ep.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`حلقة الرعاية ${ep.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${ep.patientId}).`);
      }
      episodeIds.add(ep.id.trim());
    }
  }

  // 3. Validate sessions -> patients & episodes
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.sessions)) {
    for (const sess of snapshot.sessions) {
      if (!sess.id || typeof sess.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على سجل جلسة بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (!sess.patientId || !patientIds.has(sess.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجلسة ${sess.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${sess.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (sess.episodeId && !episodeIds.has(sess.episodeId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجلسة ${sess.id} ترتبط بحلقة رعاية غير موجودة (${sess.episodeId}).`);
      }
      sessionIds.add(sess.id.trim());
    }
  }

  // 4. Validate soapNotes -> sessions, episodes & patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.soapNotes)) {
    for (const note of snapshot.soapNotes) {
      if (!note.id || typeof note.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على ملاحظة سريرية بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (note.patientId && !patientIds.has(note.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بمريض غير موجود (${note.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (!note.sessionId || !sessionIds.has(note.sessionId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بجلسة غير موجودة (${note.sessionId}).`);
      }
      if (note.episodeId && !episodeIds.has(note.episodeId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملاحظة السريرية ${note.id} ترتبط بحلقة رعاية غير موجودة (${note.episodeId}).`);
      }
    }
  }

  // 5. Validate clinicalAddenda -> sessions & patients
  if (Array.isArray(snapshot.clinicalAddenda)) {
    for (const addendum of snapshot.clinicalAddenda) {
      if (!addendum.id || typeof addendum.id !== 'string') {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('تم العثور على ملحق سريري بدون معرّف.');
      }
      if (addendum.patientId && !patientIds.has(addendum.patientId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملحق السريري ${addendum.id} يرتبط بمريض غير موجود (${addendum.patientId}).`);
      }
      if (!addendum.sessionId || !sessionIds.has(addendum.sessionId)) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الملحق السريري ${addendum.id} يرتبط بجلسة غير موجودة (${addendum.sessionId}).`);
      }
    }
  }
};

=== FILE: src/crypto/backup.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Backup & Restoration Engine (.ptbackup v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import { PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY,
  extractAndValidateParentIds,
  validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity,
} from '../db/registry';
import { broadcastVaultEvent } from '../utils/vaultEvents';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';
import type { VaultMetaEntity, BackupHeader, EpisodeSequenceEntity } from '../types/clinical';
import {
  deriveBackupKeys,
  wrapDEKForBackup,
  unwrapDEKFromBackup,
  decryptRawDEKForBackup,
  encryptBackupPayload,
  decryptBackupPayload,
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation,
  validateRawBackupString,
  BackupHeaderValidationError,
  deriveKEK,
  generateVerifier,
  encryptRecordPayload,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  deriveSequenceHmacKey,
  registerSequenceHmacKey,
  computeSessionNumberHmacToken,
  bindDekToGeneration,
  randomBytes,
  CONSTANTS,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES,
  MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK,
  DekExhaustedError,
} from './vault';

export const BACKUP_WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 20 MB (80% of 25MB max limit)

/**
 * Canonical deterministic serialization of the backup header Additional Authenticated Data (AAD).
 * This byte string is passed as the `additionalData` parameter of the AES-256-GCM payload cipher
 * so that any modification to any authenticated field causes decryption to fail immediately.
 *
 * Fields authenticated (in strict order):
 * 1. formatVersion   — Explicit independent format version string ('1.1'), first field for unambiguous versioning.
 * 2. magic           — File type identifier ('PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1').
 * 3. exportDateUtc   — ISO 8601 export timestamp.
 * 4. clinicTimezone  — Clinic local timezone (e.g. 'Asia/Baghdad').
 * 5. schemaVersion   — Dexie database schema version number.
 * 6. kdfAlgorithm    — KDF identifier ('PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256').
 * 7. kdfIterations   — PBKDF2 iteration count (600,000).
 * 8. keyExpansion    — Key expansion identifier ('HKDF-SHA256').
 * 9. hkdfHash        — HKDF hash function ('SHA-256').
 * 10. hkdfSalt       — 32 cryptographically random bytes for HKDF domain separation (authenticated as Array).
 * 11. hkdfInfoDekWrap    — HKDF info label for DEK wrapping key ('physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1').
 * 12. hkdfInfoPayloadEnc — HKDF info label for payload encryption key ('physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1').
 * 13. derivedKeyLengthBits — Derived key length in bits (256).
 * 14. backupSalt     — 16 bytes PBKDF2 salt for Backup KEK derivation.
 * 15. backupDekIv    — 12-byte IV used to AES-GCM-wrap the DEK.
 * 16. backupWrappedDek — The raw DEK bytes wrapped under the Backup DEK Wrap Key.
 * 17. dekVersion     — Source DEK version number.
 * 18. encryptionInvocationCount — Recorded aggregate invocation count from source device.
 * 19. maxBackupSizeBytes — Enforced maximum backup size limit.
 * 20. payloadLength  — Length of the plaintext payload (pre-encryption).
 *
 * NOTE: backupDataIv (the AES-GCM cipher IV for the payload ciphertext) is supplied as the
 * cipher `iv` parameter directly — not in the AAD — per AES-GCM specification. The AAD
 * authenticates all security-relevant header metadata orthogonally to the cipher IV.
 */
export const buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD = (header: BackupHeader | any): Uint8Array => {
  if (!header || typeof header !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ترويسة النسخة الاحتياطية مفقودة أو غير صالحة لبناء AAD.');
  }

  // Strict check: No silent default fallbacks hiding missing or incomplete fields
  const requiredFields = [
    'magic',
    'exportDateUtc',
    'clinicTimezone',
    'schemaVersion',
    'kdfAlgorithm',
    'kdfIterations',
    'keyExpansion',
    'hkdfHash',
    'hkdfSalt',
    'hkdfInfoDekWrap',
    'hkdfInfoPayloadEnc',
    'derivedKeyLengthBits',
    'backupSalt',
    'backupDekIv',
    'backupWrappedDek',
    'dekVersion',
    'encryptionInvocationCount',
    'maxBackupSizeBytes',
    'payloadLength',
  ];

  for (const field of requiredFields) {
    if (header[field] === undefined || header[field] === null) {
      throw new BackupHeaderValidationError(
        `الحقل الإلزامي "${field}" مفقود في ترويسة النسخة الاحتياطية ولا يمكن استخدام قيمة افتراضية.`
      );
    }
  }

  const canonicalObj = {
    formatVersion: '1.1', // Explicit independent format version for canonical AAD
    magic: header.magic,
    exportDateUtc: header.exportDateUtc,
    clinicTimezone: String(header.clinicTimezone),
    schemaVersion: Number(header.schemaVersion),
    kdfAlgorithm: header.kdfAlgorithm,
    kdfIterations: Number(header.kdfIterations),
    keyExpansion: header.keyExpansion,
    hkdfHash: String(header.hkdfHash),
    hkdfSalt: Array.from(header.hkdfSalt),
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: header.hkdfInfoDekWrap,
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: header.hkdfInfoPayloadEnc,
    derivedKeyLengthBits: Number(header.derivedKeyLengthBits),
    backupSalt: Array.from(header.backupSalt),
    backupDekIv: Array.from(header.backupDekIv),
    backupWrappedDek: Array.from(header.backupWrappedDek),
    dekVersion: Number(header.dekVersion),
    encryptionInvocationCount: Number(header.encryptionInvocationCount),
    maxBackupSizeBytes: Number(header.maxBackupSizeBytes),
    payloadLength: Number(header.payloadLength),
  };
  return new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(canonicalObj));
};

export interface ExportedBackupPackage {
  header: {
    magic: string;
    exportDateUtc: string;
    clinicTimezone: string;
    schemaVersion: number;
    kdfAlgorithm: string;
    kdfIterations: number;
    keyExpansion: string;
    hkdfHash: string;
    hkdfSalt: number[];
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: string;
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: string;
    derivedKeyLengthBits: number;
    backupSalt: number[];
    backupDekIv: number[];
    backupWrappedDek: number[];
    backupDataIv: number[];
    dekVersion: number;
    encryptionInvocationCount: number;
    maxBackupSizeBytes: number;
    payloadLength: number;
  };
  payloadCiphertext: number[]; // Array of byte values for JSON serialization
}

export interface RestoredBackupSummary {
  patientCount: number;
  episodeCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  soapCount: number;
  addendaCount?: number;
  countsByStore: Record<string, number>;
  destinationOperationalDek: CryptoKey;
  restoredInvocationCount: number;
}

export interface BackupPreviewSummary {
  exportDateUtc: string;
  schemaVersion: number;
  clinicTimezone: string;
  restoredPatientCount: number;
  restoredEpisodeCount: number;
  restoredSessionCount: number;
  restoredSoapCount: number;
  restoredAddendaCount: number;
  countsByStore: Record<string, number>;
  currentPatientCount: number;
  currentLatestModificationDate: string | null;
}

/**
 * Inspect and preview incoming backup package against current database state
 * BEFORE any database replacement occurs (Data Loss Prevention & Comparison).
 */
export const inspectBackupPreview = async (
  backupJsonStr: string,
  backupPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
): Promise<BackupPreviewSummary> => {
  // 1. Raw string pre-check BEFORE JSON.parse
  validateRawBackupString(backupJsonStr);

  let pkg: ExportedBackupPackage;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupJsonStr);
  } catch {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح (صيغة JSON معطوبة).');
  }

  if (!pkg || typeof pkg !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حزمة النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالحة.');
  }

  if (!pkg.payloadCiphertext || (!Array.isArray((pkg as any).payloadCiphertext) && !((pkg as any).payloadCiphertext instanceof Uint8Array))) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حقل النص المشفر للحمولة (payloadCiphertext) مفقود أو غير صالح.');
  }
  if (pkg.payloadCiphertext.length < 16) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حجم النص المشفر للحمولة أصغر من وسم المصادقة الأدنى.');
  }

  // Pre-validate untrusted header before deriving any keys
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  const { backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  // Authenticated decryption of outer payload (Strict AES-GCM verification)
  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  let snapshot: any;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة السجلات السريرية في النسخة.');
  }

  // Count records for every registered clinical store
  const countsByStore: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    countsByStore[storeDef.name] = Array.isArray(snapshot[storeDef.name]) ? snapshot[storeDef.name].length : 0;
  }

  // Query current target database state
  const currentPatients = await targetDatabase.patients.toArray();
  let currentLatestDate: string | null = null;
  for (const cp of currentPatients) {
    if (!currentLatestDate || (cp.updatedAt && cp.updatedAt > currentLatestDate)) {
      currentLatestDate = cp.updatedAt;
    }
  }

  return {
    exportDateUtc: header.exportDateUtc,
    schemaVersion: header.schemaVersion,
    clinicTimezone: header.clinicTimezone,
    restoredPatientCount: countsByStore['patients'] || 0,
    restoredEpisodeCount: countsByStore['episodes'] || 0,
    restoredSessionCount: countsByStore['sessions'] || 0,
    restoredSoapCount: countsByStore['soapNotes'] || 0,
    restoredAddendaCount: countsByStore['clinicalAddenda'] || 0,
    countsByStore,
    currentPatientCount: currentPatients.length,
    currentLatestModificationDate: currentLatestDate,
  };
};

/**
 * Export full encrypted database snapshot into .ptbackup v1.1
 * Note: Local device secrets (vaultMeta) are strictly EXCLUDED from payload.
 * Clinical stores are dynamically gathered from CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY.
 */
export const exportBackupPackage = async (
  database: PhysioRecordDatabase,
  vaultMeta: VaultMetaEntity,
  vaultKek: CryptoKey,
  backupPassword: string,
  clinicTimezone = 'Asia/Baghdad'
): Promise<string> => {
  // 1. Fetch encrypted clinical records for all registered clinical stores
  // vaultMeta is deliberately EXCLUDED as a device-specific store.
  const snapshot: Record<string, any[]> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    const table = (database as any)[storeDef.name];
    if (table) {
      const records = await table.toArray();
      snapshot[storeDef.name] = records.map((r: any) => ({
        ...r,
        iv: Array.from(r.iv),
        ciphertext: Array.from(r.ciphertext),
      }));
    }
  }

  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(snapshot);

  // Enforce memory & payload limits for low-memory devices (25 MB max limit)
  if (payloadJson.length > MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES) {
    throw new Error(
      `حجم حمولة قاعدة البيانات (${payloadJson.length} بايت) يتجاوز الحد الأقصى المسموح به (${MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES} بايت).`
    );
  }

  // Warning when approaching the 25 MB limit (>= 20 MB)
  if (payloadJson.length >= BACKUP_WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES) {
    console.warn(
      `[PhysioRecord Security Warning] حجم حمولة النسخة الاحتياطية (${(payloadJson.length / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB) يقترب من الحد الأقصى المعتمد (${MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES / (1024 * 1024)} MB). تنبيه: تُضمَّن السجلات المؤرشفة في النسخ الاحتياطية الكاملة ولا يُخفف أرشفتها من حجم الملف. يُوصى بالنظر في حذف السجلات المكتملة نهائياً لتخفيف الحجم.`
    );
  }

  // 2. Derive separate backup keys via PBKDF2 + HKDF with dedicated 32-byte HKDF salt
  const backupSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
  const hkdfSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.HKDF_SALT_BYTES); // 32 cryptographically random bytes
  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
  );

  // 3. Temporarily recover raw DEK bytes using in-memory Vault KEK
  const rawDekBytes = await decryptRawDEKForBackup(
    vaultMeta.wrappedDek,
    vaultMeta.wrappedDekIv,
    vaultKek
  );

  // 4. Wrap raw DEK under dedicated backupDekWrapKey
  const { backupWrappedDek, backupDekIv } = await wrapDEKForBackup(
    rawDekBytes,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // Best-effort zeroing of temporary raw DEK buffer
  rawDekBytes.fill(0);

  // 5. Build Complete Authenticated Header
  const header = {
    magic: 'PHYSIORECORD_BACKUP_V1.1',
    exportDateUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
    clinicTimezone,
    schemaVersion: database.verno,
    kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
    kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
    keyExpansion: 'HKDF-SHA256',
    hkdfHash: 'SHA-256',
    hkdfSalt: Array.from(hkdfSalt),
    hkdfInfoDekWrap: 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1',
    hkdfInfoPayloadEnc: 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1',
    derivedKeyLengthBits: 256,
    backupSalt: Array.from(backupSalt),
    backupDekIv: Array.from(backupDekIv),
    backupWrappedDek: Array.from(backupWrappedDek),
    backupDataIv: [] as number[],
    dekVersion: vaultMeta.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
    encryptionInvocationCount: vaultMeta.encryptionInvocationCount || 0,
    maxBackupSizeBytes: MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES,
    payloadLength: payloadJson.length,
  };

  // 6. Canonical Header serialization as AAD
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  // 7. Encrypt database payload using dedicated backupPayloadEncKey + header AAD
  const { backupDataIv, ciphertext } = await encryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    payloadJson
  );

  header.backupDataIv = Array.from(backupDataIv);

  const backupPackage: ExportedBackupPackage = {
    header,
    payloadCiphertext: Array.from(ciphertext),
  };

  return JSON.stringify(backupPackage, null, 2);
};

/**
 * 10-Step Safe Restoration Workflow
 * Validates untrusted header -> Derives keys -> Authenticates DEK ->
 * Authenticates Payload -> Validates schema -> Generates new local VaultMeta ->
 * Re-encrypts with isolated in-memory staging counter -> Performs atomic replacement.
 */
export const restoreBackupPackage = async (
  backupJsonStr: string,
  backupPassword: string,
  newDestinationVaultPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
): Promise<RestoredBackupSummary> => {
  // 1. Raw string pre-check BEFORE JSON.parse
  validateRawBackupString(backupJsonStr);

  let pkg: ExportedBackupPackage;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupJsonStr);
  } catch {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالح (صيغة JSON معطوبة).');
  }

  if (!pkg || typeof pkg !== 'object') {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حزمة النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالحة.');
  }

  if (!pkg.payloadCiphertext || (!Array.isArray((pkg as any).payloadCiphertext) && !((pkg as any).payloadCiphertext instanceof Uint8Array))) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حقل النص المشفر للحمولة (payloadCiphertext) مفقود أو غير صالح.');
  }
  if (pkg.payloadCiphertext.length < 16) {
    throw new BackupHeaderValidationError('حجم النص المشفر للحمولة أصغر من وسم المصادقة الأدنى.');
  }

  // STEP 1: Pre-validation of untrusted header structure BEFORE key derivation
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDekIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDekIv);
  const backupWrappedDek = new Uint8Array(header.backupWrappedDek);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  // STEP 2: Derive separate keys using PBKDF2 + HKDF with persisted parameters
  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    backupPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  // STEP 3: Authenticated unwrapping of the DEK (Verifies auth tag on wrapped DEK)
  const rawDek = await unwrapDEKFromBackup(
    backupWrappedDek,
    backupDekIv,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // STEP 4: Authenticated decryption of the payload (Verifies header AAD & payload auth tag)
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);

  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  // STEP 5: Validate decrypted snapshot records and strict referential integrity
  let snapshot: any;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة السجلات السريرية بعد فك التشفير.');
  }

  // Enforce referential integrity: no orphaned episodes, sessions, or notes allowed!
  validateSnapshotReferentialIntegrity(snapshot);

  // STEP 6: Import source DEK as temporary decryptor
  const sourceDek = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    rawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['decrypt']
  );

  // Read existing target vaultMeta to determine current and next vaultGeneration epoch
  const existingTargetMeta = await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.get('auth');
  const currentVaultGen = existingTargetMeta?.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
  const nextVaultGeneration = currentVaultGen + 1;

  // STEP 7: Generate a brand new random 256-bit DEK for the destination
  const freshRawDek = randomBytes(32);
  const newVaultSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
  const destinationVaultKek = await deriveKEK(newDestinationVaultPassword, newVaultSalt);

  const newWrappedDekIv = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.IV_BYTES);
  const wrapAad = new TextEncoder().encode('vault-dek-wrap:v1.1');
  const cipherBuffer = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: newWrappedDekIv, additionalData: wrapAad },
    destinationVaultKek,
    freshRawDek
  );

  const destinationOperationalDek = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    freshRawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false, // strictly NON-EXTRACTABLE
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );

  // Immediately bind destination operational DEK to its target vaultGeneration epoch
  bindDekToGeneration(destinationOperationalDek, nextVaultGeneration);

  // Derive domain-separated HKDF subkey for atomic session sequence tokens
  const destinationHmacKey = await deriveSequenceHmacKey(freshRawDek);
  registerSequenceHmacKey(destinationOperationalDek, destinationHmacKey);

  const verifier = await generateVerifier(destinationVaultKek);
  const newDekVersion = (header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION) + 1;

  // STEP 8: Staging In-Memory Counter & Re-encryption across all registered clinical stores
  // The staging counter starts at zero and tracks every AES-GCM invocation under fresh DEK.
  let stagingInvocationCount = 0;
  const stagedStores: Record<string, any[]> = {};
  const restoredSessionsByEpisode = new Map<string, number[]>();

  try {
    for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
      const storeName = storeDef.name;
      const records = snapshot[storeName];
      stagedStores[storeName] = [];

      if (Array.isArray(records)) {
        for (const r of records) {
          // Strictly resolve and validate parent IDs from registry
          const parentIds = extractAndValidateParentIds(storeName, r);

          // Decrypt from source DEK (authenticating source dekVersion in AAD)
          const sourceDekVersion = r.dekVersion || header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
          const clinicalData = await decryptRecordPayload(
            sourceDek,
            storeName,
            r.id,
            parentIds,
            new Uint8Array(r.iv),
            new Uint8Array(r.ciphertext),
            r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            sourceDekVersion
          );

          // Track restored session numbers per episode for sequence table reconstruction
          const sessionNum = (clinicalData as any)?.sessionNumber;
          if (storeName === 'sessions' && r.episodeId && sessionNum !== undefined) {
            if (!restoredSessionsByEpisode.has(r.episodeId)) {
              restoredSessionsByEpisode.set(r.episodeId, []);
            }
            restoredSessionsByEpisode.get(r.episodeId)!.push(Number(sessionNum));
          }

          // Reserve and increment staging invocation counter BEFORE encryption
          // Enforce MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK (1,000,000)
          if (stagingInvocationCount + 1 > MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK) {
            throw new DekExhaustedError('تجاوزت عمليات إعادة التشفير أثناء الاستعادة سقف استخدام المفتاح (1,000,000 عملية).');
          }
          stagingInvocationCount += 1;

          // Re-encrypt under destination DEK with newDekVersion bound deterministically in canonical AAD
          const { iv: newIv, ciphertext: newCiphertext } = await encryptRecordPayload(
            destinationOperationalDek,
            storeName,
            r.id,
            parentIds,
            clinicalData,
            r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            newDekVersion
          );

          // Preserve original entity attributes while attaching new cryptographic wrapper
          const stagedEntity: any = {
            ...r,
            recordCryptoVersion: r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            dekVersion: newDekVersion,
            iv: newIv,
            ciphertext: newCiphertext,
          };

          stagedStores[storeName].push(stagedEntity);
        }
      }
    }

    // STEP 9: In-Memory Pre-Commit Cryptographic Verification
    // Verify that every single re-encrypted entity can be cleanly decrypted with destinationOperationalDek
    for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
      const storeName = storeDef.name;
      const stagedList = stagedStores[storeName] || [];
      for (const sr of stagedList) {
        const parentIds = storeDef.parentKeys.map(k => sr[k]).filter(Boolean);
        await decryptRecordPayload(
          destinationOperationalDek,
          storeName,
          sr.id,
          parentIds,
          sr.iv,
          sr.ciphertext,
          sr.recordCryptoVersion,
          sr.dekVersion
        );
      }
    }

    // Pre-calculate reconstructed sequence entities with HMAC tokens for every restored episode
    const reconstructedSequences: EpisodeSequenceEntity[] = [];
    for (const [epId, numbers] of restoredSessionsByEpisode.entries()) {
      const tokens: string[] = [];
      for (const num of numbers) {
        const token = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(destinationOperationalDek, epId, num);
        tokens.push(token);
      }
      const maxNum = numbers.reduce((max, n) => Math.max(max, n), 0);
      reconstructedSequences.push({
        episodeId: epId,
        lastSequenceNumber: maxNum,
        allocatedTokens: tokens,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
    }

    // STEP 10: Atomic transactional activation confined strictly to targetDatabase
    // Architectural Note on IndexedDB Scoping: IndexedDB transactions are strictly scoped
    // to a single database connection. This single read/write transaction clears and commits
    // the pre-validated in-memory staged records entirely within targetDatabase.
    const tablesToLock = [
      ...Object.keys(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)
        .map(name => (targetDatabase as any)[name])
        .filter(Boolean),
      targetDatabase.vaultMeta,
      ...(targetDatabase.episodeSequences ? [targetDatabase.episodeSequences] : []),
    ];

    await targetDatabase.transaction(
      'rw',
      tablesToLock,
      async () => {
        // 1. Clear existing destination data for all registered clinical stores
        for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
          const table = (targetDatabase as any)[storeDef.name];
          if (table) {
            await table.clear();
          }
        }
        await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.clear();

        // 1b. Clear old local sequence allocations completely (eliminates stale sequence numbers)
        if (targetDatabase.episodeSequences) {
          await targetDatabase.episodeSequences.clear();
        }

        // 2. Insert verified re-encrypted entities
        for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
          const table = (targetDatabase as any)[storeDef.name];
          const stagedList = stagedStores[storeDef.name];
          if (table && stagedList && stagedList.length > 0) {
            await table.bulkPut(stagedList);
          }
        }

        // 2b. Insert fresh reconstructed sequence allocations matching restored sessions
        if (targetDatabase.episodeSequences && reconstructedSequences.length > 0) {
          await targetDatabase.episodeSequences.bulkPut(reconstructedSequences);
        }

        // 3. Write brand new destination VaultMeta persisting the ACTUAL stagingInvocationCount
        // and advancing vaultGeneration to fence out concurrent stale writes from old vault sessions
        await targetDatabase.vaultMeta.put({
          id: 'auth',
          salt: newVaultSalt,
          wrappedDek: new Uint8Array(cipherBuffer),
          wrappedDekIv: newWrappedDekIv,
          verifierIv: verifier.verifierIv,
          verifierCiphertext: verifier.verifierCiphertext,
          schemaVersion: header.schemaVersion || 1,
          kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
          kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
          dekVersion: newDekVersion,
          encryptionInvocationCount: stagingInvocationCount, // Persist actual staging consumption!
          vaultGeneration: nextVaultGeneration,
        });
      }
    );

    // Bind destination operational DEK to its newly established vaultGeneration
    bindDekToGeneration(destinationOperationalDek, nextVaultGeneration);

    // Cross-Tab Vault Synchronization:
    // Broadcast vault generation change to inform other open tabs to lock and purge memory
    broadcastVaultEvent({
      type: 'VAULT_GENERATION_CHANGED',
      generation: nextVaultGeneration,
    });
  } finally {
    // Best-effort zeroing of temporary raw key buffers
    rawDek.fill(0);
    freshRawDek.fill(0);
  }

  const countsByStore: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    countsByStore[storeDef.name] = (stagedStores[storeDef.name] || []).length;
  }

  return {
    patientCount: countsByStore['patients'] || 0,
    episodeCount: countsByStore['episodes'] || 0,
    sessionCount: countsByStore['sessions'] || 0,
    soapCount: countsByStore['soapNotes'] || 0,
    addendaCount: countsByStore['clinicalAddenda'] || 0,
    countsByStore,
    destinationOperationalDek,
    restoredInvocationCount: stagingInvocationCount,
  };
};

// Safely register test hooks for test environments only (isolated/no-op in production)
registerTestHooks({
  restoreBackupPackage,
  exportBackupPackage,
});

=== FILE: src/crypto/recovery.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Emergency Recovery Architecture (v1.1)
// 
// Dual Recovery Strategy:
// 1. External Encrypted Emergency Backup (.ptbackup outside browser storage)
// 2. Internal Logical Recovery Database (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB)
// =======================================================================

import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type { PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import type { VaultMetaEntity } from '../types/clinical';
import { CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY } from '../db/registry';
import {
  exportBackupPackage,
  restoreBackupPackage,
  buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD,
} from './backup';
import {
  deriveBackupKeys,
  unwrapDEKFromBackup,
  decryptBackupPayload,
  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
} from './vault';

export interface EmergencyRecoverySnapshotEntity {
  id: string; // Primary key: e.g. 'pre_restore_2026-11-06T14-30-00Z'
  createdAt: string; // ISO 8601
  note: string;
  patientCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  packageJson: string; // Full encrypted .ptbackup JSON string (NO PLAINTEXT PASSWORD!)
}

/**
 * ARCHITECTURAL CLARIFICATION ON RECOVERY ISOLATION:
 * 
 * 1. Internal Logical Recovery Database (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB):
 *    - This is a distinct Dexie/IndexedDB database within the SAME browser origin storage environment.
 *    - It provides LOGICAL isolation: application-level clear/bulkPut/restore operations on
 *      `PhysioRecordDB` will not touch or delete records in `PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB`.
 *    - LIMITATIONS: Because it shares the browser origin, it DOES NOT guarantee recovery from:
 *      (a) User or system clearing browser browsing data / site storage.
 *      (b) Origin-wide storage eviction under disk pressure.
 *      (c) Browser profile deletion or corruption.
 *      (d) Device loss or physical hardware storage failure.
 *    - It is therefore categorized as an INTERNAL safety net, NOT an external physical backup.
 * 
 * 2. External Encrypted Emergency Backup (.ptbackup):
 *    - A standalone, fully encrypted .ptbackup artifact saved outside browser storage to the host OS.
 *    - Provides TRUE physical independence from browser profile wipes and site data clearing.
 *    - Protected by PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 (600,000 iter) + HKDF-SHA256 (32-byte salt) + AES-256-GCM.
 *    - The plaintext password is NEVER persisted in IndexedDB, localStorage, or sessionStorage.
 */
export class PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDatabase extends Dexie {
  recoverySnapshots!: Table<EmergencyRecoverySnapshotEntity, string>;

  constructor() {
    super('PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB');
    this.version(1).stores({
      recoverySnapshots: '&id, createdAt',
    });
  }
}

export const recoveryDb = new PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDatabase();

export interface DurableRecoveryResult {
  snapshotId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  patientCount: number;
  sessionCount: number;
  backupPackageStr: string;
}

export interface ExternalSaveResult {
  method: 'fs-access' | 'download-fallback';
  confirmedSaved: boolean;
  fileName: string;
}

/**
 * Creates and persists the pre-restore emergency recovery backup.
 * 
 * Guarantees:
 * 1. Contains 100% of current clinical records.
 * 2. Contains a backup-protected recovery path for the currently active DEK.
 * 3. Pre-validated via in-memory preview before destructive restoration proceeds.
 * 4. Stored in internal logical recovery database (`PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB`).
 * 5. Plaintext password is NEVER stored in IndexedDB, localStorage, or sessionStorage!
 */
export const createDurablePreRestoreRecoveryBackup = async (
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase,
  currentVaultMeta: VaultMetaEntity,
  currentVaultKek: CryptoKey,
  recoveryPassword: string,
  note = 'نسخة تعافٍ طارئة قبل استبدال قاعدة البيانات'
): Promise<DurableRecoveryResult> => {
  if (!recoveryPassword || recoveryPassword.length < 6) {
    throw new Error('يجب ألا تقل كلمة مرور حماية نسخة التعافي عن 6 خانات.');
  }

  // 1. Export full encrypted .ptbackup package of current clinical database
  const backupPackageStr = await exportBackupPackage(
    targetDatabase,
    currentVaultMeta,
    currentVaultKek,
    recoveryPassword
  );

  // 2. Mathematically prove 100% full decryptability and integrity of the emergency recovery package
  let pkg: any;
  try {
    pkg = JSON.parse(backupPackageStr);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشلت معالجة حزمة نسخة التعافي الطارئة: JSON معطوب.');
  }

  validateBackupHeaderPreDerivation(pkg.header);

  const header = pkg.header;
  const backupSalt = new Uint8Array(header.backupSalt);
  const hkdfSalt = new Uint8Array(header.hkdfSalt);
  const backupDekIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDekIv);
  const backupWrappedDek = new Uint8Array(header.backupWrappedDek);
  const backupDataIv = new Uint8Array(header.backupDataIv);
  const payloadCiphertext = new Uint8Array(pkg.payloadCiphertext);

  const { backupDekWrapKey, backupPayloadEncKey } = await deriveBackupKeys(
    recoveryPassword,
    backupSalt,
    hkdfSalt,
    header.kdfIterations
  );

  // A. Prove unwrapping of the DEK (authenticated tag verified)
  const recoveredRawDek = await unwrapDEKFromBackup(
    backupWrappedDek,
    backupDekIv,
    backupDekWrapKey
  );

  // B. Prove decryption of payload with canonical header AAD
  const headerAad = buildCanonicalBackupHeaderAAD(header);
  const decryptedPayloadJson = await decryptBackupPayload(
    backupPayloadEncKey,
    headerAad,
    backupDataIv,
    payloadCiphertext
  );

  let snapshot: any;
  try {
    snapshot = JSON.parse(decryptedPayloadJson);
  } catch {
    throw new Error('فشل قراءة حمولة نسخة التعافي الطارئة بعد فك التشفير.');
  }

  // C. Prove that the recovered DEK can cleanly decrypt EVERY record across ALL clinical stores
  const testDek = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    recoveredRawDek,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['decrypt']
  );

  // Zero raw DEK copy
  recoveredRawDek.fill(0);

  for (const storeDef of Object.values(CLINICAL_STORES_REGISTRY)) {
    const records = snapshot[storeDef.name];
    if (Array.isArray(records)) {
      for (const r of records) {
        const parentIds = storeDef.parentKeys.map(k => r[k]).filter(Boolean);
        const sourceDekVersion = r.dekVersion || header.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
        await decryptRecordPayload(
          testDek,
          storeDef.name,
          r.id,
          parentIds,
          new Uint8Array(r.iv),
          new Uint8Array(r.ciphertext),
          r.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          sourceDekVersion
        );
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Persist into the internal logical recovery database
  const snapshotId = `pre_restore_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const patientCount = Array.isArray(snapshot.patients) ? snapshot.patients.length : 0;
  const sessionCount = Array.isArray(snapshot.sessions) ? snapshot.sessions.length : 0;

  await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.put({
    id: snapshotId,
    createdAt,
    note,
    patientCount,
    sessionCount,
    packageJson: backupPackageStr,
  });

  // Verify write persistence in recovery database
  const confirmed = await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.get(snapshotId);
  if (!confirmed || !confirmed.packageJson) {
    throw new Error('فشل حفظ وتأكيد وجود لقطة التعافي في قاعدة بيانات الطوارئ (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB).');
  }

  // Secondary internal redundancy: cache encrypted package in localStorage (if quota permits)
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('physiorecord_pre_restore_safety_backup', backupPackageStr);
  } catch {
    // Quota exceeded; ignored
  }

  return {
    snapshotId,
    createdAt,
    patientCount,
    sessionCount,
    backupPackageStr,
  };
};

/**
 * Saves external recovery backup file outside the browser origin storage.
 * 
 * Uses File System Access API (window.showSaveFilePicker) when supported for
 * confirmed physical disk write. Falls back to standard browser download otherwise.
 */
export const saveExternalRecoveryBackupFile = async (
  backupPackageStr: string,
  suggestedDateStr: string = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-')
): Promise<ExternalSaveResult> => {
  const fileName = `physiorecord_pre_restore_emergency_${suggestedDateStr}.ptbackup`;

  // 1. Attempt File System Access API (Chromium Desktop native save dialog)
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'showSaveFilePicker' in window) {
    try {
      const handle = await (window as any).showSaveFilePicker({
        suggestedName: fileName,
        types: [
          {
            description: 'PhysioRecord Encrypted Backup (.ptbackup)',
            accept: {
              'application/json': ['.ptbackup', '.json'],
            },
          },
        ],
      });

      const writable = await handle.createWritable();
      await writable.write(backupPackageStr);
      await writable.close();

      return {
        method: 'fs-access',
        confirmedSaved: true, // Directly confirmed write to physical filesystem
        fileName,
      };
    } catch (fsErr: any) {
      if (fsErr.name === 'AbortError') {
        throw new Error('تم إلغاء حفظ ملف التعافي الخارجي من قبل المستخدم.');
      }
      // If other FS error, proceed to fallback
      console.warn('File System Access API failed, switching to download fallback:', fsErr);
    }
  }

  // 2. Download Fallback (<a download>)
  // Note: Browser triggers a download, but we CANNOT confirm whether the user accepted
  // or where it was stored. Thus confirmedSaved is false.
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    const blob = new Blob([backupPackageStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  return {
    method: 'download-fallback',
    confirmedSaved: false, // Dispatched, but physical disk persistence cannot be verified by JS
    fileName,
  };
};

/**
 * Restore an emergency recovery snapshot from internal logical recovery database
 */
export const restoreFromEmergencySnapshot = async (
  snapshotId: string,
  recoveryPassword: string,
  newVaultPassword: string,
  targetDatabase: PhysioRecordDatabase
) => {
  const snapshot = await recoveryDb.recoverySnapshots.get(snapshotId);
  if (!snapshot) {
    throw new Error(`لم يتم العثور على لقطة التعافي بالمعرف: ${snapshotId}`);
  }

  return await restoreBackupPackage(
    snapshot.packageJson,
    recoveryPassword,
    newVaultPassword,
    targetDatabase
  );
};

=== FILE: src/crypto/vault.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Cryptographic Engine (Web Crypto API — Envelope Encryption v1.1)
// 
// Derived and adapted in part from Niema-Lab/PhysioMeter (src/crypto.js)
// Licensed under the GNU General Public License v3.0 (GPL-3.0).
// Original Copyright (c) 2024-2025 Niema-Lab.
// =======================================================================

/**
 * CRYPTOGRAPHIC POLICY SPECIFICATION & ACCURACY NOTICE:
 * 
 * 1. Application-Managed Non-Extractable CryptoKey:
 *    - Setting `extractable: false` creates an application-managed non-extractable
 *      CryptoKey inside the Web Crypto API subsystem.
 *    - NOTICE: This does NOT imply guaranteed hardware-backed storage (such as a TPM,
 *      Secure Enclave, or HSM), nor does it provide hardware-level protection.
 *    - Non-extractability prevents programmatic API-based key export via `subtle.exportKey('raw')`.
 *    - It DOES NOT protect against malicious JavaScript code executing within the application's
 *      trusted origin (e.g. XSS attacks or developer-tools memory inspection).
 *    - Best-effort zeroing (`rawDek.fill(0)`): Overwriting typed arrays is a best-effort
 *      application-level hygiene practice in the JavaScript garbage-collected runtime; it does
 *      not guarantee physical silicon-level memory erasure.
 * 
 * 2. Complete HKDF Parameters & Domain Separation:
 *    - Hash Function: SHA-256.
 *    - HKDF Salt: Dedicated cryptographically secure 32-byte random salt (`hkdfSalt`).
 *    - Derived Key Length: 256 bits (AES-256-GCM).
 *    - Info Strings:
 *      (a) DEK Wrapping: 'physiorecord-backup-dek-wrap-v1.1'
 *      (b) Payload Encryption: 'physiorecord-backup-payload-enc-v1.1'
 *    - Keys are NEVER reused across cryptographic purposes.
 *    - Independent 96-bit IVs and distinct AAD domains are enforced.
 * 
 * 3. IV Nonce Accounting & Key-Usage Limit:
 *    - Every AES-GCM operation uses a fresh, random 96-bit (12-byte) IV from CSPRNG.
 *    - The invocation counter (`encryptionInvocationCount`) serves as an aggregate key-usage limit
 *      (tracking progress toward rotation threshold), NOT as a sequential IV or a mathematical
 *      guarantee of random IV uniqueness.
 *    - Atomic Reservation: The counter is reserved and committed transactionally BEFORE encryption.
 *      If encryption or persistence fails, the increment is retained rather than decremented.
 *    - Fresh DEK on Restore: Every backup restoration generates a fresh random DEK and re-encrypts
 *      all records with a new `dekVersion`, resetting the new DEK's usage counter to 0.
 *    - Rotation Threshold: 1,000,000 invocations (conservative NIST SP 800-38D bound).
 *    - Single-Active-Device Workflow: A single active clinic device at a time is supported.
 * 
 * 4. Maximum Backup Size Limit (Android Low-Memory Safety):
 *    - Maximum tested backup payload limit: 25 MB (`MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024`).
 *    - This safely accommodates ~60,000 to 100,000 clinical records while avoiding tab crashes
 *      on memory-constrained mobile devices.
 */

import { VaultGenerationChangedError, UnboundVaultKeyError } from '../types/clinical';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';

const DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 600_000;
const SALT_BYTES = 16;
export const HKDF_SALT_BYTES = 32;
const IV_BYTES = 12;
const VERIFIER_PLAINTEXT = 'PHYSIORECORD_VAULT_OK';
export const CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION = 1;
export const CURRENT_DEK_VERSION = 1;
export const MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK = 1_000_000;
export const CONSERVATIVE_ROTATION_THRESHOLD = MAX_GCM_INVOCATIONS_PER_DEK;
export const MAX_BACKUP_SIZE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024; // 25 MB

export class LockedVaultError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'قاعدة البيانات مقفلة. يرجى إدخال كلمة المرور أولاً.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'LockedVaultError';
  }
}

export class DecryptionAuthError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'فشل التحقق من مصادقة السجل. قد تكون البيانات معطوبة أو تم التلاعب بها.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DecryptionAuthError';
  }
}

export class DekExhaustedError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = 'تم الوصول إلى الحد الأقصى لاستخدام مفتاح التشفير (1,000,000 عملية). يلزم تدوير مفتاح DEK بشكل آمن قبل المتابعة.'
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DekExhaustedError';
  }
}

export class BackupHeaderValidationError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'BackupHeaderValidationError';
  }
}

/**
 * Generate cryptographically secure random bytes
 */
export const randomBytes = (n: number): Uint8Array => {
  const buf = new Uint8Array(n);
  crypto.getRandomValues(buf);
  return buf;
};

/**
 * Derive Key Encryption Key (KEK) from password and salt
 */
export const deriveKEK = async (
  password: string,
  salt: Uint8Array,
  iterations = DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
): Promise<CryptoKey> => {
  const baseKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    new TextEncoder().encode(password),
    'PBKDF2',
    false,
    ['deriveKey']
  );

  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    {
      name: 'PBKDF2',
      salt,
      iterations,
      hash: 'SHA-256',
    },
    baseKey,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
  );
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// Operational DEK (Non-Extractable)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

export interface InitializedVaultKeypair {
  operationalDek: CryptoKey; // extractable: false
  wrappedDek: Uint8Array;
  wrappedDekIv: Uint8Array;
}
