  deriveKEK,
  encryptRecordPayload,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  atomicReserveInvocationCount,
  randomBytes,
  bindDekToGeneration,
  getDekGeneration,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
});

=== FILE: src/services/episodeService.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Episode of Care Service Layer (v2.0)
// Centralized Envelope Encryption with Mandatory DEK Reservation & Referential Integrity
// =======================================================================

import { db as defaultDb, PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import { ReferentialIntegrityError } from '../db/registry';
import type {
  EpisodeRecord,
  EpisodeClinicalData,
  EncryptedEpisodeEntity,
  EpisodeStatus,
  ClinicalSpecialty,
} from '../types/clinical';

export class EpisodeService {
  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt all episodes belonging to a specific patient.
   * Enforces referential integrity check: verifies patient existence.
   */
  static async getEpisodesByPatient(
    patientId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord[]> {
    if (!patientId || typeof patientId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف المريض غير صالح.');
    }

    // Verify patient exists
    const patientExists = await database.patients.get(patientId);
    if (!patientExists) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`المريض بالمعرف "${patientId}" غير موجود في قاعدة البيانات.`);
    }

    const encryptedEntities = await database.episodes.where('patientId').equals(patientId).toArray();
    const records: EpisodeRecord[] = [];

    for (const entity of encryptedEntities) {
      try {
        const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<EpisodeClinicalData>(
          dek,
          'episodes',
          entity.id,
          [entity.patientId],
          entity.iv,
          entity.ciphertext,
          entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
        );

        const status: EpisodeStatus = decryptedData.status || (entity as any).status || 'active';
        const primarySpecialty: ClinicalSpecialty = decryptedData.primarySpecialty || (entity as any).primarySpecialty || 'general';
        const startDate: string = decryptedData.startDate || decryptedData.onsetDate || (entity as any).startDate || entity.createdAt.split('T')[0];
        const endDate: string | undefined = decryptedData.endDate || (entity as any).endDate;

        records.push({
          id: entity.id,
          patientId: entity.patientId,
          status,
          primarySpecialty,
          startDate,
          endDate,
          createdAt: entity.createdAt,
          updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
          data: {
            ...decryptedData,
            status,
            primarySpecialty,
            startDate,
            endDate,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Failed to decrypt episode record ${entity.id}:`, err);
      }
    }

    // Sort: newest startDate or createdAt first
    records.sort((a, b) => {
      const timeA = new Date(a.startDate || a.createdAt).getTime();
      const timeB = new Date(b.startDate || b.createdAt).getTime();
      return timeB - timeA;
    });

    return records;
  }

  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt a single episode by ID.
   */
  static async getEpisodeById(
    episodeId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord | null> {
    const entity = await database.episodes.get(episodeId);
    if (!entity) return null;

    const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<EpisodeClinicalData>(
      dek,
      'episodes',
      entity.id,
      [entity.patientId],
      entity.iv,
      entity.ciphertext,
      entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
    );

    const status: EpisodeStatus = decryptedData.status || (entity as any).status || 'active';
    const primarySpecialty: ClinicalSpecialty = decryptedData.primarySpecialty || (entity as any).primarySpecialty || 'general';
    const startDate: string = decryptedData.startDate || decryptedData.onsetDate || (entity as any).startDate || entity.createdAt.split('T')[0];
    const endDate: string | undefined = decryptedData.endDate || (entity as any).endDate;

    return {
      id: entity.id,
      patientId: entity.patientId,
      status,
      primarySpecialty,
      startDate,
      endDate,
      createdAt: entity.createdAt,
      updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
      data: {
        ...decryptedData,
        status,
        primarySpecialty,
        startDate,
        endDate,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Create a new episode for an existing patient.
   * Enforces referential integrity, mandatory DEK counter reservation, and canonical AAD.
   */
  static async createEpisode(
    patientId: string,
    data: EpisodeClinicalData,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    explicitId?: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    if (!patientId || typeof patientId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف المريض غير صالح.');
    }

    // 1. Verify parent patient existence (Application-level referential integrity)
    const patient = await database.patients.get(patientId);
    if (!patient) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`لا يمكن إنشاء حلقة رعاية: المريض بالمعرف "${patientId}" غير موجود.`);
    }

    // 2. Validate mandatory clinical fields
    if (!data.title?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('عنوان حلقة الرعاية إلزامي.');
    }
    if (!data.medicalDiagnosis?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('التشخيص الطبي الأولي إلزامي.');
    }
    if (!data.clinicalImpression?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('الانطباع السريري للعلاج الطبيعي إلزامي.');
    }
    if (!data.primarySpecialty) {
      throw new Error('تحديد التخصص السريري الرئيسي إلزامي.');
    }
    if (!data.affectedBodyRegion?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('المنطقة التشريحية المصابة إلزامية.');
    }

    const id = explicitId || crypto.randomUUID();

    // Check for duplicate ID
    const duplicate = await database.episodes.get(id);
    if (duplicate) {
      throw new Error(`حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${id}" موجودة مسبقاً.`);
    }

    // Get active DEK version from vaultMeta
    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const startDate = data.startDate || data.onsetDate || now.split('T')[0];
    const status: EpisodeStatus = data.status || 'active';
    const primarySpecialty = data.primarySpecialty;

    const fullClinicalData: EpisodeClinicalData = {
      ...data,
      status,
      primarySpecialty,
      startDate,
    };

    // Centralized encryption with MANDATORY invocation counter reservation
    // All clinical metadata (status, specialty, startDate) are sealed 100% inside ciphertext
    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'episodes',
      id,
      [patientId],
      fullClinicalData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    // Entity stored in IndexedDB: strictly random IDs and technical timestamps
    const entity: EncryptedEpisodeEntity = {
      id,
      patientId,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    // Atomic write to Dexie
    await database.episodes.put(entity);

    return {
      id,
      patientId,
      status,
      primarySpecialty,
      startDate,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: fullClinicalData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Update an existing episode.
   */
  static async updateEpisode(
    episodeId: string,
    data: EpisodeClinicalData,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getEpisodeById(episodeId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${episodeId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (existing.status === 'archived') {
      throw new Error('لا يمكن تعديل حلقة رعاية مؤرشفة. يرجى إلغاء الأرشفة أولاً.');
    }

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const updatedData: EpisodeClinicalData = {
      ...existing.data,
      ...data,
      status: existing.status,
      primarySpecialty: data.primarySpecialty || existing.primarySpecialty,
      startDate: data.startDate || data.onsetDate || existing.startDate,
      endDate: data.endDate || existing.endDate,
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'episodes',
      episodeId,
      [existing.patientId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedEpisodeEntity = {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.episodes.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      status: updatedData.status || existing.status,
      primarySpecialty: updatedData.primarySpecialty,
      startDate: updatedData.startDate || existing.startDate,
      endDate: updatedData.endDate,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Change status of an episode (active, on_hold, completed, archived).
   */
  static async setEpisodeStatus(
    episodeId: string,
    newStatus: EpisodeStatus,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    statusNotes?: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getEpisodeById(episodeId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${episodeId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    let endDate = existing.endDate;
    if (newStatus === 'completed' && !endDate) {
      endDate = now.split('T')[0];
    } else if (newStatus === 'active') {
      endDate = undefined; // Re-opened
    }

    const updatedData: EpisodeClinicalData = {
      ...existing.data,
      status: newStatus,
      endDate,
      statusNotes: statusNotes || existing.data.statusNotes,
    };

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'episodes',
      episodeId,
      [existing.patientId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedEpisodeEntity = {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.episodes.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      status: newStatus,
      primarySpecialty: existing.primarySpecialty,
      startDate: existing.startDate,
      endDate,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Close episode with discharge summary and end date.
   */
  static async closeEpisode(
    episodeId: string,
    dischargeInfo: {
      endDate: string;
      dischargeSummary: string;
      dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
    },
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getEpisodeById(episodeId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${episodeId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (!dischargeInfo.dischargeSummary?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('ملخص الخروج والنتائج السريرية إلزامي لإغلاق حلقة الرعاية.');
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const updatedData: EpisodeClinicalData = {
      ...existing.data,
      status: 'completed',
      endDate: dischargeInfo.endDate || now.split('T')[0],
      dischargeSummary: dischargeInfo.dischargeSummary.trim(),
      dischargeCriteriaSummary: dischargeInfo.dischargeCriteriaSummary?.trim(),
    };

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'episodes',
      episodeId,
      [existing.patientId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedEpisodeEntity = {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.episodes.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      status: 'completed',
      primarySpecialty: existing.primarySpecialty,
      startDate: existing.startDate,
      endDate: updatedData.endDate,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Soft-archive episode (Default safe clinical action).
   */
  static async archiveEpisode(
    episodeId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    return this.setEpisodeStatus(episodeId, 'archived', dek, undefined, database);
  }

  /**
   * Unarchive episode back to active state.
   */
  static async unarchiveEpisode(
    episodeId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> {
    return this.setEpisodeStatus(episodeId, 'active', dek, undefined, database);
  }

  /**
   * Safeguarded permanent deletion of an episode.
   * REJECTS deletion if there are child sessions attached to protect historical records.
   */
  static async deleteEpisodeSafeguarded(
    episodeId: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<void> {
    const childSessionCount = await database.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).count();
    if (childSessionCount > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `لا يمكن الحذف الدائم: حلقة الرعاية تحتوي على (${childSessionCount}) جلسات علاجية مرتبطة. يرجى أرشفتها بدلاً من ذلك لحفظ السجل التاريخي للمريض.`
      );
    }
    await database.episodes.delete(episodeId);
  }
}

=== FILE: src/services/sessionService.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Clinical Session Service Layer (v2.0)
// Centralized Envelope Encryption with Mandatory DEK Reservation & Referential Integrity
// =======================================================================

import { db as defaultDb, PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  computeSessionNumberHmacToken,
  getDekGeneration,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import { registerTestHooks } from '../utils/testHooks';
import { ReferentialIntegrityError } from '../db/registry';
import { EpisodeService } from './episodeService';
import { SoapService } from './soapService';
import {
  CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError,
  VaultGenerationChangedError,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
  type SessionRecord,
  type SessionClinicalData,
  type EncryptedSessionEntity,
  type SessionType,
  type SessionStatus,
  type AttendanceStatus,
} from '../types/clinical';

/**
 * Compute SHA-256 digest for content integrity verification
 */
async function computeSha256Digest(data: string): Promise<string> {
  const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(data);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * Keyed FIFO Async Mutex to guarantee atomic sequential execution per resource key (e.g. episodeId).
 * Solves concurrency race conditions without blocking concurrent operations on different keys.
 */
class AsyncKeyedMutex {
  private queues = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();

  async runExclusive<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const currentQueue = this.queues.get(key) || Promise.resolve();

    let release: () => void;
    const nextInQueue = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
      release = resolve;
    });

    const tail = currentQueue.then(() => nextInQueue, () => nextInQueue);
    this.queues.set(key, tail);

    await currentQueue;
    try {
      return await fn();
    } finally {
      if (this.queues.get(key) === tail) {
        this.queues.delete(key);
      }
      release!();
    }
  }
}

const sessionCreationMutex = new AsyncKeyedMutex();

/**
 * Test hook signature for intercepting session creation steps during automated E2E concurrency tests.
 * Only active when explicitly set by tests.
 */
export type SessionCreationTestHook = (
  stage: 'after-reservation' | 'before-commit',
  context: {
    episodeId: string;
    sessionNumber: number;
    expectedVaultGeneration: number;
  }
) => Promise<void> | void;

let sessionCreationTestHook: SessionCreationTestHook | null = null;

export const setSessionCreationTestHook = (hook: SessionCreationTestHook | null) => {
  sessionCreationTestHook = hook;
};

// Safely register test hook on window for test environments only (no-op in production)
registerTestHooks({
  setSessionCreationTestHook,
});

export class SessionService {
  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt all sessions belonging to a specific Episode.
   * Enforces referential integrity check: verifies episode and parent patient existence.
   * Chronologically sorts sessions by sessionNumber and sessionDate ascending.
   */
  static async getSessionsByEpisode(
    episodeId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SessionRecord[]> {
    if (!episodeId || typeof episodeId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف حلقة الرعاية غير صالح.');
    }

    const episode = await database.episodes.get(episodeId);
    if (!episode) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${episodeId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    const encryptedEntities = await database.sessions.where('episodeId').equals(episodeId).toArray();
    const records: SessionRecord[] = [];

    for (const entity of encryptedEntities) {
      try {
        const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<SessionClinicalData>(
          dek,
          'sessions',
          entity.id,
          [entity.patientId, entity.episodeId],
          entity.iv,
          entity.ciphertext,
          entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
        );

        const sessionNumber: number = decryptedData.sessionNumber || 1;
        const sessionDate: string =
          decryptedData.sessionDate || (entity as any).sessionDate || entity.createdAt.split('T')[0];
        const sessionType: SessionType =
          decryptedData.sessionType || (entity as any).sessionType || 'follow_up';
        const status: SessionStatus =
          decryptedData.status || (entity as any).sessionStatus || (entity as any).status || 'draft';
        const attendanceStatus: AttendanceStatus = decryptedData.attendanceStatus || 'attended';

        records.push({
          id: entity.id,
          episodeId: entity.episodeId,
          patientId: entity.patientId,
          sessionNumber,
          sessionDate,
          sessionType,
          status,
          attendanceStatus,
          createdAt: entity.createdAt,
          updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
          data: {
            ...decryptedData,
            sessionNumber,
            sessionDate,
            sessionType,
            status,
            attendanceStatus,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Failed to decrypt session record ${entity.id}:`, err);
      }
    }

    // Sort chronologically ascending by actual decrypted clinical date (sessionDate),
    // with sessionNumber and createdAt as deterministic tiebreakers
    records.sort((a, b) => {
      const dateA = new Date(a.sessionDate).getTime();
      const dateB = new Date(b.sessionDate).getTime();
      if (dateA !== dateB) {
        return dateA - dateB;
      }
      if (a.sessionNumber !== b.sessionNumber) {
        return a.sessionNumber - b.sessionNumber;
      }
      return new Date(a.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(b.createdAt).getTime();
    });

    return records;
  }

  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt a single session by its unique ID.
   */
  static async getSessionById(
    sessionId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SessionRecord | null> {
    if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') return null;

    const entity = await database.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!entity) return null;

    const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<SessionClinicalData>(
      dek,
      'sessions',
      entity.id,
      [entity.patientId, entity.episodeId],
      entity.iv,
      entity.ciphertext,
      entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
    );

    const sessionNumber: number = decryptedData.sessionNumber || 1;
    const sessionDate: string =
      decryptedData.sessionDate || (entity as any).sessionDate || entity.createdAt.split('T')[0];
    const sessionType: SessionType =
      decryptedData.sessionType || (entity as any).sessionType || 'follow_up';
    const status: SessionStatus =
      decryptedData.status || (entity as any).sessionStatus || (entity as any).status || 'draft';
    const attendanceStatus: AttendanceStatus = decryptedData.attendanceStatus || 'attended';

    return {
      id: entity.id,
      episodeId: entity.episodeId,
      patientId: entity.patientId,
      sessionNumber,
      sessionDate,
      sessionType,
      status,
      attendanceStatus,
      createdAt: entity.createdAt,
      updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
      data: {
        ...decryptedData,
        sessionNumber,
        sessionDate,
        sessionType,
        status,
        attendanceStatus,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Atomically reserve the next session number for an episode in IndexedDB.
   * Uses a Dexie readwrite transaction on `episodeSequences` to guarantee mutual exclusion
   * across multiple browser tabs and windows sharing the same database origin.
   * Protects session numbers by storing them as opaque HMAC-SHA-256 tokens rather than plaintext integers.
   */
  static async reserveNextSessionNumber(
    episodeId: string,
    requestedNumber: number | undefined,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb,
    explicitExpectedGeneration?: number
  ): Promise<number> {
    const actualBoundGeneration = getDekGeneration(dek);
    if (actualBoundGeneration === undefined) {
      throw new UnboundVaultKeyError(
        'تم رفض حجز رقم الجلسة: مفتاح التشفير التشغيلي (DEK) غير مرتبط بأي جيل خزينة معتمد (Unbound Vault Key). لا يمكن حجز رقم تسلسلي بمفتاح مجهول الجيل.'
      );
    }

    if (
      explicitExpectedGeneration !== undefined &&
      explicitExpectedGeneration !== actualBoundGeneration
    ) {
      throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
        `تم رفض حجز رقم الجلسة: الجيل الممرر صراحة (${explicitExpectedGeneration}) لا يطابق جيل الخزينة الفعلي المرتبط بالمفتاح (${actualBoundGeneration}).`
      );
    }

    const boundGen = actualBoundGeneration;

    // 1. If sequence record is not yet initialized, read existing sessions outside transaction to seed it
    const existingSeq = await database.episodeSequences.get(episodeId);
    let seededTokens: string[] = [];
    let maxSeed = 0;

    if (!existingSeq) {
      // Check vaultGeneration before reading sessions to prevent seeding with stale DEK
      const currentMeta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
      const currentGen = currentMeta?.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
      if (currentGen !== boundGen) {
        throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
          `تم رفض حجز رقم الجلسة: تم تغيير جيل الخزينة (المفتاح الحالي ينتمي للجيل: ${boundGen}، بينما جيل الخزينة في قاعدة البيانات: ${currentGen}) بسبب عملية استعادة كاملة في تبويب آخر. يرجى إعادة فتح الخزينة بالمفتاح الصحيح.`
        );
      }

      // Read and decrypt existing sessions to discover any pre-existing numbers
      const existingSessions = await this.getSessionsByEpisode(episodeId, dek, database);
      for (const s of existingSessions) {
        if (s.sessionNumber) {
          const t = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(dek, episodeId, s.sessionNumber);
          seededTokens.push(t);
          if (s.sessionNumber > maxSeed) maxSeed = s.sessionNumber;
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Pre-compute HMAC tokens OUTSIDE the transaction to avoid PrematureCommitError
    let requestedToken: string | undefined;
    if (requestedNumber !== undefined) {
      requestedToken = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(dek, episodeId, requestedNumber);
    }

    const baseSeq = existingSeq?.lastSequenceNumber || maxSeed || 0;
    const candidateTokens = new Map<number, string>();
    for (let c = baseSeq + 1; c <= baseSeq + 30; c++) {
      candidateTokens.set(c, await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(dek, episodeId, c));
    }

    // 3. Execute atomic reservation inside an exclusive IndexedDB readwrite transaction
    // MUST cover BOTH episodeSequences and vaultMeta to atomically verify vaultGeneration
    return await database.transaction('rw', [database.episodeSequences, database.vaultMeta], async () => {
      // ATOMIC GENERATION CHECK: Fences out stale DEK from allocating numbers or writing HMAC tokens
      const currentMeta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
      const currentGen = currentMeta?.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
      if (currentGen !== boundGen) {
        throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
          `تم رفض حجز رقم الجلسة: تم تغيير جيل الخزينة (المفتاح الحالي ينتمي للجيل: ${boundGen}، بينما جيل الخزينة في قاعدة البيانات: ${currentGen}) بسبب عملية استعادة كاملة في تبويب آخر. يرجى إعادة فتح الخزينة بالمفتاح الصحيح.`
        );
      }

      let seq = await database.episodeSequences.get(episodeId);

      if (!seq) {
        seq = {
          episodeId,
          lastSequenceNumber: maxSeed,
          allocatedTokens: [...seededTokens],
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
      }

      let allocated: number;

      if (requestedNumber !== undefined && requestedToken !== undefined) {
        if (seq.allocatedTokens.includes(requestedToken)) {
          throw new Error(
            `رقم الجلسة #${requestedNumber} مستخدم مسبقاً في هذه الحلقة. يجب أن يكون رقم الجلسة فريداً ضمن حلقة الرعاية.`
          );
        }
        seq.allocatedTokens.push(requestedToken);
        if (requestedNumber > seq.lastSequenceNumber) {
          seq.lastSequenceNumber = requestedNumber;
        }
        allocated = requestedNumber;
      } else {
        let candidate = seq.lastSequenceNumber + 1;
        let candidateToken = candidateTokens.get(candidate);
        while (candidateToken && seq.allocatedTokens.includes(candidateToken)) {
          candidate++;
          candidateToken = candidateTokens.get(candidate);
        }
        if (!candidateToken) {
          throw new Error('فشل تخصيص رقم متسلسل للجلسة ضمن نافذة الحجز المسبق.');
        }
        seq.lastSequenceNumber = candidate;
        seq.allocatedTokens.push(candidateToken);
        allocated = candidate;
      }

      seq.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      await database.episodeSequences.put(seq);
      return allocated;
    });
  }

  /**
   * Release an allocated session number token back if session creation fails prior to persistence,
   * or when a draft session is deleted.
   */
  static async releaseSessionNumber(
    episodeId: string,
    sessionNumber: number,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<void> {
    try {
      // Pre-compute token OUTSIDE transaction to prevent IDB transaction timeout
      const token = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(dek, episodeId, sessionNumber);
      await database.transaction('rw', database.episodeSequences, async () => {
        const seq = await database.episodeSequences.get(episodeId);
        if (seq) {
          seq.allocatedTokens = seq.allocatedTokens.filter((t) => t !== token);
          seq.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
          await database.episodeSequences.put(seq);
        }
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to release session number token:', err);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Create a new Clinical Session within an active Episode of Care.
   * Enforces referential integrity, parent matching, active episode status, and mandatory DEK reservation.
   * Combines an episode-keyed FIFO mutex (for in-tab concurrency) with an atomic IndexedDB readwrite
   * transaction on `episodeSequences` to guarantee mutual exclusion and unique numbering across browser tabs.
   */
  static async createSession(
    patientId: string,
    episodeId: string,
    data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    explicitId?: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb,
    keyGeneration?: number
  ): Promise<SessionRecord> {
    if (!patientId || typeof patientId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف المريض غير صالح.');
    }
    if (!episodeId || typeof episodeId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف حلقة الرعاية غير صالح.');
    }

    // Atomic serialization per episode: ensures concurrent requests calculate distinct sequential numbers
    return await sessionCreationMutex.runExclusive(episodeId, async () => {
      // 1. Capture bound vaultGeneration epoch from DEK in memory (FAIL-CLOSED: WeakMap is the SOLE authoritative source!)
      const actualBoundGeneration = getDekGeneration(dek);
      if (actualBoundGeneration === undefined) {
        throw new UnboundVaultKeyError(
          'تم رفض إنشاء الجلسة: مفتاح التشفير التشغيلي (DEK) غير مرتبط بأي جيل خزينة معتمد (Unbound Vault Key). لا يمكن استنتاج جيل المفتاح من قاعدة البيانات.'
        );
      }

      if (
        keyGeneration !== undefined &&
        keyGeneration !== actualBoundGeneration
      ) {
        throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
          `تم رفض إنشاء الجلسة: جيل المفتاح الممرر صراحة (${keyGeneration}) لا يطابق جيل الخزينة الفعلي المرتبط بالمفتاح (${actualBoundGeneration}).`
        );
      }

      const expectedVaultGeneration = actualBoundGeneration;

      // 2. Referential integrity: check patient exists
      const patient = await database.patients.get(patientId);
      if (!patient) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`لا يمكن إنشاء جلسة: المريض بالمعرف "${patientId}" غير موجود.`);
      }

      // 3. Referential integrity: check episode exists and belongs to this patient
      const episode = await EpisodeService.getEpisodeById(episodeId, dek, database);
      if (!episode) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`لا يمكن إنشاء جلسة: حلقة الرعاية بالمعرف "${episodeId}" غير موجودة.`);
      }
      if (episode.patientId !== patientId) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(
          `فشل التحقق من النزاهة المرجعية: حلقة الرعاية "${episodeId}" لا تنتمي للمريض "${patientId}".`
        );
      }

      // 4. Episode lifecycle rule: Cannot create session in closed or archived episode
      if (episode.status === 'completed' || episode.status === 'archived') {
        throw new Error(
          `لا يمكن إنشاء جلسة جديدة: حلقة الرعاية في حالة (${episode.status === 'completed' ? 'مكتملة' : 'مؤرشفة'}). يرجى إعادة فتح الحلقة أولاً.`
        );
      }

      // 5. Validate mandatory session fields
      if (!data.sessionType) {
        throw new Error('نوع الجلسة العلاجية إلزامي (Session Type is required).');
      }

      const id = explicitId || crypto.randomUUID();

      // Check duplicate ID
      const duplicate = await database.sessions.get(id);
      if (duplicate) {
        throw new Error(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${id}" موجودة مسبقاً.`);
      }

      // Cross-tab atomic reservation in IndexedDB: guarantees unique sessionNumber across tabs
      // AND verifies vaultGeneration inside the atomic reservation transaction
      const sessionNumber = await this.reserveNextSessionNumber(
        episodeId,
        data.sessionNumber,
        dek,
        database,
        expectedVaultGeneration
      );

      // Pre-compute HMAC reservation token outside transaction to avoid non-IDB async delays
      const sessionToken = await computeSessionNumberHmacToken(dek, episodeId, sessionNumber);

      // Optional test hook invocation (stage: 'after-reservation')
      if (sessionCreationTestHook) {
        await sessionCreationTestHook('after-reservation', {
          episodeId,
          sessionNumber,
          expectedVaultGeneration,
        });
      }

      try {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        const sessionDate = data.sessionDate || now.split('T')[0];
        const sessionDurationMinutes = data.sessionDurationMinutes || 45;
        const sessionType = data.sessionType;
        const status: SessionStatus = 'draft'; // Always starts as draft
        const attendanceStatus: AttendanceStatus = data.attendanceStatus || 'attended';

        const fullClinicalData: SessionClinicalData = {
          ...data,
          sessionNumber,
          sessionDate,
          sessionDurationMinutes,
          sessionType,
          status,
          attendanceStatus,
        };

        // Centralized encryption with MANDATORY transactional invocation counter reservation
        // Canonical AAD: sessions:id:patientId,episodeId:1:dekVersion
        const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
        const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

        const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
          database,
          dek,
          'sessions',
          id,
          [patientId, episodeId],
          fullClinicalData,
          CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          activeDekVersion,
          expectedVaultGeneration
        );

        // Optional test hook invocation (stage: 'before-commit')
        if (sessionCreationTestHook) {
          await sessionCreationTestHook('before-commit', {
            episodeId,
            sessionNumber,
            expectedVaultGeneration,
          });
        }

        // Entity stored in IndexedDB: strictly random IDs and technical timestamps
        // No Plaintext Clinical Payload — Technical Metadata Remains Visible
        const entity: EncryptedSessionEntity = {
          id,
          episodeId,
          patientId,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
          recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
          iv,
          ciphertext,
        };

        // VAULT GENERATION & RESERVATION FENCE:
        // Open an exclusive Dexie read-write transaction covering sessions, episodeSequences, and vaultMeta.
        // Guarantees that:
        // 1. vaultGeneration has NOT changed (fences out concurrent Full Restore that rotated keys)
        // 2. The reserved HMAC token is still present and valid in episodeSequences
        // 3. The session entity is committed atomically in the same transaction
        await database.transaction(
          'rw',
          [database.sessions, database.episodeSequences, database.vaultMeta],
          async () => {
            const currentMeta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
            const currentGen = currentMeta?.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
            if (currentGen !== expectedVaultGeneration) {
              throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
                `تم رفض حفظ الجلسة: تم تغيير جيل الخزينة (متوقع: ${expectedVaultGeneration}، الحالي: ${currentGen}) بواسطة عملية استعادة كاملة أثناء العملية. تم إلغاء العملية لحماية اتساق التشفير وقاعدة البيانات.`
              );
            }

            const seq = await database.episodeSequences.get(episodeId);
            if (!seq || !seq.allocatedTokens.includes(sessionToken)) {
              throw new VaultGenerationChangedError(
                `تم رفض حفظ الجلسة: حجز رقم الجلسة #${sessionNumber} لم يعد موجوداً أو صالحاً في تسلسل الحلقة (ربما تم استبدال قاعدة البيانات بالكامل).`
              );
            }

            await database.sessions.put(entity);
          }
        );

        return {
          id,
          episodeId,
          patientId,
          sessionNumber,
          sessionDate,
          sessionType,
          status,
          attendanceStatus,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
          data: fullClinicalData,
        };
      } catch (err) {
        // If the error was caused by a vault generation change (e.g. database replaced by Full Restore)
        // or an unbound key error, do NOT attempt releaseSessionNumber because the local database was already replaced
        // or the reservation token was never properly allocated under a valid generation.
        if (!(err instanceof VaultGenerationChangedError) && !(err instanceof UnboundVaultKeyError)) {
          await this.releaseSessionNumber(episodeId, sessionNumber, dek, database);
        }
        throw err;
      }
    });
  }

  /**
   * Update an existing draft Clinical Session.
   * Prevents direct modifications of finalized sessions.
   */
  static async updateSession(
    sessionId: string,
    data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SessionRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getSessionById(sessionId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    // IMMUTABILITY RULE: Finalized sessions cannot be modified directly
    if (existing.status === 'finalized') {
      throw new Error(
        'لا يمكن تعديل جلسة سريرية معتمدة نهائياً. يسمح النظام فقط بإضافة ملحق سريري (Clinical Addendum) للتصحيح أو الاستكمال.'
      );
    }

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const updatedData: SessionClinicalData = {
      ...existing.data,
      ...data,
      sessionNumber: existing.sessionNumber, // preserve immutable sequence number
      status: existing.status, // preserve draft status
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'sessions',
      sessionId,
      [existing.patientId, existing.episodeId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedSessionEntity = {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.sessions.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      sessionNumber: existing.sessionNumber,
      sessionDate: updatedData.sessionDate,
      sessionType: updatedData.sessionType,
      status: updatedData.status,
      attendanceStatus: updatedData.attendanceStatus,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Cancel a draft session with documented cancellation reason.
   */
  static async cancelSession(
    sessionId: string,
    cancellationReason: string,
    attendanceStatus: 'cancelled_patient' | 'cancelled_clinic' | 'no_show',
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SessionRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getSessionById(sessionId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (existing.status === 'finalized') {
      throw new Error('لا يمكن إلغاء جلسة سريرية معتمدة ومغلقة نهائياً.');
    }

    if (!cancellationReason?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('سبب إلغاء الجلسة إلزامي للتوثيق الإداري والسريري.');
    }

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const updatedData: SessionClinicalData = {
      ...existing.data,
      status: 'cancelled',
      attendanceStatus,
      cancellationReason: cancellationReason.trim(),
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'sessions',
      sessionId,
      [existing.patientId, existing.episodeId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const entity: EncryptedSessionEntity = {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.sessions.put(entity);

    return {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      sessionNumber: existing.sessionNumber,
      sessionDate: updatedData.sessionDate,
      sessionType: updatedData.sessionType,
      status: 'cancelled',
      attendanceStatus,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Finalize a Clinical Session and its associated SOAP Note.
   * Strictly enforces:
   * 1. Attended sessions MUST have a completed SOAP note before finalization.
   * 2. Subjective chief complaint, Assessment impression, and Plan must be filled.
   * 3. Calculates content integrity digest (SHA-256) of clinical record.
   * 4. Locks both session and SOAP note against further direct edits.
   */
  static async finalizeSession(
    sessionId: string,
    therapistName: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SessionRecord> {
    if (!therapistName?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('اسم المعالج الطبيعي المعتمد إلزامي لاعتماد السجل السريري.');
    }

    const session = await this.getSessionById(sessionId, dek, database);
    if (!session) {
      throw new Error(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (session.status === 'finalized') {
      throw new Error('هذه الجلسة معتمدة مسبقاً.');
    }

    let soapDigest = '';
    // If attended, SOAP note is mandatory and ALL 4 AXES must be addressed!
    if (session.attendanceStatus === 'attended') {
      const soap = await SoapService.getSoapNoteBySession(sessionId, dek, database);
      if (!soap) {
        throw new Error(
          'لا يمكن اعتماد الجلسة السريرية: يجب توثيق وإكمال ملاحظة SOAP أولاً للجلسات التي حضرها المريض.'
        );
      }

      // 1. Check Axis S (Subjective): Chief Complaint is mandatory
      if (!soap.data.subjective?.chiefComplaint?.trim()) {
        throw new Error('الشكوى الحالية للمريض (Chief Complaint) إلزامية في محور S لاعتماد الجلسة.');
      }

      // 2. Check Axis O (Objective): Must document findings or explicitly justify not_assessed
      const obj = soap.data.objective;
      const hasObjectiveFindings = Boolean(
        obj?.objectiveFindings?.trim() ||
        obj?.clinicalObservations?.trim() ||
        obj?.functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements?.trim() ||
        obj?.dailyInterventionsSummary?.trim() ||
        (obj?.romBattery?.entries && obj.romBattery.entries.length > 0) ||
        (obj?.mmtBattery?.entries && obj.mmtBattery.entries.length > 0) ||
        (obj?.neuroScreening && (
          (obj.neuroScreening.myotomes && obj.neuroScreening.myotomes.length > 0) ||
          (obj.neuroScreening.dermatomes && obj.neuroScreening.dermatomes.length > 0) ||
          (obj.neuroScreening.reflexes && obj.neuroScreening.reflexes.length > 0)
        )) ||
        obj?.isWithinNormalLimits ||
        obj?.status === 'normal_findings'
      );
      const isExplicitlyNotAssessed =
        obj?.status === 'not_assessed' && Boolean(obj?.notAssessedReason?.trim());

      if (!hasObjectiveFindings && !isExplicitlyNotAssessed) {
        throw new Error(
          'محور الفحص والملاحظات الموضوعية (Objective — O) إلزامي لاعتماد الجلسة. يجب تدوين نتائج الفحص السريري أو تحديد حالة "لم يتم إجراء فحص موضوعي" مع تسجيل السبب السريري المبرر.'
        );
      }

      // 3. Check Axis A (Assessment): Clinical Impression is mandatory
      if (!soap.data.assessment?.clinicalImpressionCurrent?.trim()) {
        throw new Error('الانطباع والتفسير السريري (Clinical Impression) إلزامي في محور A لاعتماد الجلسة.');
      }

      // 4. Check Axis P (Plan): Next Session Plan is mandatory
      if (!soap.data.plan?.nextSessionPlan?.trim()) {
        throw new Error('الخطة للجلسة القادمة (Next Session Plan) إلزامية في محور P لاعتماد الجلسة.');
      }

      // Finalize SOAP note as well
      await SoapService.finalizeSoapNote(soap.id, therapistName.trim(), dek, database);
      soapDigest = JSON.stringify(soap.data);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    // Compute content integrity digest (SHA-256 cryptographic digest across session and SOAP payloads for content verification)
    const contentToDigest = `${JSON.stringify(session.data)}::${soapDigest}`;
    const digestHex = await computeSha256Digest(contentToDigest);

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const finalizedData: SessionClinicalData = {
      ...session.data,
      status: 'finalized',
      finalizedAt: now,
      finalizedBy: therapistName.trim(),
      attestationByTherapistName: therapistName.trim(),
      attestationTimestamp: now,
      contentIntegrityDigestSha256: digestHex,
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'sessions',
      sessionId,
      [session.patientId, session.episodeId],
      finalizedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const entity: EncryptedSessionEntity = {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: session.episodeId,
      patientId: session.patientId,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.sessions.put(entity);

    return {
      id: sessionId,
      episodeId: session.episodeId,
      patientId: session.patientId,
      sessionNumber: session.sessionNumber,
      sessionDate: finalizedData.sessionDate,
      sessionType: finalizedData.sessionType,
      status: 'finalized',
      attendanceStatus: finalizedData.attendanceStatus,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: finalizedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Delete a draft session atomically with its child draft SOAP notes.
   * Strictly rejects deletion of finalized sessions and finalized SOAP notes.
   */
  static async deleteSession(
    sessionId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<void> {
    const session = await this.getSessionById(sessionId, dek, database);
    if (!session) {
      throw new Error(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (session.status === 'finalized') {
      throw new CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError(
        'لا يمكن حذف جلسة سريرية معتمدة ومؤرخة. يجب الحفاظ على السجلات السريرية التاريخية وفق سياسة الاحتفاظ السريري.'
      );
    }

    // Append-only integrity: verify no clinical addenda exist for this session
    const addendaCount = await database.clinicalAddenda.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).count();
    if (addendaCount > 0) {
      throw new CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError(
        'لا يمكن حذف الجلسة: توجد ملاحق سريرية (Clinical Addenda) مرتبطة بها. سياسة الاحتفاظ السريري تمنع حذف السجلات التي تحتوي على ملاحق موثقة.'
      );
    }

    // Check if the associated SOAP note is finalized
    const soap = await SoapService.getSoapNoteBySession(sessionId, dek, database);
    if (soap && soap.data.isFinalized) {
      throw new CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError(
        'لا يمكن حذف الجلسة: ملاحظة SOAP المرتبطة بها معتمدة نهائياً.'
      );
    }

    await database.deleteSessionCascade(sessionId);
    await this.releaseSessionNumber(session.episodeId, session.sessionNumber, dek, database);
  }
}

=== FILE: src/services/soapService.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — SOAP Clinical Documentation & Addenda Service Layer (v2.0)
// 1:1 Session-to-SOAP Constraint, Immutability Locking, and Canonical AAD
// =======================================================================

import { db as defaultDb, PhysioRecordDatabase } from '../db/db';
import {
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import { ReferentialIntegrityError } from '../db/registry';
import {
  CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError,
  type SoapNoteRecord,
  type SoapNoteData,
  type EncryptedSoapEntity,
  type ClinicalAddendumRecord,
  type ClinicalAddendumData,
  type EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity,
  type AddendumReason,
  type SessionClinicalData,
} from '../types/clinical';

export class SoapService {
  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt the unique SOAP Note for a given Clinical Session.
   * Enforces 1:1 relationship check.
   */
  static async getSoapNoteBySession(
    sessionId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord | null> {
    if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') return null;

    const entity = await database.soapNotes.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).first();
    if (!entity) return null;

    return this.decryptSoapEntity(entity, dek);
  }

  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt a SOAP Note by its primary ID.
   */
  static async getSoapNoteById(
    soapId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord | null> {
    if (!soapId || typeof soapId !== 'string') return null;

    const entity = await database.soapNotes.get(soapId);
    if (!entity) return null;

    return this.decryptSoapEntity(entity, dek);
  }

  /**
   * Decrypt a raw EncryptedSoapEntity from the database.
   */
  private static async decryptSoapEntity(
    entity: EncryptedSoapEntity,
    dek: CryptoKey
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> {
    const parentIds = entity.episodeId
      ? [entity.patientId, entity.episodeId, entity.sessionId]
      : [entity.patientId, entity.sessionId]; // backward-compatibility fallback

    const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<SoapNoteData>(
      dek,
      'soapNotes',
      entity.id,
      parentIds,
      entity.iv,
      entity.ciphertext,
      entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
    );

    return {
      id: entity.id,
      sessionId: entity.sessionId,
      episodeId: entity.episodeId,
      patientId: entity.patientId,
      createdAt: entity.createdAt,
      updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
      data: decryptedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Create a new SOAP note for a Clinical Session.
   * Strictly enforces 1:1 relationship: rejects creation if a SOAP note already exists for this session.
   * Enforces referential integrity: session and parent patient/episode must exist and match.
   */
  static async createSoapNote(
    patientId: string,
    episodeId: string,
    sessionId: string,
    data: SoapNoteData,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    explicitId?: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> {
    if (!patientId || typeof patientId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف المريض غير صالح.');
    }
    if (!episodeId || typeof episodeId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف حلقة الرعاية غير صالح.');
    }
    if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف الجلسة غير صالح.');
    }

    // 1. Verify parent session exists
    const session = await database.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`لا يمكن إنشاء توثيق SOAP: الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    // 2. Verify referential integrity bindings
    if (session.patientId !== patientId) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(
        `فشل التحقق من النزاهة المرجعية: الجلسة ترتبط بمريض مختلف (${session.patientId} != ${patientId}).`
      );
    }
    if (session.episodeId !== episodeId) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(
        `فشل التحقق من النزاهة المرجعية: الجلسة ترتبط بحلقة رعاية مختلفة (${session.episodeId} != ${episodeId}).`
      );
    }

    // 3. STRICT 1:1 ENFORCEMENT: Reject duplicate SOAP note for the same session!
    const existingSoap = await database.soapNotes.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).first();
    if (existingSoap) {
      throw new Error(
        `توجد ملاحظة SOAP مسجلة مسبقاً للجلسة "${sessionId}". لا يمكن إنشاء أكثر من ملاحظة سريرية واحدة لنفس الجلسة العلاجية.`
      );
    }

    const id = explicitId || crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const score =
      data.subjective?.painNrsScore ??
      data.subjective?.painVasCurrent ??
      data.subjective?.painAssessment?.score ??
      0;

    const painAssessment = data.subjective?.painAssessment || {
      scale: 'NRS_0_10' as const,
      score,
      range: '0-10' as const,
      unit: 'points' as const,
      assessedAt: now.split('T')[0],
      scoreBestLast24h: data.subjective?.painVasBestLast24h,
      scoreWorstLast24h: data.subjective?.painVasWorstLast24h,
    };

    const fullData: SoapNoteData = {
      ...data,
      subjective: {
        ...data.subjective,
        painNrsScore: score,
        painAssessment,
        painVasCurrent: score,
      },
      isFinalized: false,
    };

    // Centralized encryption with MANDATORY invocation counter reservation
    // Canonical AAD: soapNotes:id:patientId,episodeId,sessionId:1:dekVersion
    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'soapNotes',
      id,
      [patientId, episodeId, sessionId],
      fullData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const entity: EncryptedSoapEntity = {
      id,
      sessionId,
      episodeId,
      patientId,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.soapNotes.put(entity);

    return {
      id,
      sessionId,
      episodeId,
      patientId,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: fullData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Update an existing draft SOAP note.
   * Rejects modification if the associated session is finalized.
   */
  static async updateSoapNote(
    soapId: string,
    data: SoapNoteData,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getSoapNoteById(soapId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`ملاحظة SOAP بالمعرف "${soapId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    // IMMUTABILITY RULE: Cannot update finalized note
    if (existing.data.isFinalized) {
      throw new Error(
        'لا يمكن تعديل ملاحظة SOAP معتمدة نهائياً. يسمح النظام بإضافة ملحق سريري (Clinical Addendum) للتصحيح.'
      );
    }

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    let score = existing.data.subjective?.painNrsScore ?? existing.data.subjective?.painVasCurrent ?? 0;
    if (
      data.subjective?.painVasCurrent !== undefined &&
      data.subjective.painVasCurrent !== existing.data.subjective?.painVasCurrent
    ) {
      score = data.subjective.painVasCurrent;
    } else if (
      data.subjective?.painNrsScore !== undefined &&
      data.subjective.painNrsScore !== existing.data.subjective?.painNrsScore
    ) {
      score = data.subjective.painNrsScore;
    } else if (data.subjective?.painAssessment?.score !== undefined) {
      score = data.subjective.painAssessment.score;
    } else if (data.subjective?.painNrsScore !== undefined) {
      score = data.subjective.painNrsScore;
    } else if (data.subjective?.painVasCurrent !== undefined) {
      score = data.subjective.painVasCurrent;
    }

    const basePainAssessment = data.subjective?.painAssessment || existing.data.subjective?.painAssessment;
    const painAssessment = basePainAssessment
      ? { ...basePainAssessment, score }
      : {
          scale: 'NRS_0_10' as const,
          score,
          range: '0-10' as const,
          unit: 'points' as const,
          assessedAt: now.split('T')[0],
          scoreBestLast24h: data.subjective?.painVasBestLast24h ?? existing.data.subjective?.painVasBestLast24h,
          scoreWorstLast24h: data.subjective?.painVasWorstLast24h ?? existing.data.subjective?.painVasWorstLast24h,
        };

    const updatedData: SoapNoteData = {
      ...existing.data,
      ...data,
      subjective: {
        ...existing.data.subjective,
        ...data.subjective,
        painNrsScore: score,
        painAssessment,
        painVasCurrent: score,
      },
      isFinalized: false,
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'soapNotes',
      soapId,
      [existing.patientId, existing.episodeId, existing.sessionId],
      updatedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedSoapEntity = {
      id: soapId,
      sessionId: existing.sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.soapNotes.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: soapId,
      sessionId: existing.sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: updatedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Internal finalization helper called during session finalization.
   */
  static async finalizeSoapNote(
    soapId: string,
    therapistName: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> {
    const existing = await this.getSoapNoteById(soapId, dek, database);
    if (!existing) {
      throw new Error(`ملاحظة SOAP بالمعرف "${soapId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    const finalizedData: SoapNoteData = {
      ...existing.data,
      isFinalized: true,
      finalizedAt: now,
      finalizedBy: therapistName,
    };

    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'soapNotes',
      soapId,
      [existing.patientId, existing.episodeId, existing.sessionId],
      finalizedData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const updatedEntity: EncryptedSoapEntity = {
      id: soapId,
      sessionId: existing.sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.soapNotes.put(updatedEntity);

    return {
      id: soapId,
      sessionId: existing.sessionId,
      episodeId: existing.episodeId,
      patientId: existing.patientId,
      createdAt: existing.createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      data: finalizedData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Delete an unfinalized draft SOAP note.
   * Strictly rejects deletion if the SOAP note is finalized or its parent session is finalized.
   */
  static async deleteSoapNote(
    soapId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<void> {
    const soap = await this.getSoapNoteById(soapId, dek, database);
    if (!soap) {
      throw new Error(`ملاحظة SOAP بالمعرف "${soapId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    if (soap.data.isFinalized) {
      throw new CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError(
        'لا يمكن حذف ملاحظة SOAP معتمدة نهائياً. يجب الحفاظ على السجلات السريرية المعتمدة وفق سياسة الاحتفاظ.'
      );
    }

    const sessionEntity = await database.sessions.get(soap.sessionId);
    if (sessionEntity) {
      const sessionDecrypted = await decryptRecordPayload<SessionClinicalData>(
        dek,
        'sessions',
        sessionEntity.id,
        [sessionEntity.patientId, sessionEntity.episodeId],
        sessionEntity.iv,
        sessionEntity.ciphertext,
        sessionEntity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
        sessionEntity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
      );
      if (sessionDecrypted.status === 'finalized') {
        throw new CannotDeleteFinalizedRecordError(
          'لا يمكن حذف ملاحظة SOAP التابعة لجلسة سريرية معتمدة ومؤرخة.'
        );
      }
    }

    await database.soapNotes.delete(soapId);
  }

  /**
   * Add a Clinical Addendum (correction/amendment) to a finalized session or SOAP note.
   * Preserves the immutable original record and appends a cryptographically authenticated amendment.
   */
  static async addClinicalAddendum(
    sessionId: string,
    targetTable: 'sessions' | 'soapNotes',
    targetId: string,
    data: {
      reasonForAmendment: AddendumReason;
      amendmentText: string;
      signedByTherapistName: string;
    },
    dek: CryptoKey,
    explicitId?: string,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord> {
    if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف الجلسة غير صالح لإضافة ملحق.');
    }
    if (!targetId || typeof targetId !== 'string') {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError('معرّف السجل المستهدف غير صالح لإضافة ملحق.');
    }
    if (!data.amendmentText?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('نص التصحيح أو الإضافة السريرية إلزامي (Amendment Text is required).');
    }
    if (!data.signedByTherapistName?.trim()) {
      throw new Error('اسم وتوقيع المعالج الطبيعي إلزامي لاعتماد الملحق السريري.');
    }

    const session = await database.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session) {
      throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`الجلسة بالمعرف "${sessionId}" غير موجودة.`);
    }

    // Append-only rule: clinical addenda can ONLY be attached to finalized records
    const sessionDecrypted = await decryptRecordPayload<SessionClinicalData>(
      dek,
      'sessions',
      session.id,
      [session.patientId, session.episodeId],
      session.iv,
      session.ciphertext,
      session.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      session.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
    );

    if (sessionDecrypted.status !== 'finalized') {
      throw new Error(
        'لا يمكن إضافة ملحق سريري لجلسة مسودة أو غير معتمدة. الملاحق السريرية مخصصة حصرياً للسجلات المعتمدة نهائياً.'
      );
    }

    if (targetTable === 'soapNotes') {
      let soap = await this.getSoapNoteById(targetId, dek, database);
      if (!soap) {
        // Fallback: check if targetId refers to the sessionId
        soap = await this.getSoapNoteBySession(targetId, dek, database);
      }
      if (!soap) {
        throw new ReferentialIntegrityError(`ملاحظة SOAP بالمعرف "${targetId}" غير موجودة.`);
      }
      if (!soap.data.isFinalized) {
        throw new Error(
          'لا يمكن إضافة ملحق سريري لملاحظة SOAP غير معتمدة. الملاحق السريرية مخصصة حصرياً للسجلات المعتمدة نهائياً.'
        );
      }
    }

    const id = explicitId || crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    const addendumData: ClinicalAddendumData = {
      targetTable,
      targetId,
      reasonForAmendment: data.reasonForAmendment,
      amendmentText: data.amendmentText.trim(),
      signedByTherapistName: data.signedByTherapistName.trim(),
      createdAt: now,
    };

    const meta = await database.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    // Canonical AAD: clinicalAddenda:id:patientId,sessionId:1:dekVersion
    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      database,
      dek,
      'clinicalAddenda',
      id,
      [session.patientId, sessionId],
      addendumData,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const entity: EncryptedClinicalAddendumEntity = {
      id,
      sessionId,
      patientId: session.patientId,
      targetId,
      createdAt: now,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    await database.clinicalAddenda.put(entity);

    return {
      id,
      sessionId,
      patientId: session.patientId,
      targetId,
      createdAt: now,
      data: addendumData,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Fetch and decrypt all Clinical Addenda associated with a given Session.
   */
  static async getAddendaBySession(
    sessionId: string,
    dek: CryptoKey,
    database: PhysioRecordDatabase = defaultDb
  ): Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord[]> {
    if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') return [];

    const entities = await database.clinicalAddenda.where('sessionId').equals(sessionId).toArray();
    const records: ClinicalAddendumRecord[] = [];

    for (const entity of entities) {
      try {
        const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<ClinicalAddendumData>(
          dek,
          'clinicalAddenda',
          entity.id,
          [entity.patientId, entity.sessionId],
          entity.iv,
          entity.ciphertext,
          entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
          entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
        );

        records.push({
          id: entity.id,
          sessionId: entity.sessionId,
          patientId: entity.patientId,
          targetId: entity.targetId,
          createdAt: entity.createdAt,
          data: decryptedData,
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Failed to decrypt addendum ${entity.id}:`, err);
      }
    }

    records.sort((a, b) => new Date(a.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(b.createdAt).getTime());
    return records;
  }

  /**
   * Append-only enforcement: Modifying an existing Clinical Addendum is strictly forbidden.
   */
  static async updateClinicalAddendum(): Promise<never> {
    throw new Error(
      'محظور: الملاحق السريرية (Clinical Addenda) هي سجل تراكمي غير قابل للتعديل (Append-only Ledger).'
    );
  }

  /**
   * Append-only enforcement: Deleting an existing Clinical Addendum is strictly forbidden.
   */
  static async deleteClinicalAddendum(): Promise<never> {
    throw new Error(
      'محظور: الملاحق السريرية (Clinical Addenda) هي سجل تراكمي غير قابل للحذف (Append-only Ledger).'
    );
  }
}

=== FILE: src/context/EpisodeContext.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Episode Context (Phase 2B.1)
// Reactive State Management & Strict Memory Scrubbing on Vault Lock
// =======================================================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { useVault } from './VaultContext';
import { EpisodeService } from '../services/episodeService';
import type { EpisodeRecord, EpisodeClinicalData, EpisodeStatus } from '../types/clinical';

interface EpisodeContextType {
  episodes: EpisodeRecord[];
  loadingEpisodes: boolean;
  activePatientId: string | null;
  loadEpisodesForPatient: (patientId: string) => Promise<EpisodeRecord[]>;
  createEpisode: (patientId: string, data: EpisodeClinicalData) => Promise<EpisodeRecord>;
  updateEpisode: (episodeId: string, data: EpisodeClinicalData) => Promise<EpisodeRecord>;
  setEpisodeStatus: (episodeId: string, status: EpisodeStatus, notes?: string) => Promise<EpisodeRecord>;
  closeEpisode: (
    episodeId: string,
    dischargeInfo: {
      endDate: string;
      dischargeSummary: string;
      dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
    }
  ) => Promise<EpisodeRecord>;
  archiveEpisode: (episodeId: string) => Promise<void>;
  unarchiveEpisode: (episodeId: string) => Promise<void>;
  deleteEpisodeSafeguarded: (episodeId: string) => Promise<void>;
  clearEpisodes: () => void;
}

const EpisodeContext = createContext<EpisodeContextType | null>(null);

export const EpisodeProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isLocked, dek } = useVault();
  const [episodes, setEpisodes] = useState<EpisodeRecord[]>([]);
  const [activePatientId, setActivePatientId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingEpisodes, setLoadingEpisodes] = useState(false);

  // Synchronization refs for sensitive memory protection
  const isLockedRef = useRef(isLocked);
  isLockedRef.current = isLocked;
  const dekRef = useRef(dek);
  dekRef.current = dek;

  const clearEpisodes = useCallback(() => {
    setEpisodes([]);
    setActivePatientId(null);
  }, []);

  // Scrub memory when vault is locked
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLocked) {
      clearEpisodes();
    }
  }, [isLocked, clearEpisodes]);

  const loadEpisodesForPatient = useCallback(
    async (patientId: string): Promise<EpisodeRecord[]> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) {
        clearEpisodes();
        return [];
      }

      setLoadingEpisodes(true);
      setActivePatientId(patientId);

      try {
        const records = await EpisodeService.getEpisodesByPatient(patientId, dekRef.current);
        if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) {
          clearEpisodes();
          return [];
        }
        setEpisodes(records);
        return records;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load episodes for patient:', err);
        setEpisodes([]);
        return [];
      } finally {
        setLoadingEpisodes(false);
      }
    },
    [clearEpisodes]
  );

  const createEpisode = async (
    patientId: string,
    data: EpisodeClinicalData
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const created = await EpisodeService.createEpisode(patientId, data, dek);
    setEpisodes(prev => [created, ...prev]);
    return created;
  };

  const updateEpisode = async (
    episodeId: string,
    data: EpisodeClinicalData
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const updated = await EpisodeService.updateEpisode(episodeId, data, dek);
    setEpisodes(prev => prev.map(ep => (ep.id === episodeId ? updated : ep)));
    return updated;
  };

  const setEpisodeStatus = async (
    episodeId: string,
    status: EpisodeStatus,
    notes?: string
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const updated = await EpisodeService.setEpisodeStatus(episodeId, status, dek, notes);
    setEpisodes(prev => prev.map(ep => (ep.id === episodeId ? updated : ep)));
    return updated;
  };

  const closeEpisode = async (
    episodeId: string,
    dischargeInfo: {
      endDate: string;
      dischargeSummary: string;
      dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
    }
  ): Promise<EpisodeRecord> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const updated = await EpisodeService.closeEpisode(episodeId, dischargeInfo, dek);
    setEpisodes(prev => prev.map(ep => (ep.id === episodeId ? updated : ep)));
    return updated;
  };

  const archiveEpisode = async (episodeId: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const updated = await EpisodeService.archiveEpisode(episodeId, dek);
    setEpisodes(prev =>
      prev.map(ep => (ep.id === episodeId ? updated : ep))
    );
  };

  const unarchiveEpisode = async (episodeId: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const updated = await EpisodeService.unarchiveEpisode(episodeId, dek);
    setEpisodes(prev =>
      prev.map(ep => (ep.id === episodeId ? updated : ep))
    );
  };

  const deleteEpisodeSafeguarded = async (episodeId: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    await EpisodeService.deleteEpisodeSafeguarded(episodeId);
    setEpisodes(prev => prev.filter(ep => ep.id !== episodeId));
  };

  return (
    <EpisodeContext.Provider
      value={{
        episodes,
        loadingEpisodes,
        activePatientId,
        loadEpisodesForPatient,
        createEpisode,
        updateEpisode,
        setEpisodeStatus,
        closeEpisode,
        archiveEpisode,
        unarchiveEpisode,
        deleteEpisodeSafeguarded,
        clearEpisodes,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </EpisodeContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useEpisodes = (): EpisodeContextType => {
  const context = useContext(EpisodeContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useEpisodes must be used within an EpisodeProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

// PhysioRecord — Language & Internationalization Context (v2.0)

interface LanguageContextType {

  setLanguage: (lang: SupportedLanguage) => void;
