const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextType | null>(null);

export const LanguageProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<SupportedLanguage>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('physiorecord_language');
      if (saved === 'en' || saved === 'ar') return saved;
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }
    return 'ar';
  });

  const isRtl = language === 'ar';

  const setLanguage = (lang: SupportedLanguage) => {
    setLanguageState(lang);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('physiorecord_language', lang);
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }
  };

  const toggleLanguage = () => {
    setLanguage(language === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar');
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dir = isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
    document.documentElement.lang = language;
  }, [language, isRtl]);

  const t = (key: string): string => {
    return translations[language][key] || translations['ar'][key] || key;
  };

  const bi = (arText: string, enText: string): string => {
    return language === 'ar' ? arText : enText;
  };

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ language, setLanguage, toggleLanguage, isRtl, t, bi }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useLanguage = (): LanguageContextType => {
  const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

=== FILE: src/context/PatientContext.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Patient Data Context (Envelope Encryption v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db/db';
import { useVault } from './VaultContext';
import {
  encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation,
  decryptRecordPayload,
  CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import type { PatientRecord, PatientClinicalData, EncryptedPatientEntity } from '../types/clinical';

interface PatientContextType {
  patients: PatientRecord[];
  activePatients: PatientRecord[];
  archivedPatients: PatientRecord[];
  loading: boolean;
  savePatient: (data: PatientClinicalData, existingId?: string) => Promise<PatientRecord>;
  archivePatient: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  unarchivePatient: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  deletePatientPermanently: (
    id: string,
    backupProof?: { verifiedBackupString: string; bypassForTest?: boolean }
  ) => Promise<void>;
  refreshPatients: () => Promise<void>;
}

const PatientContext = createContext<PatientContextType | null>(null);

export const PatientProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isLocked, dek } = useVault();
  const [patients, setPatients] = useState<PatientRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // Synchronization refs to eliminate asynchronous state leakage on vault lock
  const isLockedRef = useRef(isLocked);
  isLockedRef.current = isLocked;

  const dekRef = useRef<CryptoKey | null>(dek);
  dekRef.current = dek;

  const fetchGenerationRef = useRef(0);

  // Load and decrypt all patients using DEK and verifying AAD
  const refreshPatients = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isLocked || !dek) {
      setPatients([]);
      return;
    }

    const currentGeneration = ++fetchGenerationRef.current;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const encryptedEntities = await db.patients.toArray();
      const decryptedList: PatientRecord[] = [];

      for (const entity of encryptedEntities) {
        // Abort immediately if the vault was locked while async decryption was running!
        if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current || fetchGenerationRef.current !== currentGeneration) {
          return;
        }

        try {
          // Decrypt with DEK and verify AAD binding (including dekVersion)
          const decryptedData = await decryptRecordPayload<PatientClinicalData>(
            dekRef.current,
            'patients',
            entity.id,
            [], // patients table has no parent IDs
            entity.iv,
            entity.ciphertext,
            entity.recordCryptoVersion || CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
            entity.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION
          );

          decryptedList.push({
            id: entity.id,
            createdAt: entity.createdAt,
            updatedAt: entity.updatedAt,
            isArchived: entity.isArchived ?? 0,
            data: decryptedData,
          });
        } catch (decryptErr) {
          console.error(`Failed to decrypt patient record ${entity.id} (AAD or Auth Tag failure):`, decryptErr);
        }
      }

      // Check again before committing to React state
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current || fetchGenerationRef.current !== currentGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      // Sort by newest updated
      decryptedList.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.updatedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt).getTime());
      setPatients(decryptedList);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error fetching patients from local database:', err);
    } finally {
      if (fetchGenerationRef.current === currentGeneration) {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
  }, [isLocked, dek]);

  // Sync with vault lock status
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLocked && dek) {
      refreshPatients();
    } else {
      setPatients([]); // Best-effort sensitive memory cleanup on lock
    }
  }, [isLocked, dek, refreshPatients]);

  // Create or Update Patient Record
  const savePatient = async (data: PatientClinicalData, existingId?: string): Promise<PatientRecord> => {
    if (!dek || isLocked) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const id = existingId || crypto.randomUUID();
    const existingPatient = existingId ? patients.find(p => p.id === existingId) : null;
    const isArchived = existingPatient?.isArchived ?? 0;
    const createdAt = existingPatient?.createdAt || now;

    // Get active dekVersion
    const meta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    const activeDekVersion = meta?.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION;

    // Centralized encryption with MANDATORY invocation counter reservation
    // Atomically reserves counter on database.vaultMeta first, then encrypts with Web Crypto outside Dexie transaction
    const { iv, ciphertext } = await encryptRecordWithMandatoryReservation(
      db,
      dek,
      'patients',
      id,
      [],
      data,
      CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      activeDekVersion
    );

    const entity: EncryptedPatientEntity = {
      id,
      createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      isArchived,
      recordCryptoVersion: CURRENT_RECORD_CRYPTO_VERSION,
      dekVersion: activeDekVersion,
      iv,
      ciphertext,
    };

    // Atomic Dexie write
    await db.patients.put(entity);

    const record: PatientRecord = {
      id,
      createdAt,
      updatedAt: now,
      isArchived,
      data,
    };

    setPatients(prev => {
      // If locked in the meantime, do not retain decrypted state
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) return [];
      const filtered = prev.filter(p => p.id !== id);
      return [record, ...filtered];
    });

    return record;
  };

  // Soft-archive patient (Default safe clinical operation)
  const archivePatient = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await db.archivePatient(id);
    setPatients(prev =>
      prev.map(p => (p.id === id ? { ...p, isArchived: 1, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } : p))
    );
  };

  // Restore archived patient
  const unarchivePatient = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await db.unarchivePatient(id);
    setPatients(prev =>
      prev.map(p => (p.id === id ? { ...p, isArchived: 0, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } : p))
    );
  };

  // Permanent physical deletion (Safeguarded with Safety Gate & Backup Proof)
  const deletePatientPermanently = async (
    id: string,
    backupProof?: { verifiedBackupString: string; bypassForTest?: boolean }
  ): Promise<void> => {
    await db.deletePatientPermanently(id, backupProof);
    setPatients(prev => prev.filter(p => p.id !== id));
  };

  const activePatients = patients.filter(p => !p.isArchived);
  const archivedPatients = patients.filter(p => p.isArchived === 1);

  return (
    <PatientContext.Provider
      value={{
        patients,
        activePatients,
        archivedPatients,
        loading,
        savePatient,
        archivePatient,
        unarchivePatient,
        deletePatientPermanently,
        refreshPatients,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </PatientContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const usePatients = () => {
  const context = useContext(PatientContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('usePatients must be used within a PatientProvider');
  return context;
};

=== FILE: src/context/SessionContext.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Clinical Session & SOAP Context (Phase 2B.2)
// Reactive State Management & Strict Memory Scrubbing on Vault Lock
// =======================================================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { useVault } from './VaultContext';
import { SessionService } from '../services/sessionService';
import { SoapService } from '../services/soapService';
import {
  VaultGenerationChangedError,
  UnboundVaultKeyError,
  type SessionRecord,
  type SessionClinicalData,
  type SoapNoteRecord,
  type SoapNoteData,
  type ClinicalAddendumRecord,
  type AddendumReason,
} from '../types/clinical';

interface SessionContextType {
  sessions: SessionRecord[];
  loadingSessions: boolean;
  activeEpisodeId: string | null;
  loadSessionsForEpisode: (episodeId: string) => Promise<SessionRecord[]>;
  createSession: (
    patientId: string,
    episodeId: string,
    data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>
  ) => Promise<SessionRecord>;
  updateSession: (
    sessionId: string,
    data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>
  ) => Promise<SessionRecord>;
  cancelSession: (
    sessionId: string,
    cancellationReason: string,
    attendanceStatus: 'cancelled_patient' | 'cancelled_clinic' | 'no_show'
  ) => Promise<SessionRecord>;
  finalizeSession: (sessionId: string, therapistName: string) => Promise<SessionRecord>;
  deleteSession: (sessionId: string) => Promise<void>;
  getSoapNote: (sessionId: string) => Promise<SoapNoteRecord | null>;
  saveSoapNote: (
    patientId: string,
    episodeId: string,
    sessionId: string,
    data: SoapNoteData,
    existingSoapId?: string
  ) => Promise<SoapNoteRecord>;
  getAddenda: (sessionId: string) => Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord[]>;
  addAddendum: (
    sessionId: string,
    targetTable: 'sessions' | 'soapNotes',
    targetId: string,
    data: {
      reasonForAmendment: AddendumReason;
      amendmentText: string;
      signedByTherapistName: string;
    }
  ) => Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord>;
  clearSessions: () => void;
}

const SessionContext = createContext<SessionContextType | null>(null);

export const SessionProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { isLocked, dek, lock } = useVault();
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionRecord[]>([]);
  const [activeEpisodeId, setActiveEpisodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingSessions, setLoadingSessions] = useState(false);

  // Synchronization refs for sensitive memory protection
  const isLockedRef = useRef(isLocked);
  isLockedRef.current = isLocked;
  const dekRef = useRef(dek);
  dekRef.current = dek;

  const clearSessions = useCallback(() => {
    setSessions([]);
    setActiveEpisodeId(null);
  }, []);

  // Scrub memory when vault is locked
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLocked) {
      clearSessions();
    }
  }, [isLocked, clearSessions]);

  const loadSessionsForEpisode = useCallback(
    async (episodeId: string): Promise<SessionRecord[]> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) {
        clearSessions();
        return [];
      }

      setLoadingSessions(true);
      setActiveEpisodeId(episodeId);

      try {
        const records = await SessionService.getSessionsByEpisode(episodeId, dekRef.current);
        if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) {
          clearSessions();
          return [];
        }
        setSessions(records);
        return records;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load sessions for episode:', err);
        setSessions([]);
        return [];
      } finally {
        setLoadingSessions(false);
      }
    },
    [clearSessions]
  );

  const createSession = useCallback(
    async (
      patientId: string,
      episodeId: string,
      data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>
    ): Promise<SessionRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      try {
        const created = await SessionService.createSession(patientId, episodeId, data, dekRef.current);
        setSessions((prev) => [...prev, created]);
        return created;
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof VaultGenerationChangedError || err instanceof UnboundVaultKeyError) {
          console.warn('SessionContext: Generation/Unbound error detected. Locking vault.');
          lock();
        }
        throw err;
      }
    },
    [lock]
  );

  const updateSession = useCallback(
    async (
      sessionId: string,
      data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>
    ): Promise<SessionRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      const updated = await SessionService.updateSession(sessionId, data, dekRef.current);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === sessionId ? updated : s)));
      return updated;
    },
    []
  );

  const cancelSession = useCallback(
    async (
      sessionId: string,
      cancellationReason: string,
      attendanceStatus: 'cancelled_patient' | 'cancelled_clinic' | 'no_show'
    ): Promise<SessionRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      const updated = await SessionService.cancelSession(sessionId, cancellationReason, attendanceStatus, dekRef.current);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === sessionId ? updated : s)));
      return updated;
    },
    []
  );

  const finalizeSession = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string, therapistName: string): Promise<SessionRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      const finalized = await SessionService.finalizeSession(sessionId, therapistName, dekRef.current);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === sessionId ? finalized : s)));
      return finalized;
    },
    []
  );

  const deleteSession = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string): Promise<void> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      await SessionService.deleteSession(sessionId, dekRef.current);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== sessionId));
    },
    []
  );

  const getSoapNote = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string): Promise<SoapNoteRecord | null> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');
      return SoapService.getSoapNoteBySession(sessionId, dekRef.current);
    },
    []
  );

  const saveSoapNote = useCallback(
    async (
      patientId: string,
      episodeId: string,
      sessionId: string,
      data: SoapNoteData,
      existingSoapId?: string
    ): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

      if (existingSoapId) {
        return SoapService.updateSoapNote(existingSoapId, data, dekRef.current);
      } else {
        // Check if one already exists
        const existing = await SoapService.getSoapNoteBySession(sessionId, dekRef.current);
        if (existing) {
          return SoapService.updateSoapNote(existing.id, data, dekRef.current);
        }
        return SoapService.createSoapNote(patientId, episodeId, sessionId, data, dekRef.current);
      }
    },
    []
  );

  const getAddenda = useCallback(
    async (sessionId: string): Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord[]> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');
      return SoapService.getAddendaBySession(sessionId, dekRef.current);
    },
    []
  );

  const addAddendum = useCallback(
    async (
      sessionId: string,
      targetTable: 'sessions' | 'soapNotes',
      targetId: string,
      data: {
        reasonForAmendment: AddendumReason;
        amendmentText: string;
        signedByTherapistName: string;
      }
    ): Promise<ClinicalAddendumRecord> => {
      if (isLockedRef.current || !dekRef.current) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');
      return SoapService.addClinicalAddendum(sessionId, targetTable, targetId, data, dekRef.current);
    },
    []
  );

  return (
    <SessionContext.Provider
      value={{
        sessions,
        loadingSessions,
        activeEpisodeId,
        loadSessionsForEpisode,
        createSession,
        updateSession,
        cancelSession,
        finalizeSession,
        deleteSession,
        getSoapNote,
        saveSoapNote,
        getAddenda,
        addAddendum,
        clearSessions,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </SessionContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useSessions = (): SessionContextType => {
  const context = useContext(SessionContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useSessions must be used within a SessionProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

=== FILE: src/context/VaultContext.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Vault & Authentication Context (Envelope Encryption v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { db, requestPersistentStorage } from '../db/db';
import {
  generateAndWrapOperationalDEK,
  unwrapOperationalDEK,
  decryptRawDEKForBackup,
  wrapDEK,
  deriveKEK,
  generateVerifier,
  checkVerifier,
  randomBytes,
  bindDekToGeneration,
  CONSTANTS,
  CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
} from '../crypto/vault';
import { subscribeToVaultEvents, broadcastVaultEvent } from '../utils/vaultEvents';

interface VaultContextType {
  ready: boolean;
  isInitialized: boolean;
  isLocked: boolean;
  dek: CryptoKey | null; // Operational non-extractable DEK
  vaultKek: CryptoKey | null; // Active Vault KEK held only while unlocked
  vaultGeneration: number | null; // Technical epoch/generation bound to in-memory DEK
  persistenceGranted: boolean;
  setupPassword: (password: string) => Promise<void>;
  unlock: (password: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  changeVaultPassword: (oldPass: string, newPass: string) => Promise<void>;
  lock: () => void;
  wipeDatabase: () => Promise<void>;
}

const VaultContext = createContext<VaultContextType | null>(null);

const AUTO_LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30 minutes of inactivity

export const VaultProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [isInitialized, setIsInitialized] = useState(false);
  const [isLocked, setIsLocked] = useState(true);
  const [dek, setDek] = useState<CryptoKey | null>(null);
  const [vaultKek, setVaultKek] = useState<CryptoKey | null>(null);
  const [vaultGeneration, setVaultGeneration] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [persistenceGranted, setPersistenceGranted] = useState(false);

  const lastActivityRef = useRef<number>(Date.now());
  const autoLockTimerRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  // Best-effort sensitive memory cleanup and lock
  const lock = useCallback(() => {
    setDek(null);
    setVaultKek(null);
    setVaultGeneration(null);
    setIsLocked(true);
    if (autoLockTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(autoLockTimerRef.current);
      autoLockTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  // Update activity timestamp
  const updateActivity = useCallback(() => {
    lastActivityRef.current = Date.now();
  }, []);

  // Multi-tier Inactivity Monitor: Event Listener + VisibilityChange + Heartbeat
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLocked) return;

    const handleUserInteraction = () => {
      updateActivity();
    };

    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        const elapsed = Date.now() - lastActivityRef.current;
        if (elapsed >= AUTO_LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS) {
          console.warn('PhysioRecord: Auto-locking vault on resume (exceeded 30 minutes in background)');
          lock();
        }
      } else {
        // App went to background
        updateActivity();
      }
    };

    // Periodic heartbeat check every 60 seconds
    const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
      const elapsed = Date.now() - lastActivityRef.current;
      if (elapsed >= AUTO_LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        console.warn('PhysioRecord: Auto-locking vault (heartbeat inactivity trigger)');
        lock();
      }
    }, 60 * 1000);

    const events = ['mousedown', 'keydown', 'touchstart', 'scroll'];
    events.forEach(evt => window.addEventListener(evt, handleUserInteraction, { passive: true }));
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);

    return () => {
      events.forEach(evt => window.removeEventListener(evt, handleUserInteraction));
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
      clearInterval(heartbeat);
      if (autoLockTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(autoLockTimerRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [isLocked, lock, updateActivity]);

  // Initial boot check
  useEffect(() => {
    const initCheck = async () => {
      try {
        const meta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
        setIsInitialized(!!meta);
        const persistent = await requestPersistentStorage();
        setPersistenceGranted(persistent);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to read vault metadata:', err);
      } finally {
        setReady(true);
      }
    };
    initCheck();
  }, []);

  // Setup Vault with Envelope Encryption (Non-extractable DEK + Vault KEK)
  const setupPassword = useCallback(async (password: string) => {
    if (password.length < 6) {
      throw new Error('يجب ألا تقل كلمة المرور عن 6 خانات.');
    }

    // 1. Derive Vault KEK from password
    const salt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
    const kek = await deriveKEK(password, salt, CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS);

    // 2. Generate random 256-bit DEK, wrap under KEK, and import as NON-EXTRACTABLE
    const gen = 1;
    const { operationalDek, wrappedDek, wrappedDekIv } = await generateAndWrapOperationalDEK(kek, gen);
    bindDekToGeneration(operationalDek, gen);

    // 3. Generate verifier token
    const { verifierIv, verifierCiphertext } = await generateVerifier(kek);

    // 4. Store in vaultMeta
    await db.vaultMeta.put({
      id: 'auth',
      salt,
      wrappedDek,
      wrappedDekIv,
      verifierIv,
      verifierCiphertext,
      schemaVersion: 1,
      kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
      kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
      dekVersion: CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
      encryptionInvocationCount: 0,
      vaultGeneration: gen,
    });

    setDek(operationalDek);
    setVaultKek(kek);
    setVaultGeneration(gen);
    setIsInitialized(true);
    setIsLocked(false);
    updateActivity();
  }, [updateActivity]);

  // Unlock Vault: Verify KEK -> Unwrap DEK as NON-EXTRACTABLE into volatile memory
  const unlock = useCallback(async (password: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    const meta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    if (!meta) throw new Error('الخزينة غير مهيأة بعد');

    const candidateKek = await deriveKEK(
      password,
      meta.salt,
      meta.kdfIterations || CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS
    );

    const isValid = await checkVerifier(candidateKek, meta.verifierIv, meta.verifierCiphertext);
    if (!isValid) return false;

    // Unwrap DEK as non-extractable CryptoKey and bind to active vaultGeneration
    const gen = meta.vaultGeneration ?? 1;
    const recoveredDek = await unwrapOperationalDEK(meta.wrappedDek, meta.wrappedDekIv, candidateKek, gen);
    bindDekToGeneration(recoveredDek, gen);

    setDek(recoveredDek);
    setVaultKek(candidateKek);
    setVaultGeneration(gen);
    setIsLocked(false);
    updateActivity();
    return true;
  }, [updateActivity]);

  // Change Vault Password in O(1) without re-encrypting records
  const changeVaultPassword = useCallback(async (oldPass: string, newPass: string) => {
    const meta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
    if (!meta) throw new Error('الخزينة غير مهيأة');
    if (!dek) throw new Error('الخزينة مقفلة');

    // Verify old password
    const oldKek = await deriveKEK(oldPass, meta.salt, meta.kdfIterations);
    const isValid = await checkVerifier(oldKek, meta.verifierIv, meta.verifierCiphertext);
    if (!isValid) {
      throw new Error('كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة.');
    }

    // Derive new KEK with fresh salt
    const newSalt = randomBytes(CONSTANTS.SALT_BYTES);
    const newKek = await deriveKEK(newPass, newSalt, CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS);

    // Temporarily recover raw DEK bytes via oldKek and re-wrap under newKek
    const rawDekBytes = await decryptRawDEKForBackup(meta.wrappedDek, meta.wrappedDekIv, oldKek);
    const { wrappedDek, wrappedDekIv } = await wrapDEK(rawDekBytes, newKek);
    rawDekBytes.fill(0); // Immediately zero transient raw buffer

    const { verifierIv, verifierCiphertext } = await generateVerifier(newKek);

    const currentGen = meta.vaultGeneration ?? 1;

    // Update vaultMeta
    await db.vaultMeta.put({
      id: 'auth',
      salt: newSalt,
      wrappedDek,
      wrappedDekIv,
      verifierIv,
      verifierCiphertext,
      schemaVersion: meta.schemaVersion,
      kdfAlgorithm: 'PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256',
      kdfIterations: CONSTANTS.DEFAULT_PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
      dekVersion: meta.dekVersion || CURRENT_DEK_VERSION,
      encryptionInvocationCount: meta.encryptionInvocationCount || 0,
      vaultGeneration: currentGen,
    });

    bindDekToGeneration(dek, currentGen);
    setVaultKek(newKek);
    setVaultGeneration(currentGen);
    updateActivity();

    // Broadcast password change to other open tabs
    broadcastVaultEvent({
      type: 'VAULT_PASSWORD_CHANGED',
      generation: currentGen,
    });
  }, [dek, updateActivity]);

  // Cross-Tab Vault Synchronization:
  // When another tab performs Full Restore or rotates credentials, immediately lock and wipe memory
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToVaultEvents((event) => {
      if (event.type === 'VAULT_GENERATION_CHANGED' || event.type === 'VAULT_PASSWORD_CHANGED') {
        console.warn(`PhysioRecord: Cross-tab ${event.type} event received. Locking vault.`);
        lock();
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [lock]);

  // Wipe database completely
  const wipeDatabase = useCallback(async () => {
    lock();
    await db.delete();
    await db.open();
    setIsInitialized(false);
    setIsLocked(true);
  }, [lock]);

  return (
    <VaultContext.Provider
      value={{
        ready,
        isInitialized,
        isLocked,
        dek,
        vaultKek,
        vaultGeneration,
        persistenceGranted,
        setupPassword,
        unlock,
        changeVaultPassword,
        lock,
        wipeDatabase,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </VaultContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useVault = () => {
  const context = useContext(VaultContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('useVault must be used within a VaultProvider');
  return context;
};

=== FILE: src/data/clinicalToolkit/bodyChartRegions.ts ===

// Pure coordinate clamping, region resolution, and data validation

/**
 * Clamps coordinates to [0.00, 100.00] and maps a canvas percentage point (xPercent, yPercent)
 * to standard anatomical region codes and bilingual labels.
 * In Anterior view: patient's Right is canvas Left (x < 50%), patient's Left is canvas Right (x > 50%).
 * In Posterior view: patient's Left is canvas Left (x < 50%), patient's Right is canvas Right (x > 50%).
 */
export function resolveAnatomicalRegionFromPoint(
  view: BodyChartView,
  xPercentRaw: number,
  yPercentRaw: number,
  explicitRegionCode?: string
): {
  regionCode: string;
  labelAr: string;
  labelEn: string;
  xPercent: number;
  yPercent: number;
} {
  // 1. Strict Clamping & Precision to 2 decimal places
  const x = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(Number(xPercentRaw).toFixed(2))));
  const y = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(Number(yPercentRaw).toFixed(2))));

  // 2. If explicit code provided and recognized, use it
  if (explicitRegionCode && ANATOMICAL_BODY_REGIONS[explicitRegionCode]) {
    const reg = ANATOMICAL_BODY_REGIONS[explicitRegionCode];
    return {
      regionCode: reg.regionCode,
      labelAr: reg.labelAr,
      labelEn: reg.labelEn,
      xPercent: x,
      yPercent: y,
    };
  }

  // 3. Coordinate-based anatomical zone partitioning
  let code = 'head_neck';

  if (y <= 13) {
    code = 'head_neck';
  } else if (y <= 24) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      if (x < 35) code = 'shoulder_right';
      else if (x > 65) code = 'shoulder_left';
      else code = 'chest_thorax';
    } else {
      if (x < 35) code = 'shoulder_left';
      else if (x > 65) code = 'shoulder_right';
      else code = 'upper_back_thoracic';
    }
  } else if (y <= 40) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      if (x < 28) code = 'arm_right_upper';
      else if (x > 72) code = 'arm_left_upper';
      else code = 'abdomen';
    } else {
      if (x < 28) code = 'arm_left_upper';
      else if (x > 72) code = 'arm_right_upper';
      else code = 'low_back_lumbar';
    }
  } else if (y <= 49) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      if (x < 22) code = 'elbow_forearm_right';
      else if (x > 78) code = 'elbow_forearm_left';
      else code = 'pelvis_groin';
    } else {
      if (x < 22) code = 'elbow_forearm_left';
      else if (x > 78) code = 'elbow_forearm_right';
      else code = 'gluteal_sacral';
    }
  } else if (y <= 66) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      if (x < 18) code = 'wrist_hand_right';
      else if (x > 82) code = 'wrist_hand_left';
      else if (x <= 50) code = 'thigh_right';
      else code = 'thigh_left';
    } else {
      if (x < 18) code = 'wrist_hand_left';
      else if (x > 82) code = 'wrist_hand_right';
      else if (x <= 50) code = 'thigh_left';
      else code = 'thigh_right';
    }
  } else if (y <= 77) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'knee_right' : 'knee_left';
    } else {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'knee_left' : 'knee_right';
    }
  } else if (y <= 89) {
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'leg_shin_right' : 'leg_shin_left';
    } else {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'leg_calf_left' : 'leg_calf_right';
    }
  } else {
    // y > 89: Ankle and foot
    if (view === 'anterior') {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'ankle_foot_right' : 'ankle_foot_left';
    } else {
      code = x <= 50 ? 'ankle_foot_left' : 'ankle_foot_right';
    }
  }

  const resolved = ANATOMICAL_BODY_REGIONS[code] || ANATOMICAL_BODY_REGIONS.head_neck;
  return {
    regionCode: resolved.regionCode,
    labelAr: resolved.labelAr,
    labelEn: resolved.labelEn,
    xPercent: x,
    yPercent: y,
  };
}

/**
 * Pure validator function for PatientBodyChartData structure, ranges, and types.
 */
export function validatePatientBodyChartData(chart?: PatientBodyChartData): boolean {
  if (!chart) return true; // Optional field

  if (chart.chartVersion !== '1.0.0') return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(chart.markers)) return false;

  const validSymptomKeys = Object.keys(BODY_SYMPTOM_TYPES) as BodySymptomType[];

  for (const m of chart.markers) {
    if (!m || typeof m !== 'object') return false;
    if (typeof m.markerId !== 'string' || !m.markerId.trim()) return false;
    if (m.view !== 'anterior' && m.view !== 'posterior') return false;
    if (typeof m.xPercent !== 'number' || isNaN(m.xPercent) || m.xPercent < 0 || m.xPercent > 100) return false;
    if (typeof m.yPercent !== 'number' || isNaN(m.yPercent) || m.yPercent < 0 || m.yPercent > 100) return false;
    if (!validSymptomKeys.includes(m.symptomType)) return false;
    if (typeof m.anatomicalRegionCode !== 'string' || !m.anatomicalRegionCode.trim()) return false;
    if (m.severityNrs !== undefined) {
      if (typeof m.severityNrs !== 'number' || isNaN(m.severityNrs) || m.severityNrs < 0 || m.severityNrs > 10) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    if (m.description !== undefined && typeof m.description !== 'string') return false;
    if (typeof m.createdAt !== 'string' || !m.createdAt.trim()) return false;
  }

  if (chart.clinicianSummaryNotes !== undefined && typeof chart.clinicianSummaryNotes !== 'string') {
    return false;
  }
  if (typeof chart.lastUpdatedAt !== 'string' || !chart.lastUpdatedAt.trim()) {
    return false;
  }

  return true;
}

// =======================================================================

  'Anatomical studies (Foerster 1933; Keegan & Garrett 1948; Lee et al. 2008) confirm that adjacent dermatomes overlap substantially (>50%).     primarySourceCitation: 'ASIA / ISNCSCI Standards (2025).'

export function getDermatomeByRoot(root: SpinalLevel): DermatomeDefinition | undefined {
  return DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS.find(d => d.rootLevel === root);
}

export function searchDermatomes(query: string): DermatomeDefinition[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS;

  return DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS.filter(d => {
    return (
      d.rootLevel.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      d.canonicalRegionEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      d.canonicalRegionAr.includes(q) ||
      d.keySensoryPointEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      d.keySensoryPointAr.includes(q) ||
      (d.asiaCode && d.asiaCode.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  });
}

=== FILE: src/data/clinicalToolkit/dtrData.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Deep Tendon Reflexes (DTR) Reference Dataset (Phase 2C)
// Sources: NINDS Guidelines, Bickley's Bates' Guide to Physical Examination,
// DeJong's Neurologic Examination (8th Ed), Campbell WW (2013)
// =======================================================================

import type { DtrDefinition, DtrGradeDefinition } from '../../types/clinicalToolkit';

export const DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_EN =
  'Bilateral side-to-side asymmetry (e.g., 1+ on the involved side vs. 2+ on the uninvolved side) is far more diagnostically significant than symmetric hyporeflexia or briskness, which can represent normal physiological variation. True asymmetry strongly points to focal radiculopathy or peripheral nerve injury.';

export const DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_AR =
  'يُعد عدم التناظر بين الجانبين الأيمن والأيسر (Asymmetry) العلامة السريرية الأهم في فحص المنعكسات؛ إذ إن وجود فارق بين الطرفين (مثل 1+ في الطرف المصاب مقابل 2+ في الطرف السليم) يرجح بقوة وجود اعتلال جذري أو عصبي موضعي، بينما الخمول المتناظر أو النشاط المتناظر في كلا الطرفين قد يكون تبايناً فسيولوجياً طبيعياً لدى الفرد.';

export const JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_EN =
  'Jendrassik Maneuver: A reinforcement technique where the patient interlocks flexed fingers and pulls apart forcefully during lower extremity reflex testing (or clenches teeth/fists during upper extremity testing). It facilitates spinal alpha motor neuron pool excitability by reducing presynaptic inhibition. A reflex must NEVER be documented as absent (Grade 0) until reinforcement has been systematically attempted.';

export const JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_AR =
  'مناورة جيندراسيك (Jendrassik Maneuver): مناورة تعزيز إرادية يقوم فيها المريض بتشبيك أصابع يديه وشدهما بقوة للخارج أثناء فحص منعكسات الطرف السفلي (أو إطباق الأسنان بقوة عند فحص الطرف العلوي). تزيد هذه المناورة من استثارة العصبونات الحركية الشوكية عبر تقليل التثبيط قبل المشبكي. لا يجوز طبياً توثيق المنعكس بأنه غائب (الدرجة 0) إلا بعد تطبيق مناورة التعزيز وفشلها.';

export function getDtrById(id: string): DtrDefinition | undefined {
  return DTR_DEFINITIONS.find(d => d.reflexId === id);
}

// PhysioRecord — Myotomes Clinical Reference Dataset (Phase 2C)
// Sources: ASIA / ISNCSCI Standards, Kendall's Muscles (5th Ed),
// Magee Orthopedic Physical Assessment (7th Ed), DeJong's Neurologic Exam
// =======================================================================

import type { MyotomeDefinition, SpinalLevel } from '../../types/clinicalToolkit';

export const MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_EN =
  'Almost all skeletal muscles receive multisegmental innervation from two or more spinal roots through nerve plexuses. Consequently, a single-root radiculopathy typically causes paresis (partial weakness) rather than complete paralysis of a muscle group. Testing multiple muscles innervated by the same root (via different peripheral nerves) is the cornerstone for differentiating radiculopathy from peripheral nerve entrapment.';

export const MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_AR =
  'تتلقى معظم العضلات الهيكلية تعصيباً مشتركاً متعدد المستويات من جذرين شوكيين أو أكثر عبر الضفائر العصبية. لذلك، فإن اعتلال الجذر المنفرد (Radiculopathy) يؤدي عادةً إلى ضعف جزئي (Paresis) في العضلة وليس شللاً كاملاً. يُعد فحص عدة عضلات يشترك في تغذيتها نفس الجذر العصبي عبر أعصاب محيطية مختلفة هو الأساس السريري للتفريق بين اعتلال الجذر وانضغاط العصب المحيطي.';

export function getMyotomeByRoot(root: SpinalLevel): MyotomeDefinition | undefined {
  return MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS.find(m => m.rootLevel === root);
}

export function searchMyotomes(query: string): MyotomeDefinition[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS;
  const words = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);

  return MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS.filter(m => {
    const textCorpus = [
      m.rootLevel,
      m.predominantMovementEn,
      m.predominantMovementAr,
      m.testingPositionEn,
      m.testingPositionAr,
      m.differentialClinicalNotesEn,
      m.differentialClinicalNotesAr,
      ...m.keyMuscles.flatMap(k => [k.nameEn, k.nameAr, k.peripheralNerveEn, k.peripheralNerveAr])
    ].join(' ').toLowerCase();

    return words.every(w => textCorpus.includes(w));
  });
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Pure Validation & Calculation Helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Validates the structure and scientific invariants of a RomAssessmentBatteryData object.
 * CLINICAL INVARIANT: If isTested === false, measuredAngleDegrees must be null AND reasonNotTested must be provided.
 * If isTested === true, measuredAngleDegrees must be a valid number between -45 and 360.
 */
export function validateRomBatteryData(battery?: RomAssessmentBatteryData): boolean {
  if (!battery) return true;
  if (battery.batteryVersion !== '1.0.0') return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(battery.entries)) return false;

  const validMovementTypes = Object.keys(ROM_MOVEMENT_TYPES);
  const validLateralities = Object.keys(BODY_LATERALITIES);
  const validReasons = Object.keys(CLINICAL_NON_TESTABLE_REASONS);

  for (const entry of battery.entries) {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') return false;
    if (typeof entry.entryId !== 'string' || !entry.entryId.trim()) return false;
    if (typeof entry.jointId !== 'string' || !entry.jointId.trim()) return false;
    if (typeof entry.movementId !== 'string' || !entry.movementId.trim()) return false;
    if (!validMovementTypes.includes(entry.movementType)) return false;
    if (!validLateralities.includes(entry.laterality)) return false;

    // Strict Separation: isTested: false vs measured value
    if (entry.isTested === false) {
      if (entry.measuredAngleDegrees !== null) return false;
      if (!entry.reasonNotTested || !validReasons.includes(entry.reasonNotTested)) return false;
    } else if (entry.isTested === true) {
      if (typeof entry.measuredAngleDegrees !== 'number' || isNaN(entry.measuredAngleDegrees)) return false;
      if (entry.measuredAngleDegrees < -45 || entry.measuredAngleDegrees > 360) return false;
    } else {
      return false;
    }

    if (entry.painNrsScore !== undefined) {
      if (typeof entry.painNrsScore !== 'number' || isNaN(entry.painNrsScore) || entry.painNrsScore < 0 || entry.painNrsScore > 10) {
        return false;
      }
    }
  }

  return true;
}

/**
 * Validates the structure and scientific invariants of a MmtAssessmentBatteryData object.
 * CLINICAL INVARIANT: If isTested === false, gradeValue must be null AND reasonNotTested must be provided.
 * If isTested === true, gradeValue must be a valid recognized MRC grade.
 */
export function validateMmtBatteryData(battery?: MmtAssessmentBatteryData): boolean {
  if (!battery) return true;
  if (battery.batteryVersion !== '1.0.0') return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(battery.entries)) return false;

  const validMrcGrades = Object.keys(MRC_GRADES_CATALOG);
  const validLateralities = Object.keys(BODY_LATERALITIES);
  const validReasons = Object.keys(CLINICAL_NON_TESTABLE_REASONS);

  for (const entry of battery.entries) {
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object') return false;
    if (typeof entry.entryId !== 'string' || !entry.entryId.trim()) return false;
    if (typeof entry.muscleGroupId !== 'string' || !entry.muscleGroupId.trim()) return false;
    if (!validLateralities.includes(entry.laterality)) return false;
    if (entry.gravityCondition !== 'against_gravity' && entry.gravityCondition !== 'gravity_eliminated') return false;

    // Strict Separation: isTested: false vs measured grade
    if (entry.isTested === false) {
      if (entry.gradeValue !== null) return false;
      if (!entry.reasonNotTested || !validReasons.includes(entry.reasonNotTested)) return false;
    } else if (entry.isTested === true) {
      if (!entry.gradeValue || !validMrcGrades.includes(entry.gradeValue)) return false;
    } else {
      return false;
    }

    if (entry.painNrsScore !== undefined) {
      if (typeof entry.painNrsScore !== 'number' || isNaN(entry.painNrsScore) || entry.painNrsScore < 0 || entry.painNrsScore > 10) {
        return false;
      }
    }
  }

  return true;
}

/**
 * Calculates Bilateral Symmetry Index (BSI %) and Deficit Degrees compared to uninvolved reference side.
 */
export function calculateBilateralSymmetry(
  involvedAngle: number | null,
  uninvolvedAngle?: number | null
): { deficitDegrees: number | null; symmetryIndexPercent: number | null } {
  if (involvedAngle === null || uninvolvedAngle === null || uninvolvedAngle === undefined || uninvolvedAngle <= 0) {
    return { deficitDegrees: null, symmetryIndexPercent: null };
  }

  const deficit = uninvolvedAngle - involvedAngle;
  const rawRatio = (involvedAngle / uninvolvedAngle) * 100;
  const symmetryIndex = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(rawRatio.toFixed(1))));

  return {
    deficitDegrees: Number(deficit.toFixed(1)),
    symmetryIndexPercent: symmetryIndex,
  };
}

export interface LongitudinalRomComparisonResult {
  isComparable: boolean;
  deltaDegrees: number | null;
  reasonAr?: string;
  reasonEn?: string;
}

/**
 * Longitudinal Comparability Predicate:
 * CLINICAL SAFETY INVARIANT: Prohibits mixing AROM with PROM, ensures same joint, movement, and laterality,
 * and handles unexamined/non-testable states cleanly without false zero math.
 */
export function compareLongitudinalRomEntries(
  baseline: RomMeasurementEntry,
  current: RomMeasurementEntry
): LongitudinalRomComparisonResult {
  // 1. Joint & Movement Mismatch Check
  if (
    baseline.jointId !== current.jointId ||
    baseline.movementId !== current.movementId ||
    baseline.laterality !== current.laterality
  ) {
    return {
      isComparable: false,
      deltaDegrees: null,
      reasonAr: 'المفصل أو الحركة أو الجهة المفحوصة غير متطابقة.',
      reasonEn: 'Joint, movement or laterality mismatch.',
    };
  }

  // 2. Strict Movement Type Matching (AROM === AROM only; PROM === PROM only)
  if (baseline.movementType !== current.movementType) {
    return {
      isComparable: false,
      deltaDegrees: null,
      reasonAr: 'لا تجوز المقارنة المباشرة بين المدى الفاعل (AROM) والمدى المنفعل (PROM).',
      reasonEn: 'Cannot directly compare Active (AROM) and Passive (PROM) movements.',
    };
  }

  // 3. Not Tested Gap Check
  if (!baseline.isTested || !current.isTested || baseline.measuredAngleDegrees === null || current.measuredAngleDegrees === null) {
    return {
      isComparable: false,
      deltaDegrees: null,
      reasonAr: 'أحد القياسين غير مفحوص سريرياً (Not Tested).',
      reasonEn: 'One of the measurements was not clinically tested.',
    };
  }

  // 4. Valid Comparable Delta Calculation
  const delta = Number((current.measuredAngleDegrees - baseline.measuredAngleDegrees).toFixed(1));
  return {
    isComparable: true,
    deltaDegrees: delta,
  };
}

=== FILE: src/utils/testHooks.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Production Test Hooks Security Isolation
// Strictly guarantees that no sensitive crypto keys, database instances,
// or test bypass hooks are EVER exposed on window in production builds.
// =======================================================================

/**
 * Determine whether test hooks should be exposed.
 * In a standard production build (import.meta.env.MODE === 'production'),
 * this returns false unconditionally.
 */
export const isTestEnvironment = (): boolean => {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;

  // In production builds, test hooks are strictly disabled!
  if (import.meta.env.MODE === 'production') {
    return false;
  }

  // Only permit when explicitly running in test mode or development
  return Boolean(
    import.meta.env.DEV ||
    import.meta.env.MODE === 'test' ||
    import.meta.env.VITE_ENABLE_TEST_HOOKS === 'true'
  );
};

/**
 * Safely register test hooks onto window only in test environments.
 * No-op in production.
 */
export const registerTestHooks = (hooks: Record<string, any>): void => {
  if (import.meta.env.MODE === 'production' || !isTestEnvironment()) return;

  const key = '__physioRecordTestHooks';
  (window as any)[key] = {
    ...((window as any)[key] || {}),
    ...hooks,
  };
};

=== FILE: src/utils/vaultEvents.ts ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Cross-Tab Vault Synchronization (BroadcastChannel)
// Provides defense-in-depth notification across browser tabs when vault
// generation or authentication credentials change (e.g. after Full Restore).
// =======================================================================

export interface VaultSyncEvent {
  type: 'VAULT_GENERATION_CHANGED' | 'VAULT_PASSWORD_CHANGED' | 'VAULT_LOCKED';
  generation?: number;
  timestamp: number;
  senderId: string;
}

const TAB_ID = typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : Math.random().toString();
const CHANNEL_NAME = 'physiorecord_vault_sync';

let channel: BroadcastChannel | null = null;

function getChannel(): BroadcastChannel | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof BroadcastChannel === 'undefined') {
    return null;
  }
  if (!channel) {
    try {
      channel = new BroadcastChannel(CHANNEL_NAME);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('PhysioRecord: Failed to initialize BroadcastChannel for vault sync:', e);
    }
  }
  return channel;
}

/**
 * Broadcast an event to all other tabs sharing the same origin.
 */
export function broadcastVaultEvent(event: Omit<VaultSyncEvent, 'senderId' | 'timestamp'>): void {
  const ch = getChannel();
  if (ch) {
    try {
      ch.postMessage({
        ...event,
        senderId: TAB_ID,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('PhysioRecord: Failed to broadcast vault event:', e);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Subscribe to cross-tab vault events. Returns an unsubscribe function.
 */
export function subscribeToVaultEvents(onEvent: (event: VaultSyncEvent) => void): () => void {
  const ch = getChannel();
  if (!ch) return () => {};

  const listener = (msg: MessageEvent<VaultSyncEvent>) => {
    if (msg.data && msg.data.senderId !== TAB_ID) {
      onEvent(msg.data);
    }
  };

  ch.addEventListener('message', listener);
  return () => {
    ch.removeEventListener('message', listener);
  };
}

// PhysioRecord — Clinical Addendum Modal (Phase 2B.2)
// Append-only Amendment of Finalized Clinical Records

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export const AddendumModal: React.FC<AddendumModalProps> = ({

  useEffect(() => {
    setReasonForAmendment('late_entry');
    setAmendmentText('');
    setSignedByTherapistName('د. المعالج الطبيعي');
    setError(null);
  }, [isOpen, session]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!amendmentText.trim()) {
      setError('نص الملحق أو التصحيح السريري إلزامي.');
      return;
    }
    if (!signedByTherapistName.trim()) {
      setError('اسم وتوقيع المعالج إلزامي لاعتماد الملحق.');
      return;

        signedByTherapistName: signedByTherapistName.trim(),

      console.error('Failed to create addendum:', err);

      setSubmitting(false);
    }

              <FileEdit className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>

                إضافة ملحق وتصحيح سريري (Clinical Addendum)
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-200/80">
                للجلسة المعتمدة #{session.sessionNumber} بتاريخ {session.sessionDate}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-slate-300 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Legal & Architectural Context Notice */}
          <div className="p-3.5 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl text-xs text-amber-900 leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <strong>مبدأ سلامة السجلات السريرية:</strong> الملاحق السريرية ترتبط بالسجل المعتمد بشكل تتابعي آمن ومؤرخ دون استبدال أو مسح أي بيانات سابقة، لضمان الحفاظ على التسلسل الزمني والمسؤولية المهنية.
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            
            {/* Target Table Selector */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">

              </label>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTargetTable('soapNotes')}
                  className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                    targetTable === 'soapNotes'
                      ? 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-50 text-primary-900 shadow-2xs'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600'

                  توثيق SOAP السريري

                <button
                  type="button"

                  className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                    targetTable === 'sessions'
                      ? 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-50 text-primary-900 shadow-2xs'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600'

                  بيانات الجلسة الإدارية

                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"

                onChange={(e) => setSignedByTherapistName(e.target.value)}
                required
                placeholder="اسم المعالج الموثق..."
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Backup & Restoration Modal (v1.1)
// With Full Data Loss Prevention & Restore Confirmation Inspection
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db/db';
import { useVault } from '../context/VaultContext';
import { usePatients } from '../context/PatientContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import {
  exportBackupPackage,
  restoreBackupPackage,
  inspectBackupPreview,
  type BackupPreviewSummary,
} from '../crypto/backup';
import {
  createDurablePreRestoreRecoveryBackup,
  saveExternalRecoveryBackupFile,
} from '../crypto/recovery';
import {
  X,
  Download,
  Upload,
  ShieldCheck,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle,
  FileText,
  RefreshCw,
  ArrowRight,
  Database,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface BackupRestoreModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const BackupRestoreModal: React.FC<BackupRestoreModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { vaultKek, unlock } = useVault();
  const { refreshPatients } = usePatients();
  const { bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'export' | 'restore'>('export');

  // Export State
  const [exportPassword, setExportPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmExportPassword, setConfirmExportPassword] = useState('');
  const [exportLoading, setExportLoading] = useState(false);
  const [exportSuccess, setExportSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [exportError, setExportError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Restore State
  const [restoreFileContent, setRestoreFileContent] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [restoreFileName, setRestoreFileName] = useState<string>('');
  const [restorePassword, setRestorePassword] = useState('');
  const [restoreLoading, setRestoreLoading] = useState(false);
  const [previewSummary, setPreviewSummary] = useState<BackupPreviewSummary | null>(null);
  const [newVaultPassword, setNewVaultPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmNewVaultPassword, setConfirmNewVaultPassword] = useState('');
  const [emergencyRecoveryPassword, setEmergencyRecoveryPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword, setConfirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmedDataLoss, setConfirmedDataLoss] = useState(false);
  const [acknowledgedExternalRecovery, setAcknowledgedExternalRecovery] = useState(false);
  const [restoreSuccess, setRestoreSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [restoreError, setRestoreError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [emergencyRecoverySnapshotId, setEmergencyRecoverySnapshotId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [emergencyRecoveryPackage, setEmergencyRecoveryPackage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [externalSaveMethod, setExternalSaveMethod] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Handle Export
  const handleExport = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setExportError(null);

    if (exportPassword.length < 6) {
      setExportError('يجب ألا تقل كلمة مرور النسخة الاحتياطية عن 6 خانات.');
      return;
    }
    if (exportPassword !== confirmExportPassword) {
      setExportError('كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين.');
      return;
    }
    if (!vaultKek) {
      setExportError('الخزينة مقفلة أو غير مهيأة.');
      return;
    }

    setExportLoading(true);
    try {
      const meta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
      if (!meta) throw new Error('بيانات الخزينة المحلية غير موجودة.');

      const backupPackageStr = await exportBackupPackage(
        db,
        meta,
        vaultKek,
        exportPassword
      );

      // Trigger browser file download
      const blob = new Blob([backupPackageStr], { type: 'application/json' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `physiorecord_backup_${dateStr}.ptbackup`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      setExportSuccess(true);
      setExportPassword('');
      setConfirmExportPassword('');
    } catch (err: any) {
      setExportError(err?.message || 'فشل تصدير النسخة الاحتياطية.');
    } finally {
      setExportLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Handle File Selection
  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setRestoreFileName(file.name);
    setRestoreError(null);
    setPreviewSummary(null);
    setConfirmedDataLoss(false);

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      setRestoreFileContent(event.target?.result as string);
    };
    reader.onerror = () => {
      setRestoreError('فشل قراءة الملف المختار.');
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  // Step 1 of Restore: Inspect and Preview Backup (Data Loss Prevention)
  const handleInspectPreview = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setRestoreError(null);

    if (!restoreFileContent) {
      setRestoreError('يرجى اختيار ملف النسخة الاحتياطية (.ptbackup) أولاً.');
      return;
    }
    if (!restorePassword) {
      setRestoreError('يرجى إدخال كلمة مرور النسخة الاحتياطية.');
      return;
    }

    setRestoreLoading(true);
    try {
      const summary = await inspectBackupPreview(restoreFileContent, restorePassword, db);
      setPreviewSummary(summary);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setRestoreError(err?.message || 'فشل التحقق من النسخة الاحتياطية. تأكد من كلمة المرور وسلامة الملف.');
    } finally {
      setRestoreLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Step 2 of Restore: Execute Replacement with Safety Recovery Backup
  const handleExecuteRestore = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setRestoreError(null);

    if (!confirmedDataLoss) {
      setRestoreError('يجب الموافقة الصريحة على إقرار استبدال البيانات للمتابعة.');
      return;
    }
    if (newVaultPassword.length < 6) {
      setRestoreError('يجب ألا تقل كلمة مرور الخزينة الجديدة عن 6 خانات.');
      return;
    }
    if (newVaultPassword !== confirmNewVaultPassword) {
      setRestoreError('كلمتا مرور الخزينة الجديدة غير متطابقتين.');
      return;
    }
    if (emergencyRecoveryPassword.length < 6) {
      setRestoreError('يجب ألا تقل كلمة مرور حماية نسخة التعافي الطارئة عن 6 خانات.');
      return;
    }
    if (emergencyRecoveryPassword !== confirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword) {
      setRestoreError('كلمتا مرور حماية نسخة التعافي الطارئة غير متطابقتين.');
      return;
    }
    if (!acknowledgedExternalRecovery) {
      setRestoreError('يجب تأكيد إقرار حفظ نسخة التعافي الطارئة الخارجية والاحتفاظ بكلمة مرورها.');
      return;
    }
    if (!restoreFileContent) return;

    setRestoreLoading(true);
    try {
      // 1. Create durable pre-restore emergency recovery backup of existing destination database
      let emergencyId: string | null = null;
      let emergencyPkg: string | null = null;
      let saveMethodDesc: string | null = null;

      const currentMeta = await db.vaultMeta.get('auth');
      if (currentMeta && vaultKek) {
        // Enforce mandatory emergency backup creation and full verification before restore!
        try {
          // A. Internal Logical Recovery Database (PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB)
          const recoveryResult = await createDurablePreRestoreRecoveryBackup(
            db,
            currentMeta,
            vaultKek,
            emergencyRecoveryPassword,
            'نسخة تعافٍ طارئة قبل استبدال قاعدة البيانات'
          );
          emergencyId = recoveryResult.snapshotId;
          emergencyPkg = recoveryResult.backupPackageStr;

          // B. External Encrypted Emergency Backup (Physical file outside browser storage)
          try {
            const extResult = await saveExternalRecoveryBackupFile(recoveryResult.backupPackageStr);
            saveMethodDesc = extResult.confirmedSaved
              ? 'تم الحفظ مباشرة على القرص عبر File System Access API'
              : 'تم تصدير وتنزيل الملف عبر المتصفح (Download Fallback)';
          } catch (fileErr: any) {
            if (fileErr?.message?.includes('إلغاء')) {
              setRestoreLoading(false);
              setRestoreError('تم إلغاء عملية الاستعادة: ألغى المستخدم حفظ ملف نسخة التعافي الخارجي.');
              return;
            }
            console.warn('External file save fallback:', fileErr);
          }
        } catch (backupErr: any) {
          // STRICT RULE: Halt restore immediately if emergency recovery backup fails!
          setRestoreLoading(false);
          setRestoreError(`فشلت حماية البيانات السابقة: تعذر إنشاء نسخة التعافي الطارئة (${backupErr?.message || backupErr}). تم إيقاف عملية الاستعادة فوراً لحماية بياناتك من الضياع.`);
          return;
        }
      }

      // 2. Execute atomic restoration (with staging counter and new DEK)
      await restoreBackupPackage(
        restoreFileContent,
        restorePassword,
        newVaultPassword,
        db
      );

      // 3. Unlock with new password and refresh state
      await unlock(newVaultPassword);
      await refreshPatients();

      setEmergencyRecoverySnapshotId(emergencyId);
      setEmergencyRecoveryPackage(emergencyPkg);
      setExternalSaveMethod(saveMethodDesc);
      setRestoreSuccess(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setRestoreError(err?.message || 'فشل استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية.');
    } finally {
      setRestoreLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex justify-center items-center p-4" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-100 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-primary-100 text-primary-700 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Database className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                {bi('إدارة النسخ الاحتياطي والاستعادة المشفرة', 'Encrypted Backup & Restore (.ptbackup)')}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                تصدير واستعادة السجلات السريرية مشفرة محلياً بـ AES-256-GCM و HKDF
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs Bar */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-100/70 px-6 pt-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('export'); setExportSuccess(false); setExportError(null); }}
            className={`pb-3 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'export'
                ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>تصدير نسخة احتياطية (.ptbackup)</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('restore'); setRestoreSuccess(false); setRestoreError(null); }}
            className={`pb-3 px-4 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'restore'
                ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>استعادة نسخة احتياطية</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-5 flex-1">
          
          {/* TAB 1: EXPORT */}
          {activeTab === 'export' && (
            <div>
              <div className="p-4 bg-primary-50/70 border border-primary-200 rounded-2xl text-primary-950 text-xs leading-relaxed space-y-1 mb-5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-primary-800">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
                  <span>تشفير أظرف محصن ومستقل عن الجهاز (Envelope Encryption)</span>
                </div>
                <p>
                  يتم تشفير كافة السجلات السريرية بمفتاح بيانات عشوائي (DEK) محمي بكلمة مرور النسخة عبر خوارزمية HKDF-SHA256 مع ملح عشوائي مستقل (32 بايت). ملف النسخة محمي تماماً ويمكن حفظه بأمان على فلاش ميموري أو أي وسيط تخزين.
                </p>
              </div>

              {exportError && (
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 text-rose-600" />
                  <span>{exportError}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {exportSuccess && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-2xl text-emerald-800 text-xs space-y-2 mb-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-emerald-900">
                    <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                    <span>تم تصدير وتنزيل ملف النسخة الاحتياطية بنجاح!</span>
                  </div>
                  <p>
                    احتفظ بهذا الملف في مكان آمن. ستحتاج إلى كلمة المرور التي اخترتها الآن عند استعادة السجلات.
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <form onSubmit={handleExport} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    كلمة مرور حماية النسخة الاحتياطية
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={exportPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setExportPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="اختر كلمة مرور قوية (6 خانات على الأقل)"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                    dir="ltr"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    تأكيد كلمة المرور
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={confirmExportPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmExportPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="أعد إدخال كلمة المرور"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                    dir="ltr"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="pt-2">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={exportLoading || !exportPassword || !confirmExportPassword}
                    className="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 active:bg-primary-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    {exportLoading ? (
                      <>
                        <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                        <span>جاري التشفير وتوليد الحزمة...</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
                        <span>تصدير وتنزيل ملف النسخة (.ptbackup)</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: RESTORE */}
          {activeTab === 'restore' && (
            <div>
              {restoreError && (
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 text-rose-600" />
                  <span>{restoreError}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {restoreSuccess && (
                <div className="p-5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-2xl text-emerald-900 text-xs space-y-3 mb-4 text-center">
                  <CheckCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-600 mx-auto" />
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-sm">تم استعادة وتحديث قاعدة البيانات بنجاح تام!</h3>
                  <p className="text-emerald-700 leading-relaxed">
                    تم توليد مفتاح بيانات تشغيلي جديد تماماً (Non-Extractable DEK)، وإعادة تشفير كافة السجلات برقم إصدار مفتاح جديد (dekVersion)، وتثبيت استهلاك العداد المرحلي الفعلي (Staging Counter)، وتفعيل الخزينة بنجاح.
                  </p>
                  
                  {emergencyRecoverySnapshotId && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-white/80 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-slate-700 text-[11px] text-right space-y-1.5">
                      <p className="font-bold text-emerald-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                        <span>تم توثيق نسخة التعافي الطارئة لبياناتك السابقة (Dual Recovery):</span>
                      </p>
                      <p className="font-mono text-slate-600">معرف اللقطة الداخلية: {emergencyRecoverySnapshotId}</p>
                      <p className="text-slate-600">
                        <strong>التخزين الداخلي:</strong> تم الحفظ منطقياً في قاعدة <code>PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB</code>.
                      </p>
                      {externalSaveMethod && (
                        <p className="text-emerald-800 font-semibold">
                          <strong>التصدير الخارجي:</strong> {externalSaveMethod}.
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 p-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200">
                        تنبيه أمني: كلمة المرور لم تُحفظ في المتصفح نهائياً. يرجى استخدام كلمة مرور التعافي التي عينتها لفك تشفير هذه النسخة في حال الرجوع إليها.
                      </p>
                      {emergencyRecoveryPackage && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            const blob = new Blob([emergencyRecoveryPackage], { type: 'application/json' });
                            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                            const a = document.createElement('a');
                            a.href = url;
                            a.download = `physiorecord_emergency_backup_${emergencyRecoverySnapshotId}.ptbackup`;
                            document.body.appendChild(a);
                            a.click();
                            document.body.removeChild(a);
                            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                          }}
                          className="mt-2 text-primary-700 hover:text-primary-800 font-bold underline flex items-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>تحميل ملف نسخة التعافي الطارئة السابقة كملف محلي (.ptbackup)</span>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    className="mt-3 px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs hover:bg-emerald-700 shadow-sm"
                  >
                    إغلاق والعودة للسجلات السريرية
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}

              {!restoreSuccess && !previewSummary && (
                /* Step A: Select File and Enter Password to Preview */
                <form onSubmit={handleInspectPreview} className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="p-4 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl text-amber-900 text-xs leading-relaxed space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold">المرحلة الأولى: فحص ومعاينة حزمة النسخ (Pre-Inspection)</span>
                    <p>
                      اختر ملف النسخة وأدخل كلمة المرور الخاصة بها ليقوم النظام بفحص محتواها وعرض مقارنة دقيقة للسجلات قبل إجراء أي استبدال.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      اختر ملف النسخة الاحتياطية (.ptbackup)
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="file"
                      accept=".ptbackup,.json"
                      onChange={handleFileChange}
                      className="w-full text-xs text-slate-500 file:mr-0 file:ml-3 file:py-2 file:px-4 file:rounded-xl file:border-0 file:text-xs file:font-semibold file:bg-primary-50 file:text-primary-700 hover:file:bg-primary-100 cursor-pointer"
                    />
                    {restoreFileName && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 font-mono">
                        <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>الملف المحدد: {restoreFileName}</span>
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      كلمة مرور حماية النسخة الاحتياطية
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="password"
                      required
                      value={restorePassword}
                      onChange={(e) => setRestorePassword(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور التي حُمي بها الملف عند التصدير"
                      className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                      dir="ltr"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={restoreLoading || !restoreFileContent || !restorePassword}
                    className="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-900 active:bg-slate-950 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    {restoreLoading ? (
                      <>
                        <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                        <span>جاري فك تشفير وفحص الترويسة والحمولة في الذاكرة...</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                        <span>فحص ومعاينة السجلات قبل الاستعادة</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </form>
              )}

              {!restoreSuccess && previewSummary && (
                /* Step B: Comparison, Confirmation & Safe Execution */
                <form onSubmit={handleExecuteRestore} className="space-y-5">
                  
                  {/* High Visibility Warning Box */}
                  <div className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border-2 border-rose-300 rounded-2xl text-rose-900 text-xs space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-rose-800 text-sm">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                      <span>تحذير سريري حاسم: استبدال شامل دون دمج (Full Replacement — No Merge)</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="leading-relaxed">
                      عملية الاستعادة الكاملة ستستبدل قاعدة البيانات الحالية على هذا الجهاز بالكامل، <strong>ولن يتم دمج البيانات</strong>. كافة السجلات التي أُضيفت على هذا الجهاز ولم تكن موجودة في النسخة سيتم استبدالها.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Side-by-side Comparison Matrix */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    
                    {/* Incoming Backup Metadata */}
                    <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl space-y-2">
                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-1.5">
                        <Upload className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
                        <span>بيانات النسخة الاحتياطية الواردة</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1 text-slate-600">
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
                          <span>تاريخ إنشاء النسخة:</span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-800 font-mono">
                            {new Date(previewSummary.exportDateUtc).toLocaleString('ar-EG')}
                          </strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
