                          <span>إصدار المخطط:</span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-800 font-mono">{previewSummary.schemaVersion}</strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
                          <span>عدد المرضى في النسخة:</span>
                          <strong className="text-primary-700 font-bold">{previewSummary.restoredPatientCount} مريض</strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
                          <span>عدد الجلسات السريرية:</span>
                          <strong className="text-primary-700 font-bold">{previewSummary.restoredSessionCount} جلسة</strong>
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Current Destination Database State */}
                    <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl space-y-2">
                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-1.5">
                        <Database className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                        <span>الوضع الحالي على هذا الجهاز</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="space-y-1 text-slate-600">
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
                          <span>المرضى الحاليون:</span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-800 font-bold">{previewSummary.currentPatientCount} مريض</strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="flex justify-between">
                          <span>آخر تعديل محلي مسجل:</span>
                          <strong className="text-slate-800 font-mono">
                            {previewSummary.currentLatestModificationDate
                              ? new Date(previewSummary.currentLatestModificationDate).toLocaleString('ar-EG')
                              : 'لا توجد سجلات بعد'}
                          </strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 p-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 mt-2">
                          سيتم حفظ نسخة تعافٍ تلقائية (Safety Backup) للبيانات الحالية قبل الاستبدال.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                  </div>

                  {/* Explicit Confirmation Checkbox */}
                  <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                    <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={confirmedDataLoss}
                        onChange={(e) => setConfirmedDataLoss(e.target.checked)}
                        className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 rounded text-primary-600 focus:ring-primary-500 border-slate-300"
                      />
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 leading-relaxed">
                        أقر بأنني راجعت المقارنة أعلاه، وعلى علم تام بأن الاستعادة ستستبدل قاعدة البيانات الحالية على هذا الجهاز ولن يتم دمجها، وأطلب المتابعة.
                      </span>
                    </label>
                  </div>

                  {/* New Vault Password on Destination Device */}
                  <div className="space-y-3 pt-1 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                      اضبط كلمة مرور الخزينة المحلية الجديدة لهذا الجهاز (لحماية المفتاح المستعاد الجديد):
                    </p>
                    
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1">
                          كلمة مرور الخزينة الجديدة
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          required
                          value={newVaultPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setNewVaultPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="6 خانات على الأقل"
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                          dir="ltr"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1">
                          تأكيد كلمة المرور
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          required
                          value={confirmNewVaultPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setConfirmNewVaultPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="أعد إدخال كلمة المرور"
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                          dir="ltr"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Dual Pre-Restore Emergency Recovery Setup */}
                  <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-xl space-y-1.5 text-blue-900 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
                      <div className="font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 text-blue-800">
                        <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                        <span>حماية البيانات الحالية عبر استراتيجية التعافي المزدوجة (Dual Recovery)</span>
                      </div>
                      <p>
                        قبل الاستبدال، سيتم تشفير وتصدير نسخة تعافٍ كاملة (.ptbackup) لحفظها كملف خارجي مستقل على جهازك، بالإضافة لحفظها داخلياً في قاعدة <code>PhysioRecordEmergencyRecoveryDB</code> كشبكة أمان منطقية.
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-blue-700 font-medium">
                        * تنبيه تقني: التخزين الداخلي للمتصفح (IndexedDB) لا يحمي من مسح بيانات الموقع أو حذف المتصفح، لذا يُلزم تصدير الملف خارج المتصفح وحفظ كلمة مروره.
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                      اضبط كلمة مرور حماية نسخة التعافي الطارئة الخارجية:
                    </p>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1">
                          كلمة مرور نسخة التعافي الطارئة
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          required
                          value={emergencyRecoveryPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setEmergencyRecoveryPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="6 خانات على الأقل"
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                          dir="ltr"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-600 mb-1">
                          تأكيد كلمة المرور
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="password"
                          required
                          value={confirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setConfirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="أعد إدخال كلمة المرور"
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-mono"
                          dir="ltr"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Explicit Acknowledgment of External Recovery */}
                    <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 rounded-xl border border-amber-200">
                      <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={acknowledgedExternalRecovery}
                          onChange={(e) => setAcknowledgedExternalRecovery(e.target.checked)}
                          className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 rounded text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500 border-amber-300"
                        />
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-900 leading-relaxed">
                          أقر بأنني قمت بضبط كلمة مرور نسخة التعافي الطارئة وسأقوم بتأكيد حفظ الملف الخارجي (.ptbackup)، وأدرك أن التخزين الداخلي للمتصفح لا يحمي من مسح بيانات الموقع أو حذف المتصفح.
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Actions */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setPreviewSummary(null)}
                      className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-100"
                    >
                      إلغاء والعودة
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={
                        restoreLoading ||
                        !confirmedDataLoss ||
                        !acknowledgedExternalRecovery ||
                        !newVaultPassword ||
                        !confirmNewVaultPassword ||
                        !emergencyRecoveryPassword ||
                        !confirmEmergencyRecoveryPassword
                      }
                      className="flex-1 py-3 px-4 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 active:bg-rose-800 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md shadow-rose-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                    >
                      {restoreLoading ? (
                        <>
                          <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                          <span>جاري توليد المفتاح الجديد، إعادة التشفير، والتبديل الذري...</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                          <span>تأكيد واستبدال قاعدة البيانات مع توليد DEK جديد</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                </form>
              )}

            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/EpisodeDischargeModal.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Episode Discharge / Closing Modal (Phase 2B.1)
// Clinical discharge documentation, final outcome summary, and end date
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import type { EpisodeRecord } from '../types/clinical';
import { X, CheckCircle, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

interface EpisodeDischargeModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  episode: EpisodeRecord;
  onConfirmDischarge: (dischargeInfo: {
    endDate: string;
    dischargeSummary: string;
    dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
  }) => Promise<void>;
}

export const EpisodeDischargeModal: React.FC<EpisodeDischargeModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  episode,
  onConfirmDischarge,
}) => {
  const { isRtl, bi } = useLanguage();
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [dischargeReason, setDischargeReason] = useState('goals_met');
  const [dischargeSummary, setDischargeSummary] = useState('');
  const [dischargeCriteriaSummary, setDischargeCriteriaSummary] = useState(
    episode.data.dischargeCriteriaSummary || ''
  );
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!dischargeSummary.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى كتابة ملخص الخروج والنتائج السريرية المحققة.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const fullSummary = `[سبب الخروج: ${getReasonLabel(dischargeReason)}]\n${dischargeSummary.trim()}`;
      await onConfirmDischarge({
        endDate,
        dischargeSummary: fullSummary,
        dischargeCriteriaSummary: dischargeCriteriaSummary.trim() || undefined,
      });
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Failed to close episode:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'حدث خطأ أثناء إغلاق حلقة الرعاية.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const getReasonLabel = (reason: string) => {
    switch (reason) {
      case 'goals_met':
        return 'تحقق الأهداف التأهيلية المخططة بالكامل (Goals Met)';
      case 'maximum_medical_improvement':
        return 'الوصول للحد الأقصى للتحسن السريري (MMI)';
      case 'patient_self_discharged':
        return bi('خروج بطلب المريض أو انقطاع طوعي', 'Patient Self-Discharged');
      case 'referred_other':
        return bi('إحالة إلى تخصص أو مركز آخر', 'Referred to Other Specialist');
      default:
        return 'أخرى';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl max-w-xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-700 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center">
              <CheckCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold">{bi('إغلاق وتخريج حلقة الرعاية السريرية', 'Discharge & Close Episode of Care')}</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-100">{episode.data.title}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-5 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          
          {error && (
            <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* End Date & Discharge Reason */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                تاريخ انتهاء / تخريج الحلقة <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                required
                value={endDate}
                onChange={(e) => setEndDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                سبب التخريج (Discharge Reason)
              </label>
              <select
                value={dischargeReason}
                onChange={(e) => setDischargeReason(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="goals_met">تحقق الأهداف العلاجية (Goals Met)</option>
                <option value="maximum_medical_improvement">أقصى تحسن سريري ممكن (MMI)</option>
                <option value="patient_self_discharged">انقطاع أو خروج بطلب المريض</option>
                <option value="referred_other">إحالة إلى استشاري / تخصص آخر</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Discharge Summary Narrative */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              ملخص الخروج والنتائج السريرية المحققة (Discharge Summary) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <textarea
              required
              rows={4}
              value={dischargeSummary}
              onChange={(e) => setDischargeSummary(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="اكتب خلاصة الاستجابة للعلاج، التحسن في مقياس الألم VAS، استعادة المدى الحركي والقوة العضلية، والتوصيات للبرنامج المنزلي المستمر..."
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 leading-relaxed"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Discharge Criteria Check */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              معايير الخروج المكتملة (Criteria Achievement)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={dischargeCriteriaSummary}
              onChange={(e) => setDischargeCriteriaSummary(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: تحقيق 130 درجة ثني الركبة، قوة عضلية 5/5، وعدم وجود ألم أثناء المشي."
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="p-3.5 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-2xl text-[11px] text-blue-900 leading-relaxed">
            بإغلاق هذه الحلقة، سيتم تحديث حالتها إلى <strong>"مكتملة / Completed"</strong>، وسيبقى كامل سجلها السريري وتاريخ جلساتها محفوظاً ومشفرة ضمن السجل التاريخي للمريض.
          </div>

        </form>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="px-6 py-3.5 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100"
          >
            إلغاء
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSubmit}
            disabled={submitting}
            className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
          >
            <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{submitting ? 'جاري الإغلاق...' : 'تأكيد التخريج وإغلاق الحلقة'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/EpisodeModal.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Episode Creation & Editing Modal (Phase 2B.1)
// Bilingual Clinical Terminology, Pre-filling from Patient, and Review
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import type { PatientRecord, EpisodeRecord, EpisodeClinicalData, ClinicalSpecialty, OnsetMechanism } from '../types/clinical';
import { CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES, ONSET_MECHANISMS, COMMON_BODY_REGIONS } from '../data/terminology';
import {
  X,
  Layers,
  Save,
  CheckCircle2,
  Sparkles,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  AlertCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface EpisodeModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  patient: PatientRecord;
  episodeToEdit?: EpisodeRecord | null;
  onSaved: (episode: EpisodeRecord) => void;
}

export const EpisodeModal: React.FC<EpisodeModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  patient,
  episodeToEdit,
  onSaved,
}) => {
  const p = patient.data;
  const isEditing = Boolean(episodeToEdit);
  const { bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();

  // Form State
  const [isOngoingLegacy, setIsOngoingLegacy] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [primarySpecialty, setPrimarySpecialty] = useState<ClinicalSpecialty>('musculoskeletal');
  const [affectedBodyRegion, setAffectedBodyRegion] = useState('');
  const [customBodyRegion, setCustomBodyRegion] = useState('');
  const [onsetDate, setOnsetDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [onsetMechanism, setOnsetMechanism] = useState<OnsetMechanism>('insidious');
  const [medicalDiagnosis, setMedicalDiagnosis] = useState('');
  const [clinicalImpression, setClinicalImpression] = useState('');
  const [referralSource, setReferralSource] = useState('');
  const [dischargeCriteria, setDischargeCriteria] = useState('');

  // Goals
  const [shortTermGoals, setShortTermGoals] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [newShortGoal, setNewShortGoal] = useState('');
  const [longTermGoals, setLongTermGoals] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [newLongGoal, setNewLongGoal] = useState('');

  // Legacy Flags
  const [isDiagnosisConfirmed, setIsDiagnosisConfirmed] = useState(true);
  const [isOnsetDateExact, setIsOnsetDateExact] = useState(true);
  const [areBaselineGoalsFormalized, setAreBaselineGoalsFormalized] = useState(true);

  // Status & Error
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Populate form on open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    setError(null);

    if (episodeToEdit) {
      const ed = episodeToEdit.data;
      setTitle(ed.title);
      setPrimarySpecialty(episodeToEdit.primarySpecialty);
      setAffectedBodyRegion(ed.affectedBodyRegion);
      setOnsetDate(episodeToEdit.startDate || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
      setOnsetMechanism(ed.onsetMechanism || 'insidious');
      setMedicalDiagnosis(ed.medicalDiagnosis);
      setClinicalImpression(ed.clinicalImpression);
      setReferralSource(ed.referralSource || '');
      setDischargeCriteria(ed.dischargeCriteriaSummary || '');
      setShortTermGoals(ed.rehabilitationGoals?.shortTerm || []);
      setLongTermGoals(ed.rehabilitationGoals?.longTerm || []);
      setIsOngoingLegacy(Boolean(ed.isOngoingLegacyEpisode));
      setIsDiagnosisConfirmed(ed.knownVsUnknownHistoryFlags?.isDiagnosisConfirmed ?? true);
      setIsOnsetDateExact(ed.knownVsUnknownHistoryFlags?.isOnsetDateExact ?? true);
      setAreBaselineGoalsFormalized(ed.knownVsUnknownHistoryFlags?.areBaselineGoalsFormalized ?? true);
    } else {
      // Pre-fill from patient profile summary for a new Episode
      const defaultTitle = `حلقة رعاية: ${p.fullNameAr || p.fullName}`;
      setTitle(defaultTitle);
      setPrimarySpecialty('musculoskeletal');
      setAffectedBodyRegion(COMMON_BODY_REGIONS[0].en);
      setCustomBodyRegion('');
      setOnsetDate(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
      setOnsetMechanism('insidious');
      setMedicalDiagnosis(p.medicalHistory || '');
      setClinicalImpression(p.generalNotes || 'فحص أولي وتقييم حركي للحالة.');
      setReferralSource('');
      setDischargeCriteria('تحسن المدى الحركي وزوال الألم واستعادة الوظيفة اليومية بنسبة 80% فأكثر.');
      setShortTermGoals([
        'تقليل حدة الألم بمقدار نقطتين على مقياس VAS خلال أسبوعين.',
        'استعادة المدى الحركي الأولي بنسبة 50% خلال أسبوعين.',
      ]);
      setLongTermGoals([
        'العودة لكامل الأنشطة اليومية والوظيفية دون ألم خلال 6 أسابيع.',
      ]);
      setIsOngoingLegacy(false);
      setIsDiagnosisConfirmed(true);
      setIsOnsetDateExact(true);
      setAreBaselineGoalsFormalized(true);
    }
  }, [isOpen, episodeToEdit, p]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleAddShortGoal = () => {
    if (newShortGoal.trim()) {
      setShortTermGoals([...shortTermGoals, newShortGoal.trim()]);
      setNewShortGoal('');
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveShortGoal = (index: number) => {
    setShortTermGoals(shortTermGoals.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleAddLongGoal = () => {
    if (newLongGoal.trim()) {
      setLongTermGoals([...longTermGoals, newLongGoal.trim()]);
      setNewLongGoal('');
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveLongGoal = (index: number) => {
    setLongTermGoals(longTermGoals.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const finalBodyRegion = affectedBodyRegion === 'custom' ? customBodyRegion : affectedBodyRegion;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!title.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى إدخال عنوان واضح لحلقة الرعاية.');
      return;
    }
    if (!medicalDiagnosis.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى كتابة التشخيص الطبي الأولي.');
      return;
    }
    if (!clinicalImpression.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى كتابة الانطباع السريري للعلاج الطبيعي.');
      return;
    }
    if (!finalBodyRegion.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى تحديد المنطقة التشريحية المصابة.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: EpisodeClinicalData = {
        title: title.trim(),
        status: episodeToEdit?.status || 'active',
        primarySpecialty,
        startDate: onsetDate,
        endDate: episodeToEdit?.endDate,
        referralSource: referralSource.trim() || undefined,
        medicalDiagnosis: medicalDiagnosis.trim(),
        clinicalImpression: clinicalImpression.trim(),
        affectedBodyRegion: finalBodyRegion.trim(),
        onsetMechanism,
        onsetDate,
        rehabilitationGoals: {
          shortTerm: shortTermGoals,
          longTerm: longTermGoals,
        },
        dischargeCriteriaSummary: dischargeCriteria.trim() || undefined,
        isOngoingLegacyEpisode: isOngoingLegacy,
        knownVsUnknownHistoryFlags: isOngoingLegacy
          ? {
              isDiagnosisConfirmed,
              isOnsetDateExact,
              areBaselineGoalsFormalized,
            }
          : undefined,
      };

      // Pass prepared payload to parent callback
      onSaved({
        id: episodeToEdit?.id || '',
        patientId: patient.id,
        status: episodeToEdit?.status || 'active',
        primarySpecialty,
        startDate: onsetDate,
        createdAt: episodeToEdit?.createdAt || new Date().toISOString(),
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        data: payload,
      });

      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Failed to save episode:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'حدث خطأ أثناء حفظ حلقة الرعاية.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl max-w-3xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh] animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="px-6 py-5 bg-gradient-to-r from-primary-600 to-teal-700 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Layers className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">
                {isEditing ? bi('تعديل حلقة الرعاية السريرية', 'Edit Episode of Care') : bi('بدء حلقة رعاية جديدة', 'Start New Episode of Care')}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-primary-100 mt-0.5">
                {bi('المريض:', 'Patient:')} <span className="font-semibold">{p.fullNameAr || p.fullName}</span> ({p.gender === 'male' ? bi('ذكر', 'Male') : bi('أنثى', 'Female')}، {p.age || '—'} {bi('سنة', 'yrs')})
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          
          {error && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl flex items-start gap-3 text-rose-800 text-sm">
              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* New vs Ongoing Legacy Selection (Only if not editing) */}
          {!isEditing && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-2">نوع حلقة الرعاية:</span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <label className={`flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-all ${!isOngoingLegacy ? 'bg-primary-50/70 border-primary-300 ring-2 ring-primary-500/20' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100/60'}`}>
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="episodeKind"
                    checked={!isOngoingLegacy}
                    onChange={() => setIsOngoingLegacy(false)}
                    className="mt-1 text-primary-600 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 block">حلقة علاجية جديدة (New Episode)</span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">تبدأ اليوم بخطة تقييم وأهداف جديدة بالكامل.</span>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className={`flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border cursor-pointer transition-all ${isOngoingLegacy ? 'bg-amber-50/70 border-amber-300 ring-2 ring-amber-500/20' : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100/60'}`}>
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    name="episodeKind"
                    checked={isOngoingLegacy}
                    onChange={() => setIsOngoingLegacy(true)}
                    className="mt-1 text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500"
                  />
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 block">توثيق حلقة قديمة مستمرة (Legacy Ongoing)</span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">انتقال من Phase 1 لحالة كانت قيد العلاج مسبقاً.</span>
                  </div>
                </label>
              </div>

              {/* Legacy ongoing explicit flags */}
              {isOngoingLegacy && (
                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200/80 space-y-2">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 block">
                    تأصيل دقة البيانات التاريخية المعروفة (Data Certainty):
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-xs">
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={isDiagnosisConfirmed}
                        onChange={(e) => setIsDiagnosisConfirmed(e.target.checked)}
                        className="rounded text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500"
                      />
                      <span>التشخيص مؤكد طبياً</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={isOnsetDateExact}
                        onChange={(e) => setIsOnsetDateExact(e.target.checked)}
                        className="rounded text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500"
                      />
                      <span>تاريخ البدء دقيق</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={areBaselineGoalsFormalized}
                        onChange={(e) => setAreBaselineGoalsFormalized(e.target.checked)}
                        className="rounded text-amber-600 focus:ring-amber-500"
                      />
                      <span>الأهداف المبدئية موثقة</span>
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Episode Title */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              عنوان حلقة الرعاية <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: تأهيل تمزق الرباط الصليبي الأمامي للركبة اليمنى"
              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-primary-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Specialty & Body Region */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            
            {/* Primary Specialty */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                التخصص السريري الرئيسي (Primary Specialty) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                value={primarySpecialty}
                onChange={(e) => setPrimarySpecialty(e.target.value as ClinicalSpecialty)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"
              >
                {Object.values(CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES).map((spec) => (
                  <option key={spec.code} value={spec.code}>
                    {spec.en} — {spec.ar}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 block">
                {CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES[primarySpecialty].descriptionAr}
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Affected Body Region */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                المنطقة التشريحية المصابة (Body Region) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                value={affectedBodyRegion}
                onChange={(e) => setAffectedBodyRegion(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"
              >
                {COMMON_BODY_REGIONS.map((reg) => (
                  <option key={reg.en} value={reg.en}>
                    {reg.en} — {reg.ar}
                  </option>
                ))}
                <option value="custom">أخرى (كتابة يدوية)...</option>
              </select>
              {affectedBodyRegion === 'custom' && (
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={customBodyRegion}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomBodyRegion(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="حدد المنطقة التشريحية..."
                  className="mt-2 w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 text-xs"
                />
              )}
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Dates & Onset Mechanism */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                تاريخ بدء الحلقة / الإصابة <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                required
                value={onsetDate}
                onChange={(e) => setOnsetDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                آلية الإصابة / البداية (Onset Mechanism)
              </label>
              <select
                value={onsetMechanism}
                onChange={(e) => setOnsetMechanism(e.target.value as OnsetMechanism)}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"
              >
                {Object.values(ONSET_MECHANISMS).map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.code} value={m.code}>
                    {m.en} — {m.ar}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Medical Diagnosis & Clinical Impression */}
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">
                  التشخيص الطبي الأولي (Medical Diagnosis) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                {!isEditing && p.medicalHistory && (
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-teal-700 flex items-center gap-1 font-medium bg-teal-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                    <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" /> مستقى من ملخص المريض
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <textarea
                required
                rows={2}
                value={medicalDiagnosis}
                onChange={(e) => setMedicalDiagnosis(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="التشخيص الطبي الصادر من الطبيب المحول أو تقارير الأشعة والتحاليل..."
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                الانطباع والتقييم السريري للعلاج الطبيعي  <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                required
                rows={2}
                value={clinicalImpression}
                onChange={(e) => setClinicalImpression(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="الانطباع السريري للمعالج: القصور الوظيفي، العجز الحركي، استجابة الأنسجة، ونمط الألم..."
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Rehabilitation Goals Section */}
          <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 space-y-4">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
              <span>الأهداف التأهيلية السريرية (Rehabilitation Goals)</span>
            </h4>

            {/* Short-Term Goals */}
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                الأهداف قصيرة المدى (Short-Term Goals — 2 to 4 weeks):
              </span>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newShortGoal}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewShortGoal(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.preventDefault(), handleAddShortGoal())}
                  placeholder="أضف هدفاً قصير المدى ثم اضغط إضافة..."
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleAddShortGoal}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> إضافة
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {shortTermGoals.map((g, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1.5 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-700">
                    <span>{g}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveShortGoal(idx)}
                      className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Long-Term Goals */}
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                الأهداف طويلة المدى وتطلعات التخريج (Long-Term Goals — 6 to 12 weeks):
              </span>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newLongGoal}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewLongGoal(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && (e.preventDefault(), handleAddLongGoal())}
                  placeholder="أضف هدفاً طويل المدى ثم اضغط إضافة..."
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleAddLongGoal}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> إضافة
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {longTermGoals.map((g, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1.5 bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-700">
                    <span>{g}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveLongGoal(idx)}
                      className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Referral & Discharge Criteria */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                الجهة أو الطبيب المحول (Referral Source)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={referralSource}
                onChange={(e) => setReferralSource(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="د. استشاري العظام / مستشفى الملك فيصل..."
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                معايير التخريج المبدئية (Discharge Criteria)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={dischargeCriteria}
                onChange={(e) => setDischargeCriteria(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="معايير إنهاء الرعاية وتحقيق التعافي..."
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs sm:text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Clinician Review & Confirmation Banner */}
          <div className="p-4 bg-teal-50/80 border border-teal-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-teal-900 leading-relaxed">
            <span className="font-bold block mb-1">إقرار ومراجعة المعالج السريري:</span>
            بالموافقة على الحفظ، أؤكد بصفتي المعالج المسؤول مراجعة صحة التشخيص الطبي والانطباع السريري وأهداف التأهيل، وسيتم تشفير هذه الحلقة محلياً بواسطة AES-256-GCM وربطها بهوية المريض دون إمكانية فك تشفيرها إلا بمفتاح الخزنة.
          </div>

        </form>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800 bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
          >
            إلغاء
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSubmit}
            disabled={saving}
            className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm"
          >
            <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{saving ? 'جاري الحفظ والتشفير...' : isEditing ? 'حفظ التعديلات' : 'اعتماد وبدء حلقة الرعاية'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/EpisodeSessionsView.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Episode Sessions & SOAP View Component (Phase 2B.2)
// Chronological Session Timeline, 1:1 SOAP Documentation & Addenda Management
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { 
  Plus, 
  Calendar, 
  Clock, 
  FileEdit, 
  Lock, 
  Edit3, 
  Trash2, 
  XCircle, 
  History, 
  FileText, 
  AlertCircle, 
  FolderOpen,
  Activity,
  Compass,
  Dumbbell,
  TrendingUp,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Brain
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  SESSION_TYPES, 
  SESSION_STATUSES, 
  ATTENDANCE_STATUSES, 
  type EpisodeRecord, 
  type PatientRecord, 
  type SessionRecord, 
  type SoapNoteRecord, 
  type SoapNoteData, 
  type SessionClinicalData, 
  type AddendumReason, 
  type ClinicalAddendumRecord 
} from '../types/clinical';
import { useSessions } from '../context/SessionContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import { SessionModal } from './SessionModal';
import { SoapModal } from './SoapModal';
import { AddendumModal } from './AddendumModal';
import { compareLongitudinalRomEntries, BODY_LATERALITIES } from '../data/clinicalToolkit/romMmtCatalog';

interface EpisodeSessionsViewProps {
  episode: EpisodeRecord;
  patient: PatientRecord;
}

export const EpisodeSessionsView: React.FC<EpisodeSessionsViewProps> = ({
  episode,
  patient,
}) => {
  const { language, bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();
  const isEn = language === 'en';

  const {
    loadSessionsForEpisode,
    createSession,
    updateSession,
    cancelSession,
    finalizeSession,
    deleteSession,
    getSoapNote,
    saveSoapNote,
    getAddenda,
    addAddendum,
  } = useSessions();

  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // SOAP notes cache mapped by sessionId
  const [soapCache, setSoapCache] = useState<Record<string, SoapNoteRecord | null>>({});
  // Addenda counts mapped by sessionId
  const [addendaCountCache, setAddendaCountCache] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});

  // Modals state
  const [isSessionModalOpen, setIsSessionModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sessionToEdit, setSessionToEdit] = useState<SessionRecord | null>(null);

  const [activeSessionForSoap, setActiveSessionForSoap] = useState<SessionRecord | null>(null);
  const [isSoapModalOpen, setIsSoapModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [activeSessionForAddendum, setActiveSessionForAddendum] = useState<SessionRecord | null>(null);
  const [addendaListForActiveSession, setAddendaListForActiveSession] = useState<ClinicalAddendumRecord[]>([]);
  const [isAddendumModalOpen, setIsAddendumModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // Cancellation prompt
  const [sessionToCancel, setSessionToCancel] = useState<SessionRecord | null>(null);
  const [cancellationReasonInput, setCancellationReasonInput] = useState('');
  const [cancellationType, setCancellationType] = useState<'cancelled_patient' | 'cancelled_clinic' | 'no_show'>('cancelled_patient');

  // Longitudinal Tracking Accordion
  const [isLongitudinalOpen, setIsLongitudinalOpen] = useState(true);

  // Longitudinal ROM Progress Comparisons (Phase 2C - Milestone 3)
  const longitudinalRomComparisons = useMemo(() => {
    const sorted = [...sessions].sort((a, b) => a.sessionNumber - b.sessionNumber);
    const sessionsWithRom = sorted.filter(
      (s) =>
        soapCache[s.id]?.data?.objective?.romBattery?.entries &&
        soapCache[s.id]!.data.objective.romBattery!.entries.length > 0
    );

    if (sessionsWithRom.length < 2) return [];

    const baselineSession = sessionsWithRom[0];
    const latestSession = sessionsWithRom[sessionsWithRom.length - 1];

    const baselineEntries = soapCache[baselineSession.id]?.data?.objective?.romBattery?.entries || [];
    const latestEntries = soapCache[latestSession.id]?.data?.objective?.romBattery?.entries || [];

    const comparisons: Array<{
      jointNameAr: string;
      movementNameAr: string;
      movementType: string;
      laterality: string;
      baselineSessionNumber: number;
      baselineDate: string;
      baselineAngle: number | null;
      baselineIsTested: boolean;
      latestSessionNumber: number;
      latestDate: string;
      latestAngle: number | null;
      latestIsTested: boolean;
      comparison: ReturnType<typeof compareLongitudinalRomEntries>;
    }> = [];

    for (const latestEntry of latestEntries) {
      const baseMatch = baselineEntries.find(
        (b) =>
          b.jointId === latestEntry.jointId &&
          b.movementId === latestEntry.movementId &&
          b.laterality === latestEntry.laterality
      );

      if (baseMatch) {
        const comp = compareLongitudinalRomEntries(baseMatch, latestEntry);
        comparisons.push({
          jointNameAr: latestEntry.jointNameAr,
          movementNameAr: latestEntry.movementNameAr,
          movementType: latestEntry.movementType,
          laterality: BODY_LATERALITIES[latestEntry.laterality]?.ar || latestEntry.laterality,
          baselineSessionNumber: baselineSession.sessionNumber,
          baselineDate: baselineSession.sessionDate,
          baselineAngle: baseMatch.measuredAngleDegrees,
          baselineIsTested: baseMatch.isTested,
          latestSessionNumber: latestSession.sessionNumber,
          latestDate: latestSession.sessionDate,
          latestAngle: latestEntry.measuredAngleDegrees,
          latestIsTested: latestEntry.isTested,
          comparison: comp,
        });
      }
    }

    return comparisons;
  }, [sessions, soapCache]);

  // Longitudinal MMT Progress Comparisons (Phase 2C - Milestone 3)
  const longitudinalMmtComparisons = useMemo(() => {
    const sorted = [...sessions].sort((a, b) => a.sessionNumber - b.sessionNumber);
    const sessionsWithMmt = sorted.filter(
      (s) =>
        soapCache[s.id]?.data?.objective?.mmtBattery?.entries &&
        soapCache[s.id]!.data.objective.mmtBattery!.entries.length > 0
    );

    if (sessionsWithMmt.length < 2) return [];

    const baselineSession = sessionsWithMmt[0];
    const latestSession = sessionsWithMmt[sessionsWithMmt.length - 1];

    const baselineEntries = soapCache[baselineSession.id]?.data?.objective?.mmtBattery?.entries || [];
    const latestEntries = soapCache[latestSession.id]?.data?.objective?.mmtBattery?.entries || [];

    const comparisons: Array<{
      muscleNameAr: string;
      actionNameAr: string;
      laterality: string;
      baselineSessionNumber: number;
      baselineGrade: string | null;
      latestSessionNumber: number;
      latestGrade: string | null;
    }> = [];

    for (const latestEntry of latestEntries) {
      const baseMatch = baselineEntries.find(
        (b) =>
          b.muscleGroupId === latestEntry.muscleGroupId &&
          b.laterality === latestEntry.laterality
      );

      if (baseMatch && baseMatch.isTested && latestEntry.isTested) {
        comparisons.push({
          muscleNameAr: latestEntry.muscleNameAr,
          actionNameAr: latestEntry.actionNameAr,
          laterality: BODY_LATERALITIES[latestEntry.laterality]?.ar || latestEntry.laterality,
          baselineSessionNumber: baselineSession.sessionNumber,
          baselineGrade: baseMatch.gradeValue,
          latestSessionNumber: latestSession.sessionNumber,
          latestGrade: latestEntry.gradeValue,
        });
      }
    }

    return comparisons;
  }, [sessions, soapCache]);

  const fetchSessionsAndNotes = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const records = await loadSessionsForEpisode(episode.id);
      setSessions(records);

      // Fetch SOAP notes and addenda counts in parallel
      const soaps: Record<string, SoapNoteRecord | null> = {};
      const addendaCounts: Record<string, number> = {};

      await Promise.all(
        records.map(async (sess) => {
          try {
            const s = await getSoapNote(sess.id);
            soaps[sess.id] = s;
            const a = await getAddenda(sess.id);
            addendaCounts[sess.id] = a.length;
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn(`Failed to prefetch SOAP or Addenda for session ${sess.id}:`, e);
          }
        })
      );

      setSoapCache(soaps);
      setAddendaCountCache(addendaCounts);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Failed to load sessions for episode:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'فشل تحميل جلسات حلقة الرعاية.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [episode.id, loadSessionsForEpisode, getSoapNote, getAddenda]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSessionsAndNotes();
  }, [fetchSessionsAndNotes]);

  // Handle Create / Edit Session
  const handleSaveSession = async (data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>) => {
    if (sessionToEdit) {
      await updateSession(sessionToEdit.id, data);
    } else {
      await createSession(patient.id, episode.id, data);
    }
    await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
  };

  // Handle Cancel Session
  const handleConfirmCancelSession = async () => {
    if (!sessionToCancel) return;
    if (!cancellationReasonInput.trim()) {
      alert('سبب الإلغاء إلزامي.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await cancelSession(sessionToCancel.id, cancellationReasonInput.trim(), cancellationType);
      setSessionToCancel(null);
      setCancellationReasonInput('');
      await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(err?.message || 'فشل إلغاء الجلسة.');
    }
  };

  // Open SOAP Modal for a session
  const handleOpenSoap = (session: SessionRecord) => {
    setActiveSessionForSoap(session);
    setIsSoapModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Save SOAP Note
  const handleSaveSoap = async (data: SoapNoteData): Promise<SoapNoteRecord> => {
    if (!activeSessionForSoap) throw new Error('لا توجد جلسة نشطة.');
    const saved = await saveSoapNote(patient.id, episode.id, activeSessionForSoap.id, data);
    setSoapCache((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeSessionForSoap.id]: saved }));
    await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
    return saved;
  };

  // Finalize Session
  const handleFinalizeSession = async (sessionId: string, therapistName: string) => {
    await finalizeSession(sessionId, therapistName);
    setIsSoapModalOpen(false);
    await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
  };

  // Open Addendum Modal
  const handleOpenAddendum = async (session: SessionRecord) => {
    setActiveSessionForAddendum(session);
    try {
      const list = await getAddenda(session.id);
      setAddendaListForActiveSession(list);
    } catch {
      setAddendaListForActiveSession([]);
    }
    setIsAddendumModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Save Addendum
  const handleSaveAddendum = async (
    targetTable: 'sessions' | 'soapNotes',
    targetId: string,
    data: {
      reasonForAmendment: AddendumReason;
      amendmentText: string;
      signedByTherapistName: string;
    }
  ) => {
    if (!activeSessionForAddendum) return;
    await addAddendum(activeSessionForAddendum.id, targetTable, targetId, data);
    await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
  };

  const isEpisodeClosed = episode.status === 'completed' || episode.status === 'archived';

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      
      {/* Header Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
              <span>{bi('سجل الجلسات وتوثيق SOAP', 'Session Timeline & SOAP Notes')}</span>
            </h4>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-mono bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold">
              {sessions.length} {bi('جلسات', 'Sessions')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            {bi('توثيق الجلسات العلاجية وملاحظات SOAP اليومية التابعة لحلقة الرعاية هذه', 'Clinical treatment documentation and daily SOAP notes for this episode')}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setSessionToEdit(null);
              setIsSessionModalOpen(true);
            }}
            disabled={isEpisodeClosed}
            title={isEpisodeClosed ? bi('لا يمكن إضافة جلسات داخل حلقة مكتملة أو مؤرشفة', 'Cannot add sessions to a closed or archived episode') : bi('إضافة جلسة سريرية', 'Add clinical session')}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 disabled:opacity-40 shadow-2xs hover:shadow transition-all"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{bi('تسجيل جلسة سريرية جديدة', 'New Clinical Session')}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 mb-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-rose-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0" />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Sessions Content */}
      {loading && sessions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-8 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">
          {bi('جاري فك تشفير وتحديث جلسات الحلقة السريرية...', 'Decrypting and loading clinical sessions...')}
        </div>
      ) : sessions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-8 px-4 text-center rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50">
          <FolderOpen className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-1.5" />
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-0.5">
            {bi('لا توجد جلسات مسجلة لهذه الحلقة بعد', 'No sessions recorded for this episode yet')}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto mb-3">
            {bi('سجل الجلسة الأولى لتوثيق فحص المريض وملاحظات SOAP.', 'Record the first evaluation session to document clinical findings and SOAP notes.')}
          </p>
          {!isEpisodeClosed && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSessionToEdit(null);
                setIsSessionModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-primary-700 bg-primary-50 hover:bg-primary-100 border border-primary-200 transition-colors"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{bi('إضافة الجلسة الأولى الآن', 'Add First Session Now')}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {/* Longitudinal Clinical Progress Tracking (Phase 2C - Milestone 3) */}
          {(longitudinalRomComparisons.length > 0 || longitudinalMmtComparisons.length > 0) && (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-50/40 via-white to-indigo-50/20 p-4 shadow-2xs">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsLongitudinalOpen(!isLongitudinalOpen)}
                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between ${isRtl ? 'text-right' : 'text-left'}`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-teal-600 text-white shadow-2xs">
                    <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span>{bi('تتبع التقدم الحركي والعضلي عبر الجلسات', 'Longitudinal Motor & ROM Progress')}</span>
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 font-mono font-bold">
                        {longitudinalRomComparisons.length + longitudinalMmtComparisons.length} {bi('مؤشرات', 'Metrics')}
                      </span>
                    </h5>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                      {bi('مقارنة تطور المدى الحركي والقوة العضلية بين الجلسة التقييمية الأولى والجلسة الأحدث', 'Comparing ROM and muscle strength progress between baseline and latest session')}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-slate-400 p-1">
                  {isLongitudinalOpen ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </div>
              </button>

              {isLongitudinalOpen && (
                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-teal-100 space-y-3">
                  {/* ROM Comparisons */}
                  {longitudinalRomComparisons.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-teal-900 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                        <span>{bi('تطور المدى الحركي:', 'Range of Motion (ROM) Progress:')}</span>
                      </span>
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                        {longitudinalRomComparisons.map((c, idx) => (
                          <div
                            key={idx}
                            className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-teal-100 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-1"
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-bold text-slate-800">
                              <span>
                                {c.jointNameAr} — {c.movementNameAr}
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                                {c.movementType} ({c.laterality})
                              </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                              <span>
                                {bi(`الأساس (جلسة #${c.baselineSessionNumber}):`, `Baseline (S#${c.baselineSessionNumber}):`)}{' '}
                                <strong className="font-mono text-slate-800">
                                  {c.baselineIsTested ? `${c.baselineAngle}°` : bi('غير مفحوص', 'Not Tested')}
                                </strong>
                              </span>
                              <span>
                                {bi(`الأحدث (جلسة #${c.latestSessionNumber}):`, `Latest (S#${c.latestSessionNumber}):`)}{' '}
                                <strong className="font-mono text-slate-800">
                                  {c.latestIsTested ? `${c.latestAngle}°` : bi('غير مفحوص', 'Not Tested')}
                                </strong>
                              </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="pt-1 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                              {c.comparison.isComparable && c.comparison.deltaDegrees !== null ? (
                                <span
                                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-bold font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                                    c.comparison.deltaDegrees > 0
                                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300'
                                      : c.comparison.deltaDegrees < 0
                                      ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-300'
                                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300'
                                  }`}
                                >
                                  {c.comparison.deltaDegrees > 0 && '+'}
                                  {c.comparison.deltaDegrees}°
                                  <span className="font-sans text-[10px] font-normal">
                                    {c.comparison.deltaDegrees > 0
                                      ? bi('(تحسن في المدى)', '(Improved)')
                                      : c.comparison.deltaDegrees < 0
                                      ? bi('(تراجع في المدى)', '(Decreased)')
                                      : bi('(استقرار)', '(Stable)')}
                                  </span>
                                </span>
                              ) : (
                                <span className="text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] border border-amber-200">
                                  {isEn ? (c.comparison.reasonEn || 'Not directly comparable') : (c.comparison.reasonAr || 'غير قابلة للمقارنة المباشرة')}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* MMT Comparisons */}
                  {longitudinalMmtComparisons.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-indigo-900 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Dumbbell className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600" />
                        <span>{bi('تطور القوة العضلية:', 'Muscle Strength (MMT) Progress:')}</span>
                      </span>
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                        {longitudinalMmtComparisons.map((c, idx) => (
                          <div
                            key={idx}
                            className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-indigo-100 text-xs shadow-2xs space-y-1"
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-bold text-slate-800">
                              <span>{c.muscleNameAr}</span>
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                                {c.actionNameAr} ({c.laterality})
                              </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                              <span>
                                {bi(`الأساس (جلسة #${c.baselineSessionNumber}):`, `Baseline (S#${c.baselineSessionNumber}):`)}{' '}
                                <strong className="font-mono text-slate-800">{bi('درجة', 'Grade')} {c.baselineGrade}</strong>
                              </span>
                              <span>
                                {bi(`الأحدث (جلسة #${c.latestSessionNumber}):`, `Latest (S#${c.latestSessionNumber}):`)}{' '}
                                <strong className="font-mono text-slate-800">{bi('درجة', 'Grade')} {c.latestGrade}</strong>
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {sessions.map((sess) => {
            const soap = soapCache[sess.id];
            const addendaCount = addendaCountCache[sess.id] || 0;
            const typeInfo = SESSION_TYPES[sess.sessionType] || SESSION_TYPES.follow_up;
            const statusInfo = SESSION_STATUSES[sess.status] || SESSION_STATUSES.draft;
            const attendanceInfo = ATTENDANCE_STATUSES[sess.attendanceStatus] || ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.attended;

            const isFinalized = sess.status === 'finalized';
            const isCancelled = sess.status === 'cancelled';

            const hasNeuroScreening = Boolean(
              soap?.data?.objective?.neuroScreening && (
                (soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.dermatomes && soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.dermatomes.length > 0) ||
                (soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.myotomes && soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.myotomes.length > 0) ||
                (soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.reflexes && soap.data.objective.neuroScreening.reflexes.length > 0)
              )
            );

            return (
              <div
                key={sess.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-xl p-4 border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs hover:border-slate-300 transition-all flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3"
              >
                {/* Left Column: Number, Type, Date & Badges */}
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-slate-900 text-white">
                      جلسة #{sess.sessionNumber}
                    </span>

                    {/* Session Type Badge */}
                    <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold border ${typeInfo.badgeClass}`}>
                      {typeInfo.ar}
                    </span>

                    {/* Status Badge */}
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold border ${statusInfo.badgeClass}`}>
                      {statusInfo.ar}
                    </span>

                    {/* Attendance Badge if not attended */}
                    {sess.attendanceStatus !== 'attended' && (
                      <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border ${attendanceInfo.badgeClass}`}>
                        {attendanceInfo.ar}
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* Addenda count badge */}
                    {addendaCount > 0 && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <History className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-700" />
                        <span>{addendaCount} ملاحق</span>
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* Body Chart Markers Badge (Phase 2C — Milestone 2) */}
                    {soap?.data?.subjective?.bodyChart?.markers && soap.data.subjective.bodyChart.markers.length > 0 && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border border-sky-200 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Activity className="w-3 h-3 text-sky-600" />
                        <span>{bi('مخطط الجسم', 'Body Chart')}: {soap.data.subjective.bodyChart.markers.length}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* ROM Measurements Badge (Phase 2C — Milestone 3) */}
                    {soap?.data?.objective?.romBattery?.entries && soap.data.objective.romBattery.entries.length > 0 && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Compass className="w-3 h-3 text-teal-600" />
                        <span>ROM: {soap.data.objective.romBattery.entries.length}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* MMT Tests Badge (Phase 2C — Milestone 3) */}
                    {soap?.data?.objective?.mmtBattery?.entries && soap.data.objective.mmtBattery.entries.length > 0 && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-800 border border-indigo-200 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Dumbbell className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-600" />
                        <span>MMT: {soap.data.objective.mmtBattery.entries.length}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* Neuro Screening Badge */}
                    {hasNeuroScreening && (
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-violet-50 text-violet-800 border border-violet-200 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Brain className="w-3 h-3 text-violet-600" />
                        <span>{bi('الفحص العصبي', 'Neuro Screening')}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Metadata line */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>{sess.sessionDate}</span>
                    </span>
                    {sess.data.sessionTime && (
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        <span dir="ltr">{sess.data.sessionTime}</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {sess.data.sessionDurationMinutes && (
                      <span>({sess.data.sessionDurationMinutes} دقيقة)</span>
                    )}
                    {sess.data.therapistName && (
                      <span>• المعالج: <strong className="text-slate-700">{sess.data.therapistName}</strong></span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Cancellation Reason if cancelled */}
                  {isCancelled && sess.data.cancellationReason && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-700 bg-rose-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-rose-200">
                      <strong>سبب الإلغاء:</strong> {sess.data.cancellationReason}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Right Column: Actions */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 self-start md:self-center">
                  
                  {/* SOAP Documentation Button */}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleOpenSoap(sess)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all border ${
                      isFinalized
                        ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                        : soap
                        ? 'bg-primary-50 text-primary-800 border-primary-300 hover:bg-primary-100'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isFinalized ? (
                      <>
                        <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                        <span>{bi('عرض SOAP المعتمدة', 'View Finalized SOAP')}</span>
                      </>
                    ) : soap ? (
                      <>
                        <FileEdit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
                        <span>{bi('تحرير مسودة SOAP', 'Edit Draft SOAP')}</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                        <span>{bi('توثيق SOAP', 'Document SOAP')}</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </button>

                  {/* Addenda Button (if finalized) */}
                  {isFinalized && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleOpenAddendum(sess)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 transition-colors"
                      title="إضافة ملحق سريري وتصحيح للسجل المعتمد"
                    >
                      <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                      <span>{addendaCount > 0 ? `الملاحق (${addendaCount})` : 'إضافة ملحق'}</span>
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {/* Edit Draft Session */}
                  {!isFinalized && !isCancelled && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
