                        setSessionToEdit(sess);
                        setIsSessionModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
                      title="تعديل بيانات الجلسة الإدارية"
                    >
                      <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {/* Cancel Draft Session */}
                  {!isFinalized && !isCancelled && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSessionToCancel(sess);
                        setCancellationReasonInput('');
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors"
                      title="إلغاء الجلسة"
                    >
                      <XCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {/* Delete Draft Session */}
                  {!isFinalized && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={async () => {
                        if (confirm(`هل أنت متأكد من حذف مسودة الجلسة #${sess.sessionNumber}؟ (لا يمكن التراجع عن هذا الإجراء)`)) {
                          try {
                            await deleteSession(sess.id);
                            await fetchSessionsAndNotes();
                          } catch (err: any) {
                            alert(err?.message || 'فشل حذف المسودة.');
                          }
                        }
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors"
                      title="حذف مسودة الجلسة غير المعتمدة"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  )}

                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Session Modal (Create / Edit) */}
      {isSessionModalOpen && (
        <SessionModal
          isOpen={isSessionModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsSessionModalOpen(false)}
          onSave={handleSaveSession}
          episode={episode}
          sessionToEdit={sessionToEdit}
          existingSessionCount={sessions.length}
        />
      )}

      {/* SOAP Modal (Draft / View / Finalize) */}
      {isSoapModalOpen && activeSessionForSoap && (
        <SoapModal
          isOpen={isSoapModalOpen}
          onClose={() => {
            setIsSoapModalOpen(false);
            setActiveSessionForSoap(null);
          }}
          session={activeSessionForSoap}
          episode={episode}
          patient={patient}
          existingSoap={soapCache[activeSessionForSoap.id] || null}
          onSaveSoap={handleSaveSoap}
          onFinalizeSession={handleFinalizeSession}
          onOpenAddendumModal={() => {
            const s = activeSessionForSoap;
            setIsSoapModalOpen(false);
            if (s) handleOpenAddendum(s);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Addendum Modal */}
      {isAddendumModalOpen && activeSessionForAddendum && (
        <AddendumModal
          isOpen={isAddendumModalOpen}
          onClose={() => {
            setIsAddendumModalOpen(false);
            setActiveSessionForAddendum(null);
          }}
          session={activeSessionForAddendum}
          existingAddenda={addendaListForActiveSession}
          onAddAddendum={handleSaveAddendum}
        />
      )}

      {/* Cancellation Dialog Prompt */}
      {sessionToCancel && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-60 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 max-w-md w-full shadow-2xl border border-rose-200">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">
              إلغاء الجلسة #{sessionToCancel.sessionNumber}
            </h3>
            
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-4">
              يرجى تحديد سبب الإلغاء ونوع التخلف لحفظه في السجل السريري للمريض:
            </p>

            <div className="space-y-3 mb-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">جهة الإلغاء:</label>
                <select
                  value={cancellationType}
                  onChange={(e) => setCancellationType(e.target.value as any)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl"
                >
                  <option value="cancelled_patient">ألغيت من قبل المريض (Cancelled by Patient)</option>
                  <option value="cancelled_clinic">ألغيت من قبل العيادة (Cancelled by Clinic)</option>
                  <option value="no_show">تخلف عن الحضور دون إشعار (No Show)</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">سبب الإلغاء بالتفصيل <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>:</label>
                <textarea
                  value={cancellationReasonInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setCancellationReasonInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="اكتب سبب الإلغاء هنا..."
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmCancelSession}
                disabled={!cancellationReasonInput.trim()}
                className="flex-1 py-2 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                تأكيد إلغاء الجلسة
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSessionToCancel(null)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                تراجع
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/LockScreen.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Vault Lock & Setup Screen
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useVault } from '../context/VaultContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import { Lock, Unlock, ShieldAlert, KeyRound, AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, Globe } from 'lucide-react';

export const LockScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { isInitialized, setupPassword, unlock, wipeDatabase } = useVault();
  const { language, toggleLanguage, bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [showResetConfirm, setShowResetConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [resetInput, setResetInput] = useState('');

  const handleSetup = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError(bi('يجب أن تتكون كلمة المرور من 6 خانات على الأقل.', 'Password must be at least 6 characters long.'));
      return;
    }
    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError(bi('كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين.', 'Passwords do not match.'));
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await setupPassword(password);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || bi('فشل ضبط كلمة المرور', 'Failed to set password'));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleUnlock = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const success = await unlock(password);
      if (!success) {
        setError(bi('كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.', 'Incorrect password. Please try again.'));
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || bi('حدث خطأ أثناء فك التشفير', 'Error during decryption'));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleWipe = async () => {
    if (resetInput !== (bi('مسح', 'WIPE'))) {
      setError(bi('يرجى كتابة كلمة "مسح" لتأكيد إعادة التعيين.', 'Please type "WIPE" to confirm reset.'));
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await wipeDatabase();
      setShowResetConfirm(false);
      setPassword('');
      setConfirmPassword('');
      setError(bi('تم مسح قاعدة البيانات بنجاح. يمكنك الآن ضبط كلمة مرور جديدة.', 'Database wiped successfully. You can now set a new password.'));
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(bi('حدث خطأ أثناء مسح قاعدة البيانات: ', 'Error during wipe: ') + err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-slate-800 to-primary-950 flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 selection:bg-primary-500 selection:text-white relative" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      
      {/* Language Switcher Button */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={toggleLanguage}
        className="absolute top-4 right-4 sm:top-6 sm:right-6 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-white/80 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 transition-all border border-white/20 shadow-xs"
      >
        <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>{language === 'ar' ? 'English' : 'العربية'}</span>
      </button>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-slate-100">
        
        {/* Header Icon & Title */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mb-6">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl bg-primary-50 flex items-center justify-center text-primary-600 mb-3 shadow-inner">
            {isInitialized ? <Lock className="w-7 h-7" /> : <ShieldAlert className="w-7 h-7" />}
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">
            {isInitialized ? bi('الخزينة السريرية مقفلة', 'Clinical Vault Locked') : bi('تهيئة نظام PhysioRecord', 'Initialize PhysioRecord')}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
            {isInitialized
              ? bi('أدخل كلمة المرور لفك تشفير والوصول إلى سجلات المرضى', 'Enter password to decrypt and access patient records')
              : bi('قم بضبط كلمة مرور الخزينة لحماية وتشفير سجلات المرضى محلياً', 'Set vault password to encrypt and protect records locally')}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Error Alert */}
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-5 p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-rose-700 text-xs flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 text-rose-600 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="leading-relaxed">{error}</div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Unlock Form */}
        {isInitialized ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleUnlock} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                {bi('كلمة مرور الخزينة', 'Vault Password')}
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  autoFocus
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent transition-all pr-10 text-slate-800 text-left font-mono text-sm"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
                <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute right-3 top-3" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || !password}
              className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 active:bg-primary-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  <span>{bi('جاري فك التشفير والتحقق...', 'Decrypting and verifying...')}</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Unlock className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{bi('فتح الخزينة والوصول للسجلات', 'Unlock Vault & Access Records')}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          /* Initial Setup Form */
          <form onSubmit={handleSetup} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg text-amber-800 text-xs leading-relaxed flex items-start gap-2">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-amber-700" />
              <span>
                <strong>{bi('تنبيه سريري هام:', 'Important Clinical Notice:')}</strong> {bi('تعتمد هذه الخزينة على معمارية المعرفة الصفرية. يتم تشفير كافة بيانات المرضى محلياً بـ AES-256-GCM. نسيان كلمة المرور يعني فقدان البيانات نهائياً لعدم وجود أي خادم استرداد.', 'This vault operates on zero-knowledge architecture. All patient data is encrypted locally with AES-256-GCM. Losing your password results in permanent data loss as no recovery server exists.')}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                {bi('اختر كلمة مرور قوية للخزينة', 'Choose a Strong Vault Password')}
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  autoFocus
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent transition-all pr-10 text-slate-800 text-left font-mono text-sm"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
                <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute right-3 top-3" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">
                {bi('الحد الأدنى 6 خانات (يفضل استخدام أحرف وأرقام ورموز)', 'Minimum 6 characters')}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                {bi('تأكيد كلمة المرور', 'Confirm Password')}
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent transition-all pr-10 text-slate-800 text-left font-mono text-sm"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
                <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute right-3 top-3" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || !password || !confirmPassword}
              className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 active:bg-primary-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  <span>{bi('جاري التوليد والتهيئة...', 'Initializing...')}</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{bi('تهيئة الخزينة وبدء الاستخدام', 'Initialize Vault')}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        {/* Reset / Wipe options */}
        {isInitialized && (
          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
            {!showResetConfirm ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowResetConfirm(true)}
                className="text-xs text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-700 hover:underline transition-colors"
              >
                {bi('هل نسيت كلمة المرور؟ أو تريد مسح قاعدة البيانات للبدء من جديد؟', 'Forgot password or need to reset the local database?')}
              </button>
            ) : (
              <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-right">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-rose-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('تحذير: سيتم حذف كافة المرضى والسجلات بشكل نهائي وغير قابل للاسترجاع!', 'Warning: All patients and clinical records will be permanently deleted!')}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 mb-2">
                  {bi('اكتب كلمة مسح في الحقل التالي للتأكيد:', 'Type WIPE to confirm database reset:')}
                </p>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={resetInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setResetInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder={bi('اكتب: مسح', 'Type: WIPE')}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs border border-rose-300 rounded-lg mb-2 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500"
                />
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleWipe}
                    disabled={(resetInput !== bi('مسح', 'WIPE')) || loading}
                    className="flex-1 py-1.5 bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {bi('تأكيد المسح الشامل', 'Confirm Wipe')}
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setShowResetConfirm(false); setResetInput(''); }}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-300"
                  >
                    {bi('إلغاء', 'Cancel')}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

      </div>

      {/* Footer Info */}
      <footer className="mt-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
        PhysioRecord — {bi('جميع البيانات مشفرة ومحفوظة محلياً على جهازك', '100% Encrypted & Local-First')}
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

  onOpenBackupModal: () => void;
}

  onOpenSyntheticModal,
  onOpenPatientModal,

  const { patients } = usePatients();
  const { language, toggleLanguage, t } = useLanguage();

        {/* Brand & Title */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-primary-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-md shadow-primary-600/30">
            <Activity className="w-6 h-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">{t('appName')}</h1>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 border border-primary-200">
                {t('clinicBadge')}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 hidden sm:block">
              {t('appSubtitle')}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Security & Local Status Badges */}

          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
            <span>{t('encryptedLocalBadge')}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200">
            <CloudOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
            <span>{t('noCloudBadge')}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 border border-primary-200">
            <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
            <span>{patients.length} {t('patientCountBadge')}</span>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Action Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Language Toggle Button (Bilingual Support: AR / EN) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleLanguage}
            title={language === 'ar' ? 'Switch to English' : 'التحويل إلى العربية'}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors border border-slate-200"
          >
            <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
            <span className="font-semibold">{language === 'ar' ? 'English' : 'العربية'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Add Synthetic Data Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenSyntheticModal}
            title="إضافة حالات سريرية تجريبية للاختبار"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors border border-slate-200"
          >
            <Database className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
            <span>{t('syntheticData')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Backup and Restore Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenBackupModal}
            title="إدارة النسخ الاحتياطي والاستعادة المشفرة (.ptbackup)"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors border border-slate-200"
          >
            <Database className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
            <span className="hidden md:inline">{t('backupRestore')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* New Patient Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenPatientModal}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 active:bg-primary-800 transition-all shadow-sm shadow-primary-600/20"
          >
            <span>{t('newPatient')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Lock Vault Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={lock}
            title="قفل الخزينة فوراً ومسح الذاكرة"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 border border-slate-200 hover:border-rose-200 transition-all"
          >
            <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-rose-600" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{t('lockVault')}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/PatientDirectory.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Patient Directory Component (v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { usePatients } from '../context/PatientContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import type { PatientRecord } from '../types/clinical';
import { 
  Search, 
  UserPlus, 
  AlertCircle, 
  ChevronLeft, 
  ChevronRight,
  Phone, 
  Calendar, 
  Sparkles,
  HeartPulse,
  Briefcase,
  Archive,
  RotateCcw,
  Trash2
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PatientDirectoryProps {
  onSelectPatient: (patient: PatientRecord) => void;
  onOpenNewPatientModal: () => void;
  onOpenSyntheticModal: () => void;
}

export const PatientDirectory: React.FC<PatientDirectoryProps> = ({
  onSelectPatient,
  onOpenNewPatientModal,
  onOpenSyntheticModal,
}) => {
  const { 
    activePatients, 
    archivedPatients, 
    loading, 
    archivePatient, 
    unarchivePatient
  } = usePatients();
  const { bi, isRtl, language } = useLanguage();
  const isEn = language === 'en';

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'active' | 'archived'>('active');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterGender, setFilterGender] = useState<'all' | 'male' | 'female'>('all');
  const [filterRedFlagsOnly, setFilterRedFlagsOnly] = useState(false);

  const displayedList = activeTab === 'active' ? activePatients : archivedPatients;

  // In-memory zero-PII search
  const filteredPatients = useMemo(() => {
    return displayedList.filter((patient) => {
      const p = patient.data;
      const query = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch = 
        !query ||
        p.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (p.fullNameAr && p.fullNameAr.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||
        (p.phoneNumber && p.phoneNumber.includes(query)) ||
        (p.medicalHistory && p.medicalHistory.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||
        (p.occupation && p.occupation.toLowerCase().includes(query));

      const matchesGender = filterGender === 'all' || p.gender === filterGender;
      const matchesRedFlags = !filterRedFlagsOnly || (p.redFlags && p.redFlags.trim().length > 0);

      return matchesSearch && matchesGender && matchesRedFlags;
    });
  }, [displayedList, searchQuery, filterGender, filterRedFlagsOnly]);

  const ChevronIcon = isRtl ? ChevronLeft : ChevronRight;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      
      {/* Directory Tabs: Active vs Archived */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 mb-6">
        <div className="flex gap-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('active')}
            className={`pb-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'active'
                ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('المرضى النشطون', 'Active Patients')}</span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 border border-primary-200">
              {activePatients.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('archived')}
            className={`pb-3 text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'archived'
                ? 'border-amber-600 text-amber-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <Archive className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{bi('سجل الأرشيف', 'Archived Records')}</span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
              {archivedPatients.length}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search and Filters Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-xl shadow-2xs border border-slate-200 mb-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch md:items-center justify-between">
          
          {/* Search Input */}
          <div className="relative flex-1">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={bi('بحث فوري باسم المريض، رقم الهاتف، التشخيص، أو التاريخ الطبي...', 'Instant search by name, phone, diagnosis, or clinical history...')}
              className={`w-full ${isRtl ? 'pl-4 pr-11' : 'pr-4 pl-11'} py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-transparent text-xs sm:text-sm transition-all text-slate-800`}
            />
            <Search className={`w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute ${isRtl ? 'right-3.5' : 'left-3.5'} top-3`} />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className={`absolute ${isRtl ? 'left-3' : 'right-3'} top-2 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md`}
              >
                {bi('مسح', 'Clear')}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Filters */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <select
              value={filterGender}
              onChange={(e) => setFilterGender(e.target.value as any)}
              className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
            >
              <option value="all">{bi('كافة الفئات', 'All Genders')}</option>
              <option value="male">{bi('ذكور', 'Male')}</option>
              <option value="female">{bi('إناث', 'Female')}</option>
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilterRedFlagsOnly(!filterRedFlagsOnly)}
              className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium border flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
                filterRedFlagsOnly
                  ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-300'
                  : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              <AlertCircle className={`w-4 h-4 ${filterRedFlagsOnly ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
              <span>{bi('علامات حمراء تحذيرية', 'Red Flags')}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Directory Grid */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-400 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
          <p className="animate-pulse">{bi('جاري فك تشفير وتحميل السجلات السريرية المعتمدة...', 'Decrypting and loading verified clinical records...')}</p>
        </div>
      ) : filteredPatients.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filteredPatients.map((patient) => {
            const p = patient.data;
            const hasRedFlags = Boolean(p.redFlags && p.redFlags.trim().length > 0);

            return (
              <div
                key={patient.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 border border-slate-200 hover:border-primary-400 hover:shadow-sm transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
              >
                <div onClick={() => onSelectPatient(patient)} className="cursor-pointer">
                  {/* Card Header */}
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-2.5">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 hover:text-primary-700 transition-colors text-base">
                        {isEn ? (p.fullName || p.fullNameAr) : (p.fullNameAr || p.fullName)}
                      </h3>
                      {p.fullNameAr && p.fullName !== p.fullNameAr && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono" dir="ltr">
                          {isEn ? p.fullNameAr : p.fullName}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${
                      p.gender === 'male' ? 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border border-sky-200' : 'bg-pink-50 text-pink-700 border border-pink-200'
                    }`}>
                      {p.gender === 'male' ? bi('ذكر', 'Male') : bi('أنثى', 'Female')} {p.age ? `(${p.age} ${bi('سنة', 'yrs')})` : ''}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Red Flags Alert Badge */}
                  {hasRedFlags && (
                    <div className="mb-3 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg text-rose-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                      <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">{bi('محاذير / علامات حمراء منبهة', 'Clinical Alert / Red Flags')}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Medical Summary Snippet */}
                  {p.medicalHistory && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 mb-3 bg-slate-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                      {p.medicalHistory}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  {/* Metadata */}
                  <div className="space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
                    {p.phoneNumber && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        <span dir="ltr">{p.phoneNumber}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {p.occupation && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        <span>{p.occupation}</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>{bi('آخر تحديث:', 'Updated:')} {new Date(patient.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString(isEn ? 'en-US' : 'ar-SA')}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Card Action Controls */}
                <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectPatient(patient)}
                    className="text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-700 flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold"
                  >
                    <span>{bi('فتح الملف السريري', 'Open Patient File')}</span>
                    <ChevronIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>

                  {/* Archival Actions */}
                  {activeTab === 'active' ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        archivePatient(patient.id);
                      }}
                      title={bi('أرشفة المريض مع الحفاظ الكامل على كافة سجلاته السريرية', 'Archive patient and preserve all clinical records')}
                      className="text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md hover:bg-amber-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                    >
                      <Archive className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{bi('أرشفة', 'Archive')}</span>
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          unarchivePatient(patient.id);
                        }}
                        title={bi('استعادة المريض إلى القائمة النشطة', 'Restore patient to active records')}
                        className="text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md hover:bg-emerald-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                      >
                        <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>{bi('استعادة', 'Restore')}</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onSelectPatient(patient);
                        }}
                        title={bi('مراجعة السجلات التابعة للإتلاف النهائي من ملف المريض', 'Review and delete archived patient')}
                        className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 p-1 rounded hover:bg-rose-50 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>{bi('مراجعة الإتلاف', 'Review Disposal')}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ) : displayedList.length === 0 ? (
        /* Empty State */
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs max-w-lg mx-auto">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-primary-50 text-primary-600 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
            <HeartPulse className="w-8 h-8" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 mb-1">
            {activeTab === 'active' ? bi('لا يوجد مرضى مسجلون في القائمة النشطة', 'No active patient records found') : bi('الأرشيف فارغ حالياً', 'Archive is currently empty')}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-6">
            {activeTab === 'active'
              ? bi('ابدأ بتسجيل أول مريض، أو قم بإدراج بيانات سريرية تجريبية لاستكشاف إمكانيات النظام.', 'Start by adding a new patient, or generate synthetic clinical cases to test the system.')
              : bi('لم يتم نقل أي مريض إلى الأرشيف حتى الآن.', 'No patients have been archived yet.')}
          </p>
          {activeTab === 'active' && (
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 justify-center">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenNewPatientModal}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white font-medium rounded-lg text-xs transition-all shadow-2xs"
              >
                <UserPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{bi('تسجيل مريض جديد', 'New Patient')}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenSyntheticModal}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 font-medium rounded-lg text-xs transition-all"
              >
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
                <span>{bi('إدراج بيانات تجريبية جاهزة', 'Load Synthetic Cases')}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* No Search Match */
        <div className="p-8 text-center bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-slate-500 text-xs">
          {bi(`لا توجد نتائج مطابقة لبحثك: "${searchQuery}".`, `No matching results for: "${searchQuery}".`)}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/PatientModal.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Patient Creation & Editing Modal
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { usePatients } from '../context/PatientContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import type { PatientClinicalData, PatientRecord, Gender, DominantHand } from '../types/clinical';
import { 
  X, 
  Save, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  User, 
  FileText, 
  ShieldAlert 
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PatientModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  patientToEdit?: PatientRecord | null;
  onSavedSuccess?: (patient: PatientRecord) => void;
}

export const PatientModal: React.FC<PatientModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  patientToEdit,
  onSavedSuccess,
}) => {
  const { savePatient } = usePatients();
  const { bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();

  // Tab navigation inside modal
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'general' | 'medical' | 'precautions'>('general');

  // Form State
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<PatientClinicalData>({
    fullName: '',
    fullNameAr: '',
    dateOfBirth: '',
    age: undefined,
    gender: 'male',
    phoneNumber: '',
    address: '',
    occupation: '',
    dominantHand: 'right',
    medicalHistory: '',
    previousSurgeries: '',
    currentMedications: '',
    allergies: '',
    comorbidities: '',
    precautions: '',
    redFlags: '',
    psychosocialFactors: '',
    generalNotes: '',
  });

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Initialize form when opening or editing
  useEffect(() => {
    if (patientToEdit) {
      setFormData({ ...patientToEdit.data });
    } else {
      setFormData({
        fullName: '',
        fullNameAr: '',
        dateOfBirth: '',
        age: undefined,
        gender: 'male',
        phoneNumber: '',
        address: '',
        occupation: '',
        dominantHand: 'right',
        medicalHistory: '',
        previousSurgeries: '',
        currentMedications: '',
        allergies: '',
        comorbidities: '',
        precautions: '',
        redFlags: '',
        psychosocialFactors: '',
        generalNotes: '',
      });
    }
    setActiveTab('general');
    setError(null);
  }, [patientToEdit, isOpen]);

  // Calculate age automatically when DOB changes
  const handleDobChange = (dob: string) => {
    let calculatedAge: number | undefined = undefined;
    if (dob) {
      const birth = new Date(dob);
      const today = new Date();
      let age = today.getFullYear() - birth.getFullYear();
      const m = today.getMonth() - birth.getMonth();
      if (m < 0 || (m === 0 && today.getDate() < birth.getDate())) {
        age--;
      }
      calculatedAge = age >= 0 ? age : undefined;
    }
    setFormData(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      dateOfBirth: dob,
      age: calculatedAge !== undefined ? calculatedAge : prev.age,
    }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!formData.fullName.trim() && !formData.fullNameAr?.trim()) {
      setError('يرجى إدخال اسم المريض (بالعربية أو الإنجليزية).');
      setActiveTab('general');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      // Stamp the profile summary edit timestamp before saving.
      // This timestamp records when this demographic/medical background summary was last updated.
      // It is NOT a clinical measurement record — session-level ROM/MMT measurements are Phase 2.
      const stampedFormData = {
        ...formData,
        profileSummaryUpdatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      const saved = await savePatient(stampedFormData, patientToEdit?.id);
      if (onSavedSuccess) onSavedSuccess(saved);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || 'فشل حفظ بيانات المريض.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex justify-center items-center p-4" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-3xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-100 flex flex-col max-h-[92vh]">
        
        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {patientToEdit ? bi('تعديل الملف السريري للمريض', 'Edit Patient Clinical Profile') : bi('تسجيل مريض جديد', 'New Patient Registration')}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {patientToEdit
                ? 'تعديل الملخص السريري الديموغرافي — قياسات ROM/MMT المتسلسلة تُسجَّل في جلسات المرحلة الثانية'
                : 'كافة البيانات تُشفر محلياً بـ AES-256-GCM قبل حفظها في المتصفح'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Tabs */}
        <div className="px-6 pt-3 flex gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('general')}
            className={`pb-3 px-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'general'
                ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <User className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{bi('البيانات الأساسية', 'Demographics')}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('medical')}
            className={`pb-3 px-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'medical'
                ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{bi('التاريخ الطبي والجراحي', 'Medical History')}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('precautions')}
            className={`pb-3 px-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'precautions'
                ? 'border-rose-600 text-rose-600'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{bi('المحاذير والأعلام الحمراء', 'Precautions & Red Flags')}</span>
            {Boolean(formData.redFlags?.trim()) && (
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-500"></span>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-5">
          
          {error && (
            <div className="p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-700 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 1: General Info */}
          {activeTab === 'general' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    اسم المريض بالعربية <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formData.fullNameAr || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, fullNameAr: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="مثال: أحمد بن فهد الشمري"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Full Name (English)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formData.fullName || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, fullName: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="e.g. Ahmed Al-Shammari"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left font-sans"
                    dir="ltr"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('تاريخ الميلاد', 'Date of Birth')}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    value={formData.dateOfBirth || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => handleDobChange(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    العمر (سنوات)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0"
                    max="125"
                    value={formData.age !== undefined ? formData.age : ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, age: e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value, 10) : undefined })}
                    placeholder="يُحسب تلقائياً أو يُدخل يدوياً"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('الجنس', 'Gender')}
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formData.gender}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, gender: e.target.value as Gender })}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"
                  >
                    <option value="male">ذكر</option>
                    <option value="female">أنثى</option>
                    <option value="other">أخرى</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('رقم الهاتف', 'Phone Number')}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    value={formData.phoneNumber || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phoneNumber: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="05XXXXXXXX"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 text-left"
                    dir="ltr"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    المهنة ونمط العمل
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formData.occupation || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, occupation: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="مثال: موظف مكتبي، معلم، رياضي"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    اليد المهيمنة
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formData.dominantHand}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, dominantHand: e.target.value as DominantHand })}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 bg-white"
                  >
                    <option value="right">{bi('اليمنى', 'Right')}</option>
                    <option value="left">{bi('اليسرى', 'Left')}</option>
                    <option value="ambidextrous">كلتا اليدين (Ambidextrous)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  {bi('عنوان السكن', 'Residential Address')} (اختياري)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.address || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, address: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="المدينة — الحي"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: Medical History */}
          {activeTab === 'medical' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  التاريخ المرضي العام وطبيعة الشكوى الحالية
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={3}
                  value={formData.medicalHistory || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, medicalHistory: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="وصف الإصابة، آلية الحدوث، التاريخ الزمني، التشخيص الأولي..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    العمليات الجراحية السابقة
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={formData.previousSurgeries || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, previousSurgeries: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="أي جراحات سابقة مع تواريخها..."
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('الأدوية الحالية المستمرة', 'Current Medications')}
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={formData.currentMedications || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, currentMedications: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="المسكنات، مميعات الدم، أدوية الضغط..."
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('الحساسية المعروفة', 'Known Allergies')}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formData.allergies || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, allergies: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="حساسية دوائية، لاصقات طبية، لاتكس..."
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    {bi('الأمراض المصاحبة', 'Comorbidities')}
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={formData.comorbidities || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, comorbidities: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="سكري، ضغط، هشاشة عظام، قلب..."
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: Precautions & Red Flags */}
          {activeTab === 'precautions' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl">
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-rose-800 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600" />
                  <span>{bi('الأعلام الحمراء (تستدعي تحويلاً أو إيقافاً فورياً)', 'Red Flags (Immediate Medical Referral / Contraindication)')}</span>
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={formData.redFlags || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, redFlags: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="مثال: اشتباه DVT، متلازمة ذيل الفرس (Cauda Equina)، ألم صدري غير مستقر، اضطراب عصبي تصاعدي مفاجئ..."
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-rose-300 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 bg-white"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  {bi('المحاذير وموانع العلاج أثناء التأهيل', 'Clinical Precautions & Contraindications')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={formData.precautions || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, precautions: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="مثال: قيود تحميل الوزن، محاذير التواء الكتف، محاذير عظم القص بعد الجراحة..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  {bi('العوامل النفسية الاجتماعية والبيئية', 'Psychosocial Factors & Yellow Flags')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={formData.psychosocialFactors || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, psychosocialFactors: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="مستوى الدافعية، مخاوف الحركة (Kinesiophobia)، الدعم الأسري، ضغوط العمل..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  {bi('ملاحظات عامة للمعالج', 'General Clinical Notes')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={formData.generalNotes || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, generalNotes: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="أي توجيهات أو ملاحظات إضافية..."
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Modal Footer Controls */}
          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition-colors"
            >
              إلغاء
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl transition-all shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{saving ? bi('جاري التشفير والحفظ...', 'Encrypting & Saving...') : bi('حفظ بيانات المريض', 'Save Patient Record')}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/PatientProfile.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Patient Clinical Profile View (v1.1)
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { PatientRecord, EpisodeRecord, PatientDependentRecordsSummary } from '../types/clinical';
import { db } from '../db/db';
import { usePatients } from '../context/PatientContext';
import { useEpisodes } from '../context/EpisodeContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { EpisodeModal } from './EpisodeModal';
import { EpisodeDischargeModal } from './EpisodeDischargeModal';
import { EpisodeSessionsView } from './EpisodeSessionsView';
import { CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES, EPISODE_STATUSES } from '../data/terminology';
import { 
  ArrowRight, 
  Edit3, 
  Archive, 
  RotateCcw,
  Trash2, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  ShieldAlert,
  Phone, 
  MapPin, 
  Briefcase, 
  Calendar, 
  Layers, 
  FileText, 
  Activity, 
  Pill, 
  Scissors,
  ShieldCheck,
  Plus,
  PlayCircle,
  PauseCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Clock,
  Sparkles,
  Target,
  FolderOpen
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PatientProfileProps {
  patient: PatientRecord;
  onBack: () => void;
  onEdit: (patient: PatientRecord) => void;
}

export const PatientProfile: React.FC<PatientProfileProps> = ({
  patient,
  onBack,
  onEdit,
}) => {
  const { archivePatient, unarchivePatient, deletePatientPermanently } = usePatients();
  const { bi, isRtl, language } = useLanguage();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const {
    episodes,
    loadingEpisodes,
    loadEpisodesForPatient,
    createEpisode,
    updateEpisode,
    setEpisodeStatus,
    closeEpisode,
    archiveEpisode,
    unarchiveEpisode,
  } = useEpisodes();

  const [showPermanentDeleteModal, setShowPermanentDeleteModal] = useState(false);
  const [confirmNameInput, setConfirmNameInput] = useState('');
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
  const [dependentAudit, setDependentAudit] = useState<PatientDependentRecordsSummary | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showPermanentDeleteModal && patient.id) {
      db.auditPatientDependentRecords(patient.id).then(setDependentAudit).catch(console.error);
    }
  }, [showPermanentDeleteModal, patient.id]);

  // Episodes UI State
  const [isEpisodeModalOpen, setIsEpisodeModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [episodeToEdit, setEpisodeToEdit] = useState<EpisodeRecord | null>(null);
  const [episodeToDischarge, setEpisodeToDischarge] = useState<EpisodeRecord | null>(null);
  const [episodeTabFilter, setEpisodeTabFilter] = useState<'all' | 'active' | 'completed' | 'on_hold' | 'archived'>('active');
  const [expandedEpisodeId, setExpandedEpisodeId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (patient.id) {
      loadEpisodesForPatient(patient.id);
    }
  }, [patient.id, loadEpisodesForPatient]);

  const p = patient.data;
  const isArchived = Boolean(patient.isArchived);
  const hasRedFlags = Boolean(p.redFlags && p.redFlags.trim().length > 0);
  const targetConfirmName = p.fullNameAr || p.fullName;

  const handleSaveEpisode = async (record: EpisodeRecord) => {
    if (episodeToEdit) {
      await updateEpisode(episodeToEdit.id, record.data);
    } else {
      await createEpisode(patient.id, record.data);
    }
    setIsEpisodeModalOpen(false);
    setEpisodeToEdit(null);
  };

  const handleConfirmDischarge = async (dischargeInfo: {
    endDate: string;
    dischargeSummary: string;
    dischargeCriteriaSummary?: string;
  }) => {
    if (episodeToDischarge) {
      await closeEpisode(episodeToDischarge.id, dischargeInfo);
      setEpisodeToDischarge(null);
    }
  };

  const activeEpisodes = episodes.filter(e => e.status === 'active');
  const onHoldEpisodes = episodes.filter(e => e.status === 'on_hold');
  const completedEpisodes = episodes.filter(e => e.status === 'completed');
  const archivedEpisodes = episodes.filter(e => e.status === 'archived');

  const filteredEpisodes = episodes.filter(e => {
    if (episodeTabFilter === 'all') return true;
    return e.status === episodeTabFilter;
  });

  const handleArchiveToggle = async () => {
    if (isArchived) {
      await unarchivePatient(patient.id);
    } else {
      await archivePatient(patient.id);
      onBack();
    }
  };

  const handlePermanentDelete = async () => {
    if (confirmNameInput.trim() !== targetConfirmName.trim()) {
      return;
    }
    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      await deletePatientPermanently(patient.id);
      onBack();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to permanently delete patient:', err);
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      
      {/* Top Navigation Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-6">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onBack}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 transition-colors shadow-sm"
        >
          {isRtl ? <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />}
          <span>{bi('العودة لدليل المرضى', 'Back to Patients')}</span>
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Edit Profile */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onEdit(patient)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 transition-all shadow-sm"
          >
            <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
            <span>{bi('تعديل الملف السريري', 'Edit Profile')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Soft-Archive Toggle Button (Default safe action) */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleArchiveToggle}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium border transition-all ${
              isArchived
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100'
            }`}
          >
            {isArchived ? (
              <>
                <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{bi('استعادة من الأرشيف', 'Restore from Archive')}</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Archive className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{bi('أرشفة المريض', 'Archive Patient')}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Permanent Deletion Button (Only available if archived) */}
          {isArchived && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPermanentDeleteModal(true)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 transition-all"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{bi('إتلاف مادي نهائي', 'Permanent Delete')}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Archive Status Notice */}
      {isArchived && (
        <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between text-amber-900 text-xs sm:text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Archive className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>
              {bi('ملف مؤرشف: هذا المريض في سجل الأرشيف. كافة بياناته وجلساته السابقة محفوظة ومحمية، لكنه لا يظهر في قائمة المرضى النشطين.', 'Archived File: This patient is in the archive. All historical records are securely preserved.')}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Permanent Deletion Safeguard Modal with Patient Hard Deletion Safety Gate */}
      {showPermanentDeleteModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 max-w-lg w-full shadow-2xl border border-rose-200">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1">
              {bi('مراجعة وصمام أمان الحذف المادي النهائي للسجل', 'Permanent Deletion Safety Gate Review')}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-rose-700 leading-relaxed mb-4">
              هذا الإجراء مخصص للإتلاف المادي الكامل. وفقاً لسياسة الاحتفاظ السريري، لا يُسمح بإتلاف السجلات إلا بعد إثبات وجود نسخة احتياطية خارجية ناجحة ومتحقق منها.
            </p>

            {/* Audit of Dependent Records */}
            <div className="mb-4 p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-700 space-y-2">
              <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-1">السجلات السريرية التابعة المرتبطة بهذا المريض:</div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                <div className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">حلقات الرعاية: </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{dependentAudit?.episodesCount ?? '...'}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">الجلسات العلاجية: </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{dependentAudit?.sessionsCount ?? '...'}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">ملاحظات SOAP: </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{dependentAudit?.soapNotesCount ?? '...'}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">الملاحق السريرية: </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{dependentAudit?.clinicalAddendaCount ?? '...'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Safety Gate Alert Notice */}
            <div className="mb-4 p-3.5 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 rounded-xl text-xs text-amber-950 space-y-1">
              <div className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-900">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 flex-shrink-0" />
                <span>{bi('صمام أمان الحذف النهائي نشط', 'Permanent Deletion Safety Gate Active')}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="leading-relaxed">
                وفقاً لسياسة الأمان السريري، لا يُسمح بالحذف النهائي إلا بوجود إثبات تحقق من نسخة احتياطية خارجية ناجحة.
                تم تعطيل الحذف النهائي مؤقتاً لعدم توفر إثبات النسخة الخارجية في هذه الجلسة. أرشفة ملف المريض هي الإجراء السريري المعتمد لحفظ السجل وتجميده.
              </p>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-2">
              اسم المريض المراد حمايته: <strong className="font-bold select-all text-slate-900">{targetConfirmName}</strong>
            </p>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={confirmNameInput}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmNameInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="اكتب اسم المريض هنا..."
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 rounded-xl mb-4 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handlePermanentDelete}
                disabled={true}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-slate-300 text-slate-500 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold cursor-not-allowed transition-all"
                title="الحذف النهائي معطل عبر صمام الأمان (Safety Gate Active) لحماية السجلات السريرية"
              >
                {deleting ? bi('جاري التحقق...', 'Verifying...') : bi('الحذف النهائي معطل عبر صمام الأمان', 'Permanent Deletion Disabled (Safety Gate)')}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => { setShowPermanentDeleteModal(false); setConfirmNameInput(''); }}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                إغلاق
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Profile Header Banner */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm mb-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          
          <div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-2">
              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                {p.fullNameAr || p.fullName}
              </h1>
              {p.fullNameAr && p.fullName !== p.fullNameAr && (
                <span className="text-sm text-slate-400 font-mono" dir="ltr">
                  ({p.fullName})
                </span>
              )}
              <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${
                p.gender === 'male' ? 'bg-sky-50 text-sky-700 border border-sky-200' : 'bg-pink-50 text-pink-700 border border-pink-200'
              }`}>
                {p.gender === 'male' ? bi('ذكر', 'Male') : bi('أنثى', 'Female')}
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Quick Metadata Pill Badges */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-y-2 gap-x-4 text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-3">
              {p.age !== undefined && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{bi('العمر:', 'Age:')} <strong className="text-slate-800">{p.age} {bi('سنة', 'yrs')}</strong></span>
                  {p.dateOfBirth && <span className="text-slate-400">({p.dateOfBirth})</span>}
                </div>
              )}

              {p.dominantHand && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{bi('اليد المهيمنة:', 'Dominant Hand:')} <strong className="text-slate-800">{p.dominantHand === 'right' ? bi('اليمنى', 'Right') : p.dominantHand === 'left' ? bi('اليسرى', 'Left') : bi('كلتاهما', 'Ambidextrous')}</strong></span>
                </div>
              )}

              {p.phoneNumber && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <span dir="ltr">{p.phoneNumber}</span>
                </div>
              )}
