              {p.occupation && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{p.occupation}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {p.address && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{p.address}</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="text-left md:border-r md:pr-6 border-slate-100 flex flex-col justify-center">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{bi('رقم هوية السجل المشفر (UUID):', 'Encrypted Record ID (UUID):')}</span>
            <code className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md select-all mt-1">
              {patient.id}
            </code>
            <span className="text-xs text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 font-medium">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{bi('مُشفَّر بـ AES-256-GCM (تشفير حمولة السجل)', 'Encrypted with AES-256-GCM')}</span>
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 leading-tight">
              {bi('حمولة السجل بالكامل مُشفَّرة — الحقول الفردية غير مُشفَّرة باستقلالية', 'Full payload encryption at rest')}
            </span>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Red Flags Alert Box (If present) */}
      {hasRedFlags && (
        <div className="mb-6 p-5 bg-rose-50 border-2 border-rose-300 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-rose-900 mb-1">
                {bi('تنبيه سريري هام: علامات حمراء ومحاذير حرجة', 'Important Clinical Alert: Red Flags')}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-rose-800 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                {p.redFlags}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Clinical Data Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
        
        {/* Left Column: Medical History & Diagnoses */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          
          {/* Medical History Card */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 text-slate-900 font-bold text-base border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
              <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-primary-600" />
              <span>{bi('التاريخ المرضي وطبيعة الإصابة', 'Medical History & Presentation')}</span>
            </div>
            {p.medicalHistory ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                {p.medicalHistory}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">لم يتم تدوين تاريخ مرضي حتى الآن.</p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Surgeries & Medications */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 text-slate-900 font-bold text-sm">
                <Scissors className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
                <span>{bi('العمليات الجراحية السابقة', 'Previous Surgeries')}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                {p.previousSurgeries || 'لا يوجد جراحات مسجلة.'}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 text-slate-900 font-bold text-sm">
                <Pill className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                <span>{bi('الأدوية الحالية المستمرة', 'Current Medications')}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                {p.currentMedications || 'لا توجد أدوية مسجلة.'}
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Precautions & Psychosocial */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 text-slate-900 font-bold text-base border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
              <Activity className="w-5 h-5 text-primary-600" />
              <span>{bi('محاذير العلاج والعوامل النفسية والاجتماعية', 'Precautions & Psychosocial Factors')}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-4">
              {p.precautions && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">المحاذير السريرية:</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 bg-amber-50/70 p-3 rounded-xl border border-amber-200/60 leading-relaxed">
                    {p.precautions}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {p.psychosocialFactors && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">العوامل النفسية الاجتماعية (Yellow Flags):</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 leading-relaxed">
                    {p.psychosocialFactors}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {p.generalNotes && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">ملاحظات عامة:</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 leading-relaxed">
                    {p.generalNotes}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Quick Clinical Alerts & Episodes */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm border-b border-slate-100 pb-2">
              {bi('التنبيهات السريرية السريعة', 'Clinical Alerts')}
            </h3>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{bi('الحساسية المعروفة:', 'Known Allergies:')}</span>
              <span className="inline-block px-3 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium">
                {p.allergies || 'لا توجد حساسية مسجلة (NKDA)'}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 block mb-1">{bi('الأمراض المصاحبة:', 'Comorbidities:')}</span>
              <span className="inline-block px-3 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium">
                {p.comorbidities || 'لا توجد أمراض مزمنة مصاحبة'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Episodes Summary Card */}
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-primary-50 to-teal-50 rounded-xl p-6 border border-primary-200/80 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-primary-900 font-bold text-sm">
                <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-primary-600" />
                <span>{bi('حلقات الرعاية النشطة', 'Active Episodes')}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-primary-600 text-white">
                {activeEpisodes.length}
              </span>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-4">
              {activeEpisodes.length > 0
                ? `توجد ${activeEpisodes.length} حلقة علاجية جارية لهذا المريض.`
                : 'لا توجد حلقة علاجية نشطة حالياً. يمكنك بدء حلقة جديدة وتحديد أهدافها السريرية.'}
            </p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEpisodeToEdit(null);
                setIsEpisodeModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 transition-colors shadow-sm"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{bi('بدء حلقة رعاية جديدة', 'Start New Episode')}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

      {/* ======================================================================= */}
      {/* Major Section: Episodes of Care (حلقات الرعاية السريرية)              */}
      {/* ======================================================================= */}
      <section className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-slate-200 shadow-sm mb-8">
        
        {/* Section Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-slate-100">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-primary-50 text-primary-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Layers className="w-5 h-5 text-primary-600" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {bi('حلقات الرعاية السريرية', 'Episodes of Care')}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  دورات العلاج الطبيعي والتأهيل المؤرخة، الأهداف المعتمدة، وسجل المتابعة
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setEpisodeToEdit(null);
              setIsEpisodeModalOpen(true);
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 shadow-sm hover:shadow transition-all"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{bi('بدء حلقة رعاية جديدة', 'Start New Episode')}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Filter Tabs */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-4 overflow-x-auto border-b border-slate-100">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEpisodeTabFilter('active')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              episodeTabFilter === 'active'
                ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('النشطة', 'Active')}</span>
            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] ${episodeTabFilter === 'active' ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-200'}`}>
              {activeEpisodes.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEpisodeTabFilter('on_hold')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              episodeTabFilter === 'on_hold'
                ? 'bg-amber-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('المعلقة', 'On Hold')}</span>
            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] ${episodeTabFilter === 'on_hold' ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-200'}`}>
              {onHoldEpisodes.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEpisodeTabFilter('completed')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              episodeTabFilter === 'completed'
                ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('المكتملة', 'Completed')}</span>
            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] ${episodeTabFilter === 'completed' ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-200'}`}>
              {completedEpisodes.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEpisodeTabFilter('all')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              episodeTabFilter === 'all'
                ? 'bg-primary-700 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('الكل', 'All')}</span>
            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] ${episodeTabFilter === 'all' ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-200'}`}>
              {episodes.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEpisodeTabFilter('archived')}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              episodeTabFilter === 'archived'
                ? 'bg-slate-700 text-white shadow-sm'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <span>{bi('الأرشيف', 'Archived')}</span>
            <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] ${episodeTabFilter === 'archived' ? 'bg-white/20' : 'bg-slate-200'}`}>
              {archivedEpisodes.length}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Episodes List */}
        <div className="pt-6">
          {loadingEpisodes ? (
            <div className="py-12 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">
              جاري فك تشفير حلقات الرعاية التابعة للمريض...
            </div>
          ) : filteredEpisodes.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-12 px-4 text-center rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50">
              <FolderOpen className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                لا توجد حلقات رعاية في هذا التصنيف
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto mb-4">
                ابدأ حلقة رعاية جديدة لتوثيق تشخيص المريض وتحديد أهدافه العلاجية وربط جلسات العلاج الطبيعي بها.
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setEpisodeToEdit(null);
                  setIsEpisodeModalOpen(true);
                }}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>بدء أول حلقة رعاية</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {filteredEpisodes.map((ep) => {
                const ed = ep.data;
                const specialtyInfo = CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES[ep.primarySpecialty] || CLINICAL_SPECIALTIES.general;
                const statusInfo = EPISODE_STATUSES[ep.status] || EPISODE_STATUSES.active;
                const isExpanded = expandedEpisodeId === ep.id;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={ep.id}
                    className="border border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 rounded-xl p-5 bg-white transition-all shadow-xs"
                  >
                    {/* Top Row: Title, Badges & Dates */}
                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
                          <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{ed.title}</h4>
                          
                          {/* Status Badge */}
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold border ${statusInfo.badgeClass}`}>
                            <span>{isEn ? statusInfo.en : statusInfo.ar}</span>
                          </span>

                          {/* Specialty Badge */}
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold border ${specialtyInfo.badgeClass}`}>
                            <span>{isEn ? specialtyInfo.en : specialtyInfo.ar}</span>
                          </span>

                          {ed.isOngoingLegacyEpisode && (
                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200">
                              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-600" />
                              <span>حلقة مستمرة منقولة</span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        {/* Dates row */}
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>تاريخ البدء: <strong className="text-slate-700">{ep.startDate || '—'}</strong></span>
                          </span>
                          {ep.endDate && (
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500" />
                              <span>تاريخ التخريج: <strong className="text-slate-700">{ep.endDate}</strong></span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>المنطقة التشريحية: <strong className="text-slate-700">{ed.affectedBodyRegion}</strong></span>
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* Expand / Collapse toggle */}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setExpandedEpisodeId(isExpanded ? null : ep.id)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors self-start sm:self-center"
                      >
                        <span>{isExpanded ? bi('إخفاء التفاصيل', 'Hide Details') : bi('عرض التفاصيل السريرية', 'Show Details')}</span>
                        {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {/* Compact Diagnosis & Impression */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs bg-slate-50/70 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 mb-3">
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-0.5">{bi('التشخيص الطبي الأولي:', 'Primary Medical Diagnosis:')}</span>
                        <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{ed.medicalDiagnosis}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-0.5">{bi('الانطباع السريري:', 'Clinical Impression:')}</span>
                        <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{ed.clinicalImpression}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Expanded Section */}
                    {isExpanded && (
                      <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                        
                        {/* Goals Grid */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                          {/* Short-Term */}
                          <div className="bg-emerald-50/50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-100">
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-900 flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                              <span>{bi('الأهداف قصيرة المدى:', 'Short-Term Goals:')}</span>
                            </span>
                            {ed.rehabilitationGoals?.shortTerm?.length > 0 ? (
                              <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-700">
                                {ed.rehabilitationGoals.shortTerm.map((g, i) => (
                                  <li key={i}>{g}</li>
                                ))}
                              </ul>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">لا توجد أهداف محددة.</span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {/* Long-Term */}
                          <div className="bg-primary-50/50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-primary-100">
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-primary-900 flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
                              <Target className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
                              <span>{bi('الأهداف طويلة المدى والتخريج:', 'Long-Term Goals:')}</span>
                            </span>
                            {ed.rehabilitationGoals?.longTerm?.length > 0 ? (
                              <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-700">
                                {ed.rehabilitationGoals.longTerm.map((g, i) => (
                                  <li key={i}>{g}</li>
                                ))}
                              </ul>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">لا توجد أهداف محددة.</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Discharge Summary (If completed) */}
                        {ed.dischargeSummary && (
                          <div className="bg-blue-50/80 p-4 rounded-xl border border-blue-200">
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-900 flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5">
                              <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                              <span>{bi('ملخص الخروج والنتائج السريرية:', 'Discharge Summary:')}</span>
                            </span>
                            <p className="text-xs text-blue-950 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line font-medium">
                              {ed.dischargeSummary}
                            </p>
                            {ed.dischargeCriteriaSummary && (
                              <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-800 mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-blue-200/60">
                                <strong>المعايير المحققة:</strong> {ed.dischargeCriteriaSummary}
                              </p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Clinical Sessions & SOAP Notes Timeline */}
                        <EpisodeSessionsView episode={ep} patient={patient} />

                        {/* Action Buttons Row */}
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            {/* Edit Episode */}
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => {
                                setEpisodeToEdit(ep);
                                setIsEpisodeModalOpen(true);
                              }}
                              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
                            >
                              <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              <span>{bi('تعديل الحلقة', 'Edit Episode')}</span>
                            </button>

                            {/* Discharge / Close (if active or on hold) */}
                            {ep.status !== 'completed' && ep.status !== 'archived' && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setEpisodeToDischarge(ep)}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 border border-blue-200 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('إغلاق وتخريج', 'Discharge')}</span>
                              </button>
                            )}

                            {/* Put on Hold or Resume */}
                            {ep.status === 'active' && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setEpisodeStatus(ep.id, 'on_hold')}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-200 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <PauseCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('تعليق مؤقت', 'Hold')}</span>
                              </button>
                            )}

                            {ep.status === 'on_hold' && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setEpisodeStatus(ep.id, 'active')}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-200 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <PlayCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('استئناف الخطة', 'Resume')}</span>
                              </button>
                            )}

                            {ep.status === 'completed' && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setEpisodeStatus(ep.id, 'active')}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-teal-700 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 border border-teal-200 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('إعادة فتح الحلقة', 'Reopen Episode')}</span>
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            {/* Archive / Unarchive */}
                            {ep.status !== 'archived' ? (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => archiveEpisode(ep.id)}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <Archive className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('أرشفة الحلقة', 'Archive Episode')}</span>
                              </button>
                            ) : (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => unarchiveEpisode(ep.id)}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-primary-700 bg-primary-50 hover:bg-primary-100 transition-colors"
                              >
                                <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                <span>{bi('استعادة من الأرشيف', 'Restore from Archive')}</span>
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                      </div>
                    )}

                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </section>

      {/* Episode Creation & Edit Modal */}
      <EpisodeModal
        isOpen={isEpisodeModalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setIsEpisodeModalOpen(false);
          setEpisodeToEdit(null);
        }}
        patient={patient}
        episodeToEdit={episodeToEdit}
        onSaved={handleSaveEpisode}
      />

      {/* Episode Discharge Modal */}
      {episodeToDischarge && (
        <EpisodeDischargeModal
          isOpen={Boolean(episodeToDischarge)}
          onClose={() => setEpisodeToDischarge(null)}
          episode={episodeToDischarge}
          onConfirmDischarge={handleConfirmDischarge}
        />
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/SessionModal.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Clinical Session Modal (Phase 2B.2)
// Creation & Editing of Clinical Sessions with Active Episode Binding
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  X, 
  Calendar, 
  Clock, 
  FileText, 
  UserCheck, 
  AlertCircle, 
  Activity, 
  Save,
  Hash
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  SESSION_TYPES, 
  ATTENDANCE_STATUSES, 
  type SessionRecord, 
  type SessionClinicalData, 
  type SessionType, 
  type AttendanceStatus,
  type EpisodeRecord 
} from '../types/clinical';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';

interface SessionModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (data: Partial<SessionClinicalData>) => Promise<void>;
  episode: EpisodeRecord;
  sessionToEdit?: SessionRecord | null;
  existingSessionCount: number;
}

export const SessionModal: React.FC<SessionModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  episode,
  sessionToEdit,
  existingSessionCount,
}) => {
  const { isRtl, bi } = useLanguage();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Form State
  const [customSessionNumber, setCustomSessionNumber] = useState<string>('');
  const [sessionType, setSessionType] = useState<SessionType>('follow_up');
  const [sessionDate, setSessionDate] = useState<string>(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [sessionTime, setSessionTime] = useState<string>('10:00');
  const [sessionDurationMinutes, setSessionDurationMinutes] = useState<number>(45);
  const [attendanceStatus, setAttendanceStatus] = useState<AttendanceStatus>('attended');
  const [cancellationReason, setCancellationReason] = useState<string>('');
  const [therapistName, setTherapistName] = useState<string>('د. المعالج الطبيعي');
  const [administrativeNotes, setAdministrativeNotes] = useState<string>('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (sessionToEdit) {
      const d = sessionToEdit.data;
      setCustomSessionNumber(sessionToEdit.sessionNumber ? String(sessionToEdit.sessionNumber) : '');
      setSessionType(sessionToEdit.sessionType);
      setSessionDate(sessionToEdit.sessionDate);
      setSessionTime(d.sessionTime || '10:00');
      setSessionDurationMinutes(d.sessionDurationMinutes || 45);
      setAttendanceStatus(sessionToEdit.attendanceStatus);
      setCancellationReason(d.cancellationReason || '');
      setTherapistName(d.therapistName || 'د. المعالج الطبيعي');
      setAdministrativeNotes(d.administrativeNotes || '');
    } else {
      // Defaults for new session
      const isFirst = existingSessionCount === 0;
      setCustomSessionNumber('');
      setSessionType(isFirst ? 'initial_evaluation' : 'follow_up');
      setSessionDate(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
      setSessionTime('10:00');
      setSessionDurationMinutes(45);
      setAttendanceStatus('attended');
      setCancellationReason('');
      setTherapistName('د. المعالج الطبيعي');
      setAdministrativeNotes('');
    }
    setError(null);
  }, [sessionToEdit, existingSessionCount, isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (!sessionDate) {
      setError(bi('تاريخ الجلسة إلزامي.', 'Session Date is required.'));
      return;
    }

    if (attendanceStatus !== 'attended' && !cancellationReason.trim()) {
      setError('سبب عدم الحضور أو الإلغاء إلزامي.');
      return;
    }

    const parsedNumber = customSessionNumber.trim() ? parseInt(customSessionNumber.trim(), 10) : undefined;
    if (parsedNumber !== undefined && (isNaN(parsedNumber) || parsedNumber < 1)) {
      setError('رقم الجلسة يجب أن يكون رقماً صحيحاً أكبر من 0.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload: Partial<SessionClinicalData> = {
        sessionNumber: parsedNumber,
        sessionType,
        sessionDate,
        sessionTime,
        sessionDurationMinutes: Number(sessionDurationMinutes),
        attendanceStatus,
        cancellationReason: attendanceStatus !== 'attended' ? cancellationReason.trim() : undefined,
        therapistName: therapistName.trim(),
        administrativeNotes: administrativeNotes.trim() || undefined,
      };

      await onSave(payload);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Session save error:', err);
      setError(err?.message || 'حدث خطأ أثناء حفظ الجلسة.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const calculatedSessionNumber = sessionToEdit 
    ? sessionToEdit.sessionNumber 
    : existingSessionCount + 1;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl max-w-2xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden my-6">
        
        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="px-6 py-5 bg-gradient-to-r from-primary-900 to-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center text-primary-300">
              <Activity className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold">
                  {sessionToEdit ? bi('تعديل بيانات الجلسة السريرية', 'Edit Session Details') : bi('تسجيل جلسة علاجية جديدة', 'New Clinical Session')}
                </h3>
                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-mono bg-primary-500/30 text-primary-200 border border-primary-400/30">
                  {bi(`الجلسة #${calculatedSessionNumber}`, `Session #${calculatedSessionNumber}`)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
                {bi('ضمن حلقة:', 'Episode:')} <strong className="text-white">{episode.data.title}</strong>
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-slate-300 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-5 max-h-[75vh] overflow-y-auto">
          
          {error && (
            <div className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl flex items-start gap-3 text-rose-800 text-xs sm:text-sm">
              <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Session Type Radio Selection */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-2">
              {bi('نوع الجلسة العلاجية', 'Session Type')} <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
              {(Object.keys(SESSION_TYPES) as SessionType[]).map((typeKey) => {
                const info = SESSION_TYPES[typeKey];
                const isSelected = sessionType === typeKey;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={typeKey}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSessionType(typeKey)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-right transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-50/50 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-primary-500'
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 bg-white'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{info.ar}</span>
                      <span className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center ${isSelected ? 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-600' : 'border-slate-300'}`}>
                        {isSelected && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white" />}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500" dir="ltr">{info.en}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1 leading-tight">{info.descriptionAr}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Session Number, Date, Time & Duration Grid */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3.5 pt-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Hash className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                  <span>رقم الجلسة</span>
                </span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="1"
                placeholder={`تلقائي (${calculatedSessionNumber})`}
                value={customSessionNumber}
                onChange={(e) => setCustomSessionNumber(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                  <span>{bi('تاريخ الجلسة', 'Session Date')}</span>
                </span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={sessionDate}
                onChange={(e) => setSessionDate(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                  <span>وقت الانعقاد</span>
                </span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="time"
                value={sessionTime}
                onChange={(e) => setSessionTime(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                  <span>المدة (بالدقائق)</span>
                </span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="10"
                max="180"
                step="5"
                value={sessionDurationMinutes}
                onChange={(e) => setSessionDurationMinutes(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Attendance Status */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-2">
              حالة الحضور والتنفيذ (Attendance Status)
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
              {(Object.keys(ATTENDANCE_STATUSES) as AttendanceStatus[]).map((statusKey) => {
                const info = ATTENDANCE_STATUSES[statusKey];
                const isSelected = attendanceStatus === statusKey;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={statusKey}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setAttendanceStatus(statusKey)}
                    className={`py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-primary-600 bg-primary-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{info.ar}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Cancellation Reason (if not attended) */}
          {attendanceStatus !== 'attended' && (
            <div className="p-3.5 bg-amber-50 rounded-xl border border-amber-200">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-900 mb-1">
                سبب الإلغاء أو التخلف عن الحضور <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={cancellationReason}
                onChange={(e) => setCancellationReason(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="وضح سبب الإلغاء أو عدم الحضور لأرشفته في سجل المريض..."
                rows={2}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-amber-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 bg-white"
                required
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Therapist Name */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                <span>المعالج الطبيعي المسؤول</span>
              </span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={therapistName}
              onChange={(e) => setTherapistName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="اسم المعالج الموثق للجلسة..."
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Administrative Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                <span>ملاحظات إدارية وتنظيمية (اختياري — غير سريرية)</span>
              </span>
            </label>
            <textarea
              value={administrativeNotes}
              onChange={(e) => setAdministrativeNotes(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="أي ملاحظات تخص المواعيد، التأخير، أو الترتيبات الإدارية..."
              rows={2}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Actions Bar */}
          <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
            >
              إلغاء
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={submitting}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-primary-600 hover:bg-primary-700 shadow-sm transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{submitting ? 'جاري الحفظ المشفر...' : 'حفظ الجلسة كمسودة'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/SoapModal.tsx ===
// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — SOAP Clinical Documentation Modal (Phase 2C — v2.0)
// 1:1 Session-to-SOAP Documentation, 4-Axis Clinical Workflow & Immutability
// shadcn/ui Clinical Standard: Clean Typography, Neutral Palette, Zero AI Slop
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  X, 
  Save, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle, 
  FileEdit, 
  Lock, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Calendar, 
  Sliders, 
  Compass,
  Dumbbell,
  Zap,
  ClipboardList
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  SESSION_TYPES, 
  type SessionRecord, 
  type SoapNoteRecord, 
  type SoapNoteData, 
  type SoapObjectiveData,
  type PatientRecord,
  type EpisodeRecord,
  type ObjectiveAssessmentStatus,
  type PatientBodyChartData,
  type RomAssessmentBatteryData,
  type MmtAssessmentBatteryData,
  type NeuroScreeningBatteryData,
} from '../types/clinical';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import { PatientBodyChart } from './toolkit/PatientBodyChart';
import { RomMmtAssessmentPanel } from './toolkit/RomMmtAssessmentPanel';
import { NeuroScreeningPanel } from './toolkit/NeuroScreeningPanel';

interface SoapModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  session: SessionRecord;
  episode: EpisodeRecord;
  patient: PatientRecord;
  existingSoap: SoapNoteRecord | null;
  onSaveSoap: (data: SoapNoteData) => Promise<SoapNoteRecord>;
  onFinalizeSession: (sessionId: string, therapistName: string) => Promise<void>;
  onOpenAddendumModal?: () => void;
}

export const SoapModal: React.FC<SoapModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  session,
  episode,
  patient,
  existingSoap,
  onSaveSoap,
  onFinalizeSession,
  onOpenAddendumModal,
}) => {
  const { isRtl, bi } = useLanguage();

  // Active Main Tab
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'subjective' | 'objective' | 'assessment' | 'plan' | 'preview'>('subjective');

  // Sub-Tab inside Objective (O)
  const [objectiveSubTab, setObjectiveSubTab] = useState<'general' | 'rom' | 'mmt' | 'neuro'>('general');

  // Form Submission & Finalization state
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successNotice, setSuccessNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showFinalizeConfirm, setShowFinalizeConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [therapistSignature, setTherapistSignature] = useState(session.data.therapistName || 'د. المعالج الطبيعي');

  // Subjective (S) State
  const [chiefComplaint, setChiefComplaint] = useState('');
  const [symptomChangesSinceLastSession, setSymptomChangesSinceLastSession] = useState('');
  const [painNrsScore, setPainNrsScore] = useState<number>(4);
  const [painScoreAtRest, setPainScoreAtRest] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const [painScoreWithMovement, setPainScoreWithMovement] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const [painScoreBestLast24h, setPainScoreBestLast24h] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const [painScoreWorstLast24h, setPainScoreWorstLast24h] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const [painContextNotes, setPainContextNotes] = useState('');
  const [painLocationDescription, setPainLocationDescription] = useState('');
  const [bodyChart, setBodyChart] = useState<PatientBodyChartData | undefined>(undefined);
  const [homeExerciseCompliance, setHomeExerciseCompliance] = useState<'full' | 'partial' | 'non_compliant' | 'not_prescribed'>('full');
  const [homeExerciseComplianceNotes, setHomeExerciseComplianceNotes] = useState('');
  const [responseToPreviousTreatment, setResponseToPreviousTreatment] = useState('');

  // Objective (O) State
  const [objectiveStatus, setObjectiveStatus] = useState<ObjectiveAssessmentStatus>('assessed');
  const [notAssessedReason, setNotAssessedReason] = useState('');
  const [objectiveFindings, setObjectiveFindings] = useState('');
  const [clinicalObservations, setClinicalObservations] = useState('');
  const [functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements, setFunctionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements] = useState('');
  const [romBattery, setRomBattery] = useState<RomAssessmentBatteryData | undefined>(undefined);
  const [mmtBattery, setMmtBattery] = useState<MmtAssessmentBatteryData | undefined>(undefined);
  const [neuroScreening, setNeuroScreening] = useState<NeuroScreeningBatteryData | undefined>(undefined);
  const [dailyInterventionsSummary, setDailyInterventionsSummary] = useState('');

  // Assessment (A) State
  const [clinicalImpressionCurrent, setClinicalImpressionCurrent] = useState('');
  const [progressStatus, setProgressStatus] = useState<'improved' | 'unchanged' | 'regressed' | 'plateau'>('improved');
  const [patientTolerance, setPatientTolerance] = useState<'well_tolerated' | 'moderate_fatigue' | 'poorly_tolerated'>('well_tolerated');
  const [factorsImpactingProgress, setFactorsImpactingProgress] = useState('');

  // Plan (P) State
  const [nextSessionPlan, setNextSessionPlan] = useState('');
  const [proposedModifications, setProposedModifications] = useState('');
  const [homeInstructions, setHomeInstructions] = useState('');
  const [nextAppointmentDate, setNextAppointmentDate] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (existingSoap) {
      const d = existingSoap.data;
      const s = d.subjective || ({} as any);
      const o = d.objective || ({} as any);
      const a = d.assessment || ({} as any);
      const p = d.plan || ({} as any);

      setChiefComplaint(s.chiefComplaint || '');
      setSymptomChangesSinceLastSession(s.symptomChangesSinceLastSession || '');
      const nrs = s.painNrsScore ?? s.painVasCurrent ?? s.painAssessment?.score ?? 4;
      setPainNrsScore(nrs);
      setPainScoreAtRest(s.painAssessment?.scoreAtRest);
      setPainScoreWithMovement(s.painAssessment?.scoreWithMovement);
      setPainScoreBestLast24h(s.painAssessment?.scoreBestLast24h ?? s.painVasBestLast24h);
      setPainScoreWorstLast24h(s.painAssessment?.scoreWorstLast24h ?? s.painVasWorstLast24h);
      setPainContextNotes(s.painAssessment?.contextNotes || '');
      setPainLocationDescription(s.painLocationDescription || '');
      setBodyChart(s.bodyChart);
      setHomeExerciseCompliance(s.homeExerciseCompliance || 'full');
      setHomeExerciseComplianceNotes(s.homeExerciseComplianceNotes || '');
      setResponseToPreviousTreatment(s.responseToPreviousTreatment || '');

      const objStat: ObjectiveAssessmentStatus = o.status || (o.isWithinNormalLimits ? 'normal_findings' : (o.notAssessedReason ? 'not_assessed' : 'assessed'));
      setObjectiveStatus(objStat);
      setNotAssessedReason(o.notAssessedReason || '');
      setObjectiveFindings(o.objectiveFindings || '');
      setClinicalObservations(o.clinicalObservations || '');
      setFunctionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements(o.functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements || '');
      setRomBattery(o.romBattery);
      setMmtBattery(o.mmtBattery);
      setNeuroScreening(o.neuroScreening);
      setDailyInterventionsSummary(o.dailyInterventionsSummary || '');

      setClinicalImpressionCurrent(a.clinicalImpressionCurrent || '');
      setProgressStatus(a.progressStatus || 'improved');
      setPatientTolerance(a.patientTolerance || 'well_tolerated');
      setFactorsImpactingProgress(a.factorsImpactingProgress || '');

      setNextSessionPlan(p.nextSessionPlan || '');
      setProposedModifications(p.proposedModifications || '');
      setHomeInstructions(p.homeInstructions || '');
      setNextAppointmentDate(p.nextAppointmentDate || '');
    } else {
      // Clean defaults for fresh note
      setChiefComplaint(bi(
        `ألم في منطقة ${episode.data.affectedBodyRegion || 'العضو المصاب'}، وصعوبة في الحركة الوظيفية.`,
        `Pain in ${episode.data.affectedBodyRegion || 'affected area'} with functional mobility limitations.`
      ));
      setSymptomChangesSinceLastSession('');
      setPainNrsScore(4);
      setBodyChart(undefined);
      setRomBattery(undefined);
      setMmtBattery(undefined);
      setNeuroScreening(undefined);
      setObjectiveStatus('assessed');
      setClinicalImpressionCurrent('');
      setNextSessionPlan('');
    }
    setError(null);
    setSuccessNotice(null);
  }, [existingSoap, episode, session, isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isFinalized = session.status === 'finalized' || existingSoap?.data.isFinalized === true;
  const sessionTypeInfo = SESSION_TYPES[session.sessionType] || SESSION_TYPES.follow_up;

  // Build Payload
  const constructPayload = (): SoapNoteData => {
    const objectiveData: SoapObjectiveData = {
      status: objectiveStatus,
      notAssessedReason: objectiveStatus === 'not_assessed' ? notAssessedReason.trim() : undefined,
      isWithinNormalLimits: objectiveStatus === 'normal_findings',
      clinicalObservations: clinicalObservations.trim() || undefined,
      objectiveFindings: objectiveFindings.trim() || undefined,
      functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements: functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements.trim() || undefined,
      romBattery,
      mmtBattery,
      neuroScreening,
      dailyInterventionsSummary: dailyInterventionsSummary.trim() || undefined,
    };

    return {
      subjective: {
        chiefComplaint: chiefComplaint.trim(),
        symptomChangesSinceLastSession: symptomChangesSinceLastSession.trim() || undefined,
        painNrsScore,
        painAssessment: {
          scale: 'NRS_0_10',
          score: painNrsScore,
          range: '0-10',
          unit: 'points',
          assessedAt: session.sessionDate,
          scoreAtRest: painScoreAtRest,
          scoreWithMovement: painScoreWithMovement,
          scoreBestLast24h: painScoreBestLast24h,
          scoreWorstLast24h: painScoreWorstLast24h,
          contextNotes: painContextNotes.trim() || undefined,
        },
        painLocationDescription: painLocationDescription.trim() || undefined,
        bodyChart,
        homeExerciseCompliance,
        homeExerciseComplianceNotes: homeExerciseComplianceNotes.trim() || undefined,
        responseToPreviousTreatment: responseToPreviousTreatment.trim() || undefined,
      },
      objective: objectiveData,
      assessment: {
        clinicalImpressionCurrent: clinicalImpressionCurrent.trim(),
        progressStatus,
        patientTolerance,
        factorsImpactingProgress: factorsImpactingProgress.trim() || undefined,
      },
      plan: {
        nextSessionPlan: nextSessionPlan.trim(),
        proposedModifications: proposedModifications.trim() || undefined,
        homeInstructions: homeInstructions.trim() || undefined,
        nextAppointmentDate: nextAppointmentDate || undefined,
      },
    };
  };

  const handleSaveDraft = async () => {
    if (isFinalized) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccessNotice(null);

    try {
      const payload = constructPayload();
      await onSaveSoap(payload);
      setSuccessNotice(bi('تم حفظ مسودة ملاحظات SOAP وتشفيرها بنجاح.', 'SOAP draft saved and encrypted successfully.'));
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessNotice(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || bi('فشل حفظ الملاحظة.', 'Failed to save note.'));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenFinalizeModal = () => {
    if (!chiefComplaint.trim()) {
      setError(bi('شكوى المريض الحالية إلزامية لاعتماد الجلسة.', 'Chief complaint is mandatory to finalize.'));
      setActiveTab('subjective');
      return;
    }

    const hasObj = Boolean(
      objectiveFindings.trim() ||
      clinicalObservations.trim() ||
      functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements.trim() ||
      dailyInterventionsSummary.trim() ||
      (romBattery?.entries && romBattery.entries.length > 0) ||
      (mmtBattery?.entries && mmtBattery.entries.length > 0) ||
      (neuroScreening && (
        (neuroScreening.myotomes && neuroScreening.myotomes.length > 0) ||
        (neuroScreening.dermatomes && neuroScreening.dermatomes.length > 0) ||
        (neuroScreening.reflexes && neuroScreening.reflexes.length > 0)
      )) ||
      objectiveStatus === 'normal_findings'
    );

    if (!hasObj && objectiveStatus !== 'not_assessed') {
      setError(bi('يجب تسجيل الفحص الموضوعي أو اختيار سبب عدم الفحص.', 'Objective examination findings or justification is required.'));
      setActiveTab('objective');
      return;
    }

    if (objectiveStatus === 'not_assessed' && !notAssessedReason.trim()) {
      setError(bi('سبب عدم إجراء الفحص الموضوعي إلزامي للاعتماد.', 'Reason for not assessing is required.'));
      setActiveTab('objective');
      return;
    }

    if (!clinicalImpressionCurrent.trim()) {
      setError(bi('الانطباع والتحليل السريري إلزامي لاعتماد الجلسة.', 'Clinical impression is mandatory to finalize.'));
      setActiveTab('assessment');
      return;
    }

    if (!nextSessionPlan.trim()) {
      setError(bi('خطة الجلسة القادمة إلزامية لاعتماد الجلسة.', 'Next session plan is mandatory to finalize.'));
      setActiveTab('plan');
      return;
    }

    setError(null);
    setShowFinalizeConfirm(true);
  };

  const handleConfirmFinalize = async () => {
    if (!therapistSignature.trim()) {
      setError(bi('اسم المعالج الطبيعي المعتمد إلزامي.', 'Therapist name is required.'));
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const payload = constructPayload();
      await onSaveSoap(payload);
      await onFinalizeSession(session.id, therapistSignature.trim());
      setShowFinalizeConfirm(false);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err?.message || bi('فشل اعتماد الجلسة.', 'Failed to finalize session.'));
      setShowFinalizeConfirm(false);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto" dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-5xl flex flex-col max-h-[92vh] overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        {/* Header Bar */}
        <div className="px-5 py-4 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">
              #{session.sessionNumber}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm sm:text-base">
                  {bi(`توثيق الجلسة السريرية #${session.sessionNumber}`, `Clinical Session Documentation #${session.sessionNumber}`)}
                </h3>
                <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold border ${
                  isFinalized
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300'
                    : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-300'
                }`}>
                  {isFinalized ? bi('معتمدة', 'Finalized') : bi('مسودة', 'Draft')}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                {patient.data.fullNameAr || patient.data.fullName} • {bi(sessionTypeInfo.ar, sessionTypeInfo.en)} • {session.sessionDate}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-center">
            {isFinalized && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-emerald-200">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                <span>{bi('سجل مشفر ومعتمد', 'Encrypted & Finalized')}</span>
              </span>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
              title={bi('إغلاق', 'Close')}
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4-Axis Navigation Tabs Bar */}
        <div className="px-5 pt-3 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex-shrink-0 overflow-x-auto">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {[
              { id: 'subjective', code: 'S', labelAr: 'الذاتي', labelEn: 'Subjective' },
              { id: 'objective', code: 'O', labelAr: 'الموضوعي', labelEn: 'Objective' },
              { id: 'assessment', code: 'A', labelAr: 'التقييم', labelEn: 'Assessment' },
              { id: 'plan', code: 'P', labelAr: 'الخطة', labelEn: 'Plan' },
              { id: 'preview', code: '✓', labelAr: 'معاينة التقرير', labelEn: 'Preview Report' },
            ].map((tab) => {
              const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={tab.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as any)}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-t-lg text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'border-primary-600 text-primary-700 bg-white shadow-2xs'
                      : 'border-transparent text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100/80'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center font-mono ${
                    isActive ? 'bg-primary-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                  }`}>
                    {tab.code}
                  </span>
                  <span>{bi(tab.labelAr, tab.labelEn)}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Status Notices */}
        {error && (
          <div className="mx-5 mt-3 p-3 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg flex items-start gap-2 text-rose-800 text-xs">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {successNotice && (
          <div className="mx-5 mt-3 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-800 text-xs">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>{successNotice}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab Body */}
        <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1 space-y-4">
          
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {/* TAB 1: SUBJECTIVE (S)                                                  */}
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {activeTab === 'subjective' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Chief Complaint */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('شكوى المريض الحالية', 'Chief Complaint')} <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={chiefComplaint}
                  onChange={(e) => setChiefComplaint(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('صف ما يشتكي منه المريض في بداية الجلسة...', 'Describe the patient complaint at session start...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Symptom Changes Since Last Session */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('تغير الأعراض منذ الجلسة السابقة', 'Symptom Changes Since Last Session')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={symptomChangesSinceLastSession}
                  onChange={(e) => setSymptomChangesSinceLastSession(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('كيف تطورت الأعراض ومستوى الألم منذ آخر جلسة؟...', 'How have symptoms progressed since the last visit?...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:outline-hidden disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Pain Scale (NRS 0–10) */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Sliders className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
                    <span>{bi('مقياس شدة الألم (0–10)', 'Pain Intensity Scale (0–10)')}</span>
                  </label>
                  <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-mono font-bold ${
                    painNrsScore <= 3
                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800'
                      : painNrsScore <= 6
                      ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                      : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800'
                  }`}>
                    {painNrsScore} / 10
                  </span>
                </div>

                <input
                  type="range"
                  min="0"
                  max="10"
                  step="1"
                  value={painNrsScore}
                  onChange={(e) => setPainNrsScore(Number(e.target.value))}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-200 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-primary-600"
                />
                <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-medium px-0.5">
                  <span>{bi('0 (لا يوجد ألم)', '0 (No Pain)')}</span>
                  <span>{bi('5 (ألم متوسط)', '5 (Moderate)')}</span>
                  <span>{bi('10 (أقصى ألم ممكن)', '10 (Worst Possible)')}</span>
                </div>

                {/* Pain at Rest & Movement */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">
                      {bi('الألم أثناء الراحة:', 'Pain at Rest (0–10):')}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      min="0"
                      max="10"
                      value={painScoreAtRest ?? ''}
                      onChange={(e) => setPainScoreAtRest(e.target.value === '' ? undefined : Number(e.target.value))}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      placeholder="0 - 10"
                      className="w-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-100"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1">
                      {bi('الألم مع الحركة:', 'Pain with Movement (0–10):')}
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      min="0"
                      max="10"
                      value={painScoreWithMovement ?? ''}
                      onChange={(e) => setPainScoreWithMovement(e.target.value === '' ? undefined : Number(e.target.value))}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      placeholder="0 - 10"
                      className="w-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-100"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Patient Body Chart */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1.5">
                  {bi('مخطط توثيق أعراض المريض السريري', 'Patient Body Chart')}
                </label>
                <PatientBodyChart
                  value={bodyChart}
                  onChange={setBodyChart}
                  readOnly={isFinalized}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Home Exercise Compliance */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  {bi('الالتزام بالبرنامج المنزلي', 'Home Exercise Compliance')}
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                  {[
                    { id: 'full', ar: 'التزام تام', en: 'Full Compliance' },
                    { id: 'partial', ar: 'التزام جزئي', en: 'Partial Compliance' },
                    { id: 'non_compliant', ar: 'غير ملتزم', en: 'Non-compliant' },
                    { id: 'not_prescribed', ar: 'لم يحدد بعد', en: 'Not Prescribed' },
                  ].map((item) => (
                    <button
                      key={item.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !isFinalized && setHomeExerciseCompliance(item.id as any)}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border text-center transition-all ${
                        homeExerciseCompliance === item.id
                          ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {bi(item.ar, item.en)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={homeExerciseComplianceNotes}
                  onChange={(e) => setHomeExerciseComplianceNotes(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('ملاحظات الالتزام بالتمارين...', 'Notes on exercise compliance...')}
                  className="w-full px-2.5 py-1 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-100"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Response to Previous Treatment */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('الاستجابة للعلاج السابق', 'Response to Prior Session')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={responseToPreviousTreatment}
                  onChange={(e) => setResponseToPreviousTreatment(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('استجابة المريض بعد الجلسة السابقة وملاحظات التعافي...', 'Patient response following the previous session...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {/* TAB 2: OBJECTIVE (O) — Clean Structured Sub-Tabs                        */}
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {activeTab === 'objective' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              
              {/* Objective Sub-Tabs Bar */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 p-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setObjectiveSubTab('general')}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    objectiveSubTab === 'general'
                      ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <ClipboardList className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{bi('الفحص والمشاهدات والتدخلات', 'General Exam & Interventions')}</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setObjectiveSubTab('rom')}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    objectiveSubTab === 'rom'
                      ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                  <span>{bi('المدى الحركي', 'Range of Motion (ROM)')}</span>
                  {romBattery?.entries && romBattery.entries.length > 0 && (
                    <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-teal-100 text-teal-800">
                      {romBattery.entries.length}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setObjectiveSubTab('mmt')}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    objectiveSubTab === 'mmt'
                      ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Dumbbell className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-600" />
                  <span>{bi('القوة العضلية', 'Muscle Strength (MMT)')}</span>
                  {mmtBattery?.entries && mmtBattery.entries.length > 0 && (
                    <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800">
                      {mmtBattery.entries.length}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setObjectiveSubTab('neuro')}
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    objectiveSubTab === 'neuro'
                      ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                      : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                  <span>{bi('الفحص العصبي للمريض', 'Neuro Screening')}</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Sub-Tab 1: General Exam & Interventions */}
              {objectiveSubTab === 'general' && (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {/* Objective Status Selector */}
                  <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1.5">
                      {bi('حالة الفحص الموضوعي للجلسة:', 'Objective Examination Status:')}
                    </label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setObjectiveStatus('assessed')}
                        disabled={isFinalized}
