                        className={`p-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition-all text-center ${
                          objectiveStatus === 'assessed'
                            ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-2xs'
                            : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {bi('فحص موضوعي مفصل', 'Detailed Objective Exam')}
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setObjectiveStatus('normal_findings')}
                        disabled={isFinalized}
                        className={`p-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition-all text-center ${
                          objectiveStatus === 'normal_findings'
                            ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-600 shadow-2xs'
                            : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {bi('سليم ضمن الحدود الطبيعية', 'Within Normal Limits')}
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setObjectiveStatus('not_assessed')}
                        disabled={isFinalized}
                        className={`p-2.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition-all text-center ${
                          objectiveStatus === 'not_assessed'
                            ? 'bg-amber-600 text-white border-amber-600 shadow-2xs'
                            : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {bi('لم يُجرَ فحص موضوعي', 'Not Assessed')}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {objectiveStatus === 'not_assessed' && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 space-y-1">
                      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-900">
                        {bi('سبب عدم إجراء الفحص الموضوعي (إلزامي للاعتماد):', 'Reason for Not Assessing (Mandatory):')}
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={notAssessedReason}
                        onChange={(e) => setNotAssessedReason(e.target.value)}
                        disabled={isFinalized}
                        placeholder={bi('ألم حاد يمنع الحركة، إجهاد شديد، أو جلسة استشارية...', 'Acute pain, extreme fatigue, or consultation visit...')}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs border border-amber-300 rounded bg-white focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500"
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {objectiveStatus === 'normal_findings' && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 text-xs text-emerald-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 flex-shrink-0" />
                      <span>{bi('تم توثيق نتائج الفحص السريري الموضوعي كسليمة وطبيعية بالكامل.', 'All objective examination findings documented as normal and within acceptable limits.')}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {objectiveStatus === 'assessed' && (
                    <>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                          {bi('المشاهدات السريرية والفحص العيني', 'Clinical Observations')}
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                          value={clinicalObservations}
                          onChange={(e) => setClinicalObservations(e.target.value)}
                          disabled={isFinalized}
                          placeholder={bi('المشية، القوام، وجود تورم موضعي، لون الجلد، علامات الإجهاد...', 'Gait, posture, localized edema, skin temperature, fatigue signs...')}
                          rows={2}
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                          {bi('نتائج الفحص البدني والجس', 'Physical Examination & Palpation')}
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                          value={objectiveFindings}
                          onChange={(e) => setObjectiveFindings(e.target.value)}
                          disabled={isFinalized}
                          placeholder={bi('نقاط الزناد العضلية، إيلام بالجس، أو ألم مع الحركة...', 'Palpation findings, trigger points, localized tenderness...')}
                          rows={2}
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                          {bi('القياسات الوظيفية والأولية', 'Functional & Preliminary Measurements')}
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                          value={functionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements}
                          onChange={(e) => setFunctionalOrPreliminaryMeasurements(e.target.value)}
                          disabled={isFinalized}
                          placeholder={bi('قياسات إضافية (مثل: اختبار الوقوف، التوازن، أو مسافة المشي)...', 'Additional functional tests (e.g. balance, walking distance)...')}
                          rows={2}
                          className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}

                  {/* Interventions Executed in Session */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                      {bi('التدخلات والعلاجات المنفذة في هذه الجلسة', 'Interventions & Modalities Applied')}
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      value={dailyInterventionsSummary}
                      onChange={(e) => setDailyInterventionsSummary(e.target.value)}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      placeholder={bi('العلاج اليدوي، التمارين العلاجية، الأجهزة والكمادات المستخدمة...', 'Manual therapy, therapeutic exercises, modalities, ice/heat applied...')}
                      rows={2}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Sub-Tab 2: Range of Motion (ROM) */}
              {objectiveSubTab === 'rom' && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <RomMmtAssessmentPanel
                    romBattery={romBattery}
                    mmtBattery={mmtBattery}
                    onChangeRom={setRomBattery}
                    onChangeMmt={setMmtBattery}
                    activeWorkflow="rom"
                    readOnly={isFinalized}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Sub-Tab 3: Muscle Strength (MMT) */}
              {objectiveSubTab === 'mmt' && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <RomMmtAssessmentPanel
                    romBattery={romBattery}
                    mmtBattery={mmtBattery}
                    onChangeRom={setRomBattery}
                    onChangeMmt={setMmtBattery}
                    activeWorkflow="mmt"
                    readOnly={isFinalized}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Sub-Tab 4: Neuro Screening (Myotomes / Dermatomes / Reflexes) */}
              {objectiveSubTab === 'neuro' && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <NeuroScreeningPanel
                    value={neuroScreening}
                    onChange={setNeuroScreening}
                    readOnly={isFinalized}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {/* TAB 3: ASSESSMENT (A)                                                  */}
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {activeTab === 'assessment' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Clinical Impression */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('الانطباع والتحليل السريري', 'Clinical Impression & Analysis')} <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={clinicalImpressionCurrent}
                  onChange={(e) => setClinicalImpressionCurrent(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('تحليل استجابة المريض السريرية وتفسير التغيرات...', 'Clinical analysis of patient response and functional changes...')}
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Progress Trajectory */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-2">
                  {bi('مسار وتطور الحالة:', 'Progress Trajectory:')}
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
                  {[
                    { id: 'improved', ar: 'تحسن سريري', en: 'Improved' },
                    { id: 'unchanged', ar: 'مستقرة دون تغيير', en: 'Unchanged' },
                    { id: 'plateau', ar: 'ثبات مرحلي', en: 'Plateau' },
                    { id: 'regressed', ar: 'تراجع', en: 'Regressed' },
                  ].map((item) => (
                    <button
                      key={item.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !isFinalized && setProgressStatus(item.id as any)}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border text-center transition-all ${
                        progressStatus === item.id
                          ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {bi(item.ar, item.en)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Patient Tolerance */}
              <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-2">
                  {bi('تحمل المريض للتمارين والعلاج:', 'Patient Tolerance:')}
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  {[
                    { id: 'well_tolerated', ar: 'تحمل ممتاز', en: 'Well Tolerated' },
                    { id: 'moderate_fatigue', ar: 'إجهاد عضلي متوسط', en: 'Moderate Fatigue' },
                    { id: 'poorly_tolerated', ar: 'تحمل ضعيف / زيادة الألم', en: 'Poorly Tolerated' },
                  ].map((item) => (
                    <button
                      key={item.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !isFinalized && setPatientTolerance(item.id as any)}
                      disabled={isFinalized}
                      className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border text-center transition-all ${
                        patientTolerance === item.id
                          ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-900 shadow-2xs'
                          : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {bi(item.ar, item.en)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Factors or Barriers */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('عوامل أو عقبات تعيق التقدم', 'Factors Impacting Progress')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={factorsImpactingProgress}
                  onChange={(e) => setFactorsImpactingProgress(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('عوامل بيئية، نوم، التزام، أو قلق حركي...', 'Psychosocial factors, sleep, workload, fear avoidance...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {/* TAB 4: PLAN (P)                                                         */}
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {activeTab === 'plan' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Next Session Plan */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('خطة الجلسة القادمة', 'Next Session Plan')} <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={nextSessionPlan}
                  onChange={(e) => setNextSessionPlan(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('التدخلات والتمارين المخطط تنفيذها في الزيارة القادمة...', 'Interventions and exercises planned for next visit...')}
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Proposed Modifications */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('التعديلات المقترحة على مسار الخطة', 'Proposed Modifications')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={proposedModifications}
                  onChange={(e) => setProposedModifications(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('أي تعديل في تكرار الجلسات أو الأهداف المرحلية...', 'Any adjustments to frequency or goals...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Home Instructions */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  {bi('التعليمات والتوجيهات المنزلية', 'Home Instructions & Advice')}
                </label>
                <textarea
                  value={homeInstructions}
                  onChange={(e) => setHomeInstructions(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  placeholder={bi('توجيهات الحركة، بيئة العمل، والكمادات ومحاذير الجهد...', 'Ergonomic advice, heat/ice, activity precautions...')}
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Next Appointment Date */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 mb-1">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                    <span>{bi('تاريخ الموعد القادم الموصى به', 'Recommended Next Appointment')}</span>
                  </span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={nextAppointmentDate}
                  onChange={(e) => setNextAppointmentDate(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isFinalized}
                  className="w-full sm:w-60 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-slate-50"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {/* TAB 5: PREVIEW (Clean Summary)                                          */}
          {/* ======================================================================= */}
          {activeTab === 'preview' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Header Banner */}
              <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-900 text-white rounded-lg flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm">
                    {bi(`ملخص تقرير التوثيق السريري — الجلسة #${session.sessionNumber}`, `Clinical Session Summary — Session #${session.sessionNumber}`)}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                    {bi('التاريخ:', 'Date:')} {session.sessionDate} • {bi('المعالج:', 'Clinician:')} {session.data.therapistName || bi('د. المعالج الطبيعي', 'Physical Therapist')}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold self-start sm:self-center ${
                  isFinalized ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/40' : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/40'
                }`}>
                  {isFinalized ? bi('سجل معتمد', 'Finalized') : bi('مسودة قيد التحرير', 'Draft')}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Grid 4 Axes */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                
                {/* S Block */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs block border-b border-slate-200 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>{bi('S — أقوال وشكوى المريض', 'Subjective (S)')}</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-slate-700 font-bold">{bi('شدة الألم:', 'Pain:')} {painNrsScore}/10</span>
                  </span>
                  <div>
                    <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('الشكوى الحالية:', 'Chief Complaint:')}</strong>
                    <p className="text-slate-800 leading-relaxed mt-0.5 whitespace-pre-line">{chiefComplaint || '—'}</p>
                  </div>
                  {symptomChangesSinceLastSession && (
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('تطور الأعراض:', 'Symptom Evolution:')}</strong>
                      <p className="text-slate-600 mt-0.5">{symptomChangesSinceLastSession}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {bodyChart && (bodyChart.markers.length > 0 || (bodyChart.clinicianSummaryNotes && bodyChart.clinicianSummaryNotes.trim())) && (
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                      <PatientBodyChart value={bodyChart} onChange={() => {}} readOnly={true} compactPreview={true} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* O Block */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs block border-b border-slate-200 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>{bi('O — الفحص والمشاهدات', 'Objective (O)')}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600">
                      {objectiveStatus === 'normal_findings'
                        ? bi('سليم ضمن الحدود', 'Within Normal Limits')
                        : objectiveStatus === 'not_assessed'
                        ? bi('لم يُجرَ فحص', 'Not Assessed')
                        : bi('فحص مفصل', 'Detailed Exam')}
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  {clinicalObservations && (
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('المشاهدات:', 'Observations:')}</strong>
                      <p className="text-slate-800 mt-0.5">{clinicalObservations}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {dailyInterventionsSummary && (
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('العلاج المنفذ:', 'Interventions Applied:')}</strong>
                      <p className="text-slate-800 mt-0.5">{dailyInterventionsSummary}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {/* Compact ROM & MMT */}
                  {((romBattery?.entries && romBattery.entries.length > 0) || (mmtBattery?.entries && mmtBattery.entries.length > 0)) && (
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                      <RomMmtAssessmentPanel romBattery={romBattery} mmtBattery={mmtBattery} compactPreview={true} readOnly={true} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {/* Compact Neuro */}
                  {neuroScreening && (
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                      <NeuroScreeningPanel value={neuroScreening} compactPreview={true} readOnly={true} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* A Block */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs block border-b border-slate-200 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>{bi('A — التحليل والتقييم', 'Assessment (A)')}</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">{progressStatus}</span>
                  </span>
                  <div>
                    <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('الانطباع السريري:', 'Clinical Impression:')}</strong>
                    <p className="text-slate-800 leading-relaxed mt-0.5 whitespace-pre-line">{clinicalImpressionCurrent || '—'}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('التحمل:', 'Tolerance:')}</strong>
                    <span className="text-slate-600">{patientTolerance}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* P Block */}
                <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs block border-b border-slate-200 pb-1">
                    {bi('P — الخطة العلاجية', 'Plan (P)')}
                  </span>
                  <div>
                    <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('الخطة القادمة:', 'Next Plan:')}</strong>
                    <p className="text-slate-800 leading-relaxed mt-0.5 whitespace-pre-line">{nextSessionPlan || '—'}</p>
                  </div>
                  {homeInstructions && (
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('التعليمات المنزلية:', 'Home Instructions:')}</strong>
                      <p className="text-slate-600 mt-0.5">{homeInstructions}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {nextAppointmentDate && (
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-700 block">{bi('الموعد القادم:', 'Next Appointment:')}</strong>
                      <span className="text-slate-800 font-bold">{nextAppointmentDate}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

              </div>
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

        {/* Modal Bottom Actions Bar */}
        <div className="px-5 py-3.5 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 transition-colors"
            >
              {bi('إغلاق', 'Close')}
            </button>

            {isFinalized && onOpenAddendumModal && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenAddendumModal}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold text-amber-800 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 transition-colors"
              >
                <FileEdit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                <span>{bi('إضافة ملحق سريري', 'Add Addendum')}</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {!isFinalized && (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleSaveDraft}
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 shadow-2xs transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
                  <span>{submitting ? bi('جاري الحفظ...', 'Saving...') : bi('حفظ كمسودة', 'Save Draft')}</span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleOpenFinalizeModal}
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 shadow-xs hover:shadow transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{bi('اعتماد الجلسة نهائياً', 'Finalize Session')}</span>
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Confirmation Modal for Finalization */}
      {showFinalizeConfirm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-60 bg-slate-900/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-5 max-w-md w-full shadow-2xl border border-emerald-200 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>

            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-1">
              {bi('تأكيد الاعتماد السريري وإقرار المعالج', 'Clinician Attestation & Integrity Lock')}
            </h4>
            
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-3">
              {bi(
                'باعتماد هذا السجل، فإنك تقر سريرياً بصحة ودقة البيانات المسجلة. سيتم حساب بصمة سلامة المحتوى (SHA-256 Digest) لتجميد السجل ضد أي تعديل مباشر، وأي تصحيح مستقبلي سيتطلب ملحقاً سريرياً.',
                'By finalizing this record, you clinically attest to the accuracy of the documented findings. A SHA-256 integrity digest will lock the record against direct edits, and subsequent changes will require a clinical addendum.'
              )}
            </p>

            <div className="mb-4">
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                {bi('اسم المعالج الطبيعي المعتمد:', 'Attesting Physical Therapist Name:')}
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={therapistSignature}
                onChange={(e) => setTherapistSignature(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={bi('اكتب اسم المعالج لاعتماد السجل...', 'Enter clinician name...')}
                className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs border border-slate-300 rounded-md focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-hidden"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmFinalize}
                disabled={submitting || !therapistSignature.trim()}
                className="flex-1 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {submitting ? bi('جاري الاعتماد...', 'Finalizing...') : bi('تأكيد إقرار المعالج واعتماد السجل', 'Confirm Attestation & Finalize')}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowFinalizeConfirm(false)}
                className="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                {bi('إلغاء', 'Cancel')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Synthetic Clinical Demonstration Data Modal
// Conforms to shadcn/ui visual standards & strict cryptographic invariants
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { usePatients } from '../context/PatientContext';
import { useVault } from '../context/VaultContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
import { SYNTHETIC_PATIENTS } from '../data/syntheticPatients';
import { EpisodeService } from '../services/episodeService';
import { SessionService } from '../services/sessionService';
import { SoapService } from '../services/soapService';
import { X, Database, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  EpisodeClinicalData,
  SessionClinicalData,
  SoapNoteData,
} from '../types/clinical';

interface SyntheticDataModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const SyntheticDataModal: React.FC<SyntheticDataModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}) => {
  const { patients, savePatient, refreshPatients } = usePatients();
  const { dek } = useVault();
  const { bi, isRtl } = useLanguage();
  const [seeding, setSeeding] = useState(false);
  const [successCount, setSuccessCount] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const handleSeedAll = async () => {
    setSeeding(true);
    setSuccessCount(null);
    try {
      let count = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < SYNTHETIC_PATIENTS.length; i++) {
        const patientData = SYNTHETIC_PATIENTS[i];
        const existing = patients.find(
          (p) => p.data.fullNameAr === patientData.fullNameAr || p.data.fullName === patientData.fullName
        );
        const savedPatient = await savePatient(patientData, existing?.id);
        count++;

        // For Patient 1 (Ahmed Al-Shammari - Knee ACL Rehab), seed Episode of Care + 2 Sessions + Full SOAP notes
        // Demonstrating Phase 2C Body Chart, ROM/MMT Batteries, and Longitudinal Progress Tracking
        if (i === 0 && dek) {
          try {
            const existingEpisodes = await EpisodeService.getEpisodesByPatient(savedPatient.id, dek);
            let episode = existingEpisodes.find((e) => e.status !== 'archived');

            if (!episode) {
              const episodeData: EpisodeClinicalData = {
                title: 'إعادة تأهيل الركبة اليمنى بعد ترميم الرباط الصليبي (Right ACL Rehab)',
                medicalDiagnosis: 'تمزق كامل بالرباط الصليبي الأمامي الأيمن بعد الجراحة التعويضية (ACL Reconstruction)',
                affectedBodyRegion: 'الركبة اليمنى',
                primarySpecialty: 'musculoskeletal',

                  shortTerm: [
                    'استعادة عطف الركبة حتى 120 درجة',

                    'تنشيط الرباعية دون عجز',
                  ],
                  longTerm: [

                    'استعادة القوة العضلية والعودة للنشاط الرياضي',
                  ],

            ))}
          </div>

            <div className="p-3.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-emerald-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 flex-shrink-0" />

                  `Successfully seeded ${successCount} patients with ACL rehabilitation progress sessions.`
                )}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-end gap-3">

                <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                {bi('جارٍ التشفير والإدراج...', 'Encrypting & Seeding...')}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {bi('إدراج كافة الحالات السريرية الخمس', 'Seed All 5 Clinical Cases')}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Interactive Clinical Reference Toolkit Modal (Phase 2C)
// High Z-Index (z-[70]) Overlay Modal Supporting Seamless Cross-Modal Reference
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import { DermatomesMapTab } from './DermatomesMapTab';
import { MyotomesTab } from './MyotomesTab';
import { DtrReferenceTab } from './DtrReferenceTab';
import { X, Layers, Activity, Stethoscope, ShieldCheck, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';

export type ToolkitTabId = 'dermatomes' | 'myotomes' | 'dtr';

interface ClinicalToolkitModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  initialTab?: ToolkitTabId;
}

export const ClinicalToolkitModal: React.FC<ClinicalToolkitModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  initialTab = 'dermatomes',
}) => {
  const { isRtl, language } = useLanguage();
  const isAr = language === 'ar';

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ToolkitTabId>(initialTab);

  // Sync tab if initialTab changes when opening
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && initialTab) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab);
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialTab]);

  // Handle ESC key press to close modal
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div 
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 md:p-6 bg-slate-900/70 backdrop-blur-xs overflow-y-auto"
      dir={isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="toolkit-modal-title"
    >
      <div 
        className="w-full max-w-5xl my-auto bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl sm:rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-col max-h-[94vh] overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        
        {/* Header Bar */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 flex-shrink-0">
          
          {/* Title and Icon */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-primary-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-primary-600/30 flex-shrink-0">
              <Stethoscope className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h2 id="toolkit-modal-title" className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {isAr ? 'المراجع السريرية التفاعلية' : 'Interactive Clinical Reference Toolkit'}
                </h2>
                <span className="hidden sm:inline-block text-[11px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-950 text-primary-700 dark:text-primary-300 border border-primary-200 dark:border-primary-800">
                  Phase 2C — Milestone 1
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {isAr ? 'دليل الفحص العصبي الشامل: القطاعات الجلدية، العضلية، والمنعكسات الوترية العميقة' : 'Comprehensive Neurological Reference: Dermatomes, Myotomes & Deep Tendon Reflexes'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Close Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            title={isAr ? 'إغلاق المرجع (Esc)' : 'Close toolkit (Esc)'}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors flex-shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Switcher Navigation */}
        <div className="bg-slate-100/80 dark:bg-slate-800/80 px-4 sm:px-6 py-2.5 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 overflow-x-auto flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            
            {/* Dermatomes Tab */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('dermatomes')}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'dermatomes'
                  ? 'bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{isAr ? 'خريطة القطاعات الجلدية (Dermatomes)' : 'Dermatomes Map'}</span>
            </button>

            {/* Myotomes Tab */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('myotomes')}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'myotomes'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <Activity className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{isAr ? 'القطاعات العضلية (Myotomes)' : 'Myotomes Reference'}</span>
            </button>

            {/* DTR Tab */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('dtr')}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap ${
                activeTab === 'dtr'
                  ? 'bg-primary-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <Stethoscope className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{isAr ? 'المنعكسات الوترية العميقة (DTR)' : 'Deep Tendon Reflexes'}</span>
            </button>

          </div>

          <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-primary-600" />
            <span>{isAr ? 'مراجع مفتوحة ومتاحة محلياً' : 'Local Offline Clinical Reference'}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Content Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6">
          {activeTab === 'dermatomes' && <DermatomesMapTab />}
          {activeTab === 'myotomes' && <MyotomesTab />}
          {activeTab === 'dtr' && <DtrReferenceTab />}
        </div>

        {/* Scientific Sources & Verification Footer */}
        <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-850 px-5 sm:px-6 py-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>
              {isAr
                ? 'المعايير المعتمدة: ASIA / ISNCSCI (2025) • Foerster (1933) • Lee et al. (2008) • Kendall (5th Ed) • NINDS Guidelines'
                : 'Validated Standards: ASIA / ISNCSCI (2025) • Foerster (1933) • Lee et al. (2008) • Kendall (5th Ed) • NINDS Guidelines'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 transition-colors"
          >
            {isAr ? 'إغلاق المرجع' : 'Close'}
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: src/components/toolkit/DermatomesMapTab.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import {
  DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS,
  DERMATOMAL_OVERLAP_GENERAL_NOTE_AR,
  DERMATOMAL_OVERLAP_GENERAL_NOTE_EN,
  getDermatomeByRoot
} from '../../data/clinicalToolkit/dermatomesData';
import type { SpinalLevel, SpinalRegionGroup } from '../../types/clinicalToolkit';
import { Search, Info, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

export const DermatomesMapTab: React.FC = () => {
  const { language } = useLanguage();
  const isAr = language === 'ar';

  const [activeRoot, setActiveRoot] = useState<SpinalLevel>('C6');
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<'anterior' | 'posterior'>('anterior');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedGroupFilter, setSelectedGroupFilter] = useState<SpinalRegionGroup | 'all'>('all');

  const selectedDermatome = useMemo(() => {
    return getDermatomeByRoot(activeRoot) || DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS[0];
  }, [activeRoot]);

  // Filtered roots based on search or group
  const searchResults = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return DERMATOME_DEFINITIONS.filter(d => {
      const matchGroup = selectedGroupFilter === 'all' || d.regionGroup === selectedGroupFilter;
      if (!q) return matchGroup;

      const matchRoot = d.rootLevel.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchEn = d.canonicalRegionEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) || d.keySensoryPointEn.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchAr = d.canonicalRegionAr.includes(q) || d.keySensoryPointAr.includes(q);
      return matchGroup && (matchRoot || matchEn || matchAr);
    });
  }, [searchQuery, selectedGroupFilter]);

  const handleSelectRoot = (root: SpinalLevel) => {
    setActiveRoot(root);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Overview & Clinical Notice */}
      <div className="bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/40 border border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3 text-sky-900 dark:text-sky-200 text-sm">
        <Info className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <p className="font-semibold text-sky-950 dark:text-sky-100">
            {isAr ? 'قاعدة التداخل الحسي للقطاعات الجلدية (Dermatomal Overlap)' : 'Clinical Dermatomal Overlap Rule'}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-sky-800 dark:text-sky-300 leading-relaxed">
            {isAr ? DERMATOMAL_OVERLAP_GENERAL_NOTE_AR : DERMATOMAL_OVERLAP_GENERAL_NOTE_EN}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search and Filters Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute top-3 start-3 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={
              isAr
                ? 'ابحث بالجذر (C6, L4..) أو بالمنطقة (Thumb, Knee, إبهام القدم..)'
                : 'Search by root (C6, L4..) or region (Thumb, Knee..)'
            }
            className="w-full ps-9 pe-4 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
              className="absolute top-2.5 end-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 px-1"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Region Group Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 p-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium overflow-x-auto">
          {(['all', 'cervical', 'thoracic', 'lumbar', 'sacral'] as const).map(group => (
            <button
              key={group}
              onClick={() => setSelectedGroupFilter(group)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                selectedGroupFilter === group
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-300 shadow-sm font-semibold'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              {group === 'all' && (isAr ? 'الكل' : 'All')}
              {group === 'cervical' && (isAr ? 'رقبي (C)' : 'Cervical')}
              {group === 'thoracic' && (isAr ? 'صدري (T)' : 'Thoracic')}
              {group === 'lumbar' && (isAr ? 'قطني (L)' : 'Lumbar')}
              {group === 'sacral' && (isAr ? 'عجزي (S)' : 'Sacral')}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid: Interactive Canvas + Detail Panel */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-start">
        {/* Left / Center: Interactive SVG Body Canvas (lg:col-span-6) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-6 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-col items-center relative">
          {/* View Toggle (Anterior / Posterior) */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 p-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 shadow-sm mb-4 self-center sm:self-start">
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveView('anterior')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                activeView === 'anterior'
                  ? 'bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              {isAr ? 'المشهد الأمامي (Anterior)' : 'Anterior View'}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveView('posterior')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                activeView === 'posterior'
                  ? 'bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              {isAr ? 'المشهد الخلفي (Posterior)' : 'Posterior View'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* SVG Canvas */}
          <div className="w-full max-w-[280px] h-[480px] relative select-none flex items-center justify-center">
            {activeView === 'anterior' ? (
              /* ANTERIOR VIEW SVG */
              <svg viewBox="0 0 200 420" className="w-full h-full drop-shadow-sm">
                {/* Head C2 */}
                <circle
                  cx="100" cy="35" r="22"
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all ${activeRoot === 'C2' ? 'fill-sky-400 stroke-sky-700 stroke-2' : 'fill-slate-200 dark:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-400 hover:fill-sky-200'}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C2')}
                />
                <text x="100" y="38" fontSize="9" textAnchor="middle" fill="#475569" className="font-bold pointer-events-none">C2</text>

                {/* Neck C3 */}
                <path
                  d="M 85 58 Q 100 64 115 58 L 125 75 Q 100 82 75 75 Z"
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all ${activeRoot === 'C3' ? 'fill-sky-400 stroke-sky-700 stroke-2' : 'fill-slate-200 dark:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-400 hover:fill-sky-200'}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C3')}
                />
                <text x="100" y="70" fontSize="8" textAnchor="middle" fill="#475569" className="font-semibold pointer-events-none">C3</text>

                {/* Shoulders C4 */}
                <path
                  d="M 75 75 L 50 85 L 58 100 L 78 90 Z"
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all ${activeRoot === 'C4' ? 'fill-sky-400 stroke-sky-700 stroke-2' : 'fill-slate-200 dark:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-400 hover:fill-sky-200'}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C4')}
                />
                <path
                  d="M 125 75 L 150 85 L 142 100 L 122 90 Z"
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all ${activeRoot === 'C4' ? 'fill-sky-400 stroke-sky-700 stroke-2' : 'fill-slate-200 dark:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-400 hover:fill-sky-200'}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C4')}
                />
                <text x="100" y="87" fontSize="7" textAnchor="middle" fill="#64748b" className="pointer-events-none">C4</text>

                {/* Upper Arms C5 */}
                <path
                  d="M 50 85 L 35 130 L 45 132 L 58 100 Z"
                  className={`cursor-pointer transition-all ${activeRoot === 'C5' ? 'fill-sky-400 stroke-sky-700 stroke-2' : 'fill-slate-200 dark:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-400 hover:fill-sky-200'}`}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C5')}

                  onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('S2')}

                      : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-600 hover:border-sky-400'

                >
                  {d.rootLevel}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

            <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pb-3">
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  {selectedDermatome.asiaCode || selectedDermatome.rootLevel}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-50 mt-1">
                  {isAr ? selectedDermatome.canonicalRegionAr : selectedDermatome.canonicalRegionEn}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleSelectRoot('C6')}
                title={isAr ? 'إعادة ضبط' : 'Reset'}
                className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Structured Table */}
            <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500">{isAr ? 'نقطة الفحص المعيارية (ASIA Point):' : 'Key Sensory Point:'}</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-end max-w-[65%]">
                  {isAr ? selectedDermatome.keySensoryPointAr : selectedDermatome.keySensoryPointEn}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-start justify-between bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500">{isAr ? 'المنطقة باللغة المقابلة:' : 'Secondary Language:'}</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-end max-w-[65%]">
                  {isAr ? selectedDermatome.canonicalRegionEn : selectedDermatome.canonicalRegionAr}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-start justify-between bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-500">{isAr ? 'المصدر العلمي:' : 'Scientific Source:'}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-end text-[11px]">
                  {selectedDermatome.primarySourceCitation}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Overlap & Clinical Pearl */}
            <div className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 border-s-4 border-amber-500 p-3 rounded-e-lg text-xs text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 space-y-1">
              <p className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                <span>💡</span>
                <span>{isAr ? 'ملاحظة التداخل والتباين السريري:' : 'Clinical Overlap & Anatomical Variation:'}</span>
              </p>
              <p className="leading-relaxed">
                {isAr ? selectedDermatome.overlapClinicalNotesAr : selectedDermatome.overlapClinicalNotesEn}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Deep Tendon Reflexes (DTR) Reference Tab Component (Phase 2C)
// Interactive Reference for Tendon Reflexes, NINDS 0–4+ Grading, and Jendrassik
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import {
  DTR_DEFINITIONS,
  NINDS_REFLEX_GRADES,
  DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_AR,
  DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_EN,
  JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_AR,
  JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_EN,
  getDtrById
} from '../../data/clinicalToolkit/dtrData';
import type { DtrReflexId, DtrGrade } from '../../types/clinicalToolkit';
import { Activity, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2, Stethoscope, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

export const DtrReferenceTab: React.FC = () => {
  const { language } = useLanguage();
  const isAr = language === 'ar';

  const [selectedReflexId, setSelectedReflexId] = useState<DtrReflexId>('patellar');
  const [selectedSimulatedGrade, setSelectedSimulatedGrade] = useState<DtrGrade>('2+');

  const selectedReflex = getDtrById(selectedReflexId) || DTR_DEFINITIONS[3]; // Default to Patellar
  const activeGradeObj = NINDS_REFLEX_GRADES.find(g => g.grade === selectedSimulatedGrade) || NINDS_REFLEX_GRADES[2];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* 1. Clinical Pearls: Asymmetry and Jendrassik Notices */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        
        {/* Asymmetry Rule Notice */}
        <div className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3 text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-200 text-sm">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <p className="font-semibold text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-100 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>{isAr ? 'قاعدة عدم التناظر بين الجانبين (Asymmetry Rule)' : 'Bilateral Asymmetry Clinical Rule'}</span>
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 leading-relaxed">
              {isAr ? DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_AR : DTR_ASYMMETRY_RULE_EN}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Jendrassik Maneuver Notice */}
        <div className="bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/40 border border-sky-200 dark:border-sky-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3 text-sky-950 dark:text-sky-200 text-sm">
          <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <p className="font-semibold text-sky-900 dark:text-sky-100 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>{isAr ? 'مناورة جيندراسيك للتعزيز (Jendrassik Maneuver)' : 'Jendrassik Reinforcement Maneuver'}</span>
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-sky-800 dark:text-sky-300 leading-relaxed">
              {isAr ? JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_AR : JENDRASSIK_MANEUVER_NOTE_EN}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* 2. Interactive Five Core Reflex Cards Selector */}
      <div>
        <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>{isAr ? 'اختر المنعكس الوتري العميق للفحص:' : 'Select Deep Tendon Reflex (DTR):'}</span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-normal text-slate-400">5 Essential Reflexes</span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3">
          {DTR_DEFINITIONS.map(dtr => {
            const isSelected = selectedReflexId === dtr.reflexId;
            return (
              <button
                key={dtr.reflexId}
                onClick={() => setSelectedReflexId(dtr.reflexId)}
                className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border text-start transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-primary-600 text-white border-primary-600 shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20 ring-2 ring-primary-500 ring-offset-1 dark:ring-offset-slate-900'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 dark:hover:border-slate-600'
                }`}
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                    <span className={`text-[11px] font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                        : 'bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-950 text-primary-700 dark:text-primary-300 border border-primary-200 dark:border-primary-800'
                    }`}>
                      {dtr.primaryRoot}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono ${isSelected ? 'text-primary-100' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>
                      {dtr.contributingRoots.join(', ')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm leading-tight mt-1">
                    {isAr ? dtr.nameAr : dtr.nameEn}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className={`text-[10px] mt-2 pt-1 border-t truncate ${

                }`}>

          })}
        </div>
      </div>

        {/* Title & Root Badges */}

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-primary-100 dark:bg-primary-950 text-primary-800 dark:text-primary-200">

                onClick={() => setSelectedSimulatedGrade(g.grade)}
                className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-center transition-all flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? g.grade === '0'
                      ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-600 shadow-md shadow-rose-600/20'
                      : g.grade === '4+'
                      ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600 shadow-md shadow-purple-600/20'
                      : 'bg-primary-600 text-white border-primary-600 shadow-md shadow-primary-600/20'
                    : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-lg font-black font-mono">{g.grade}</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-medium truncate w-full text-center">
                  {g.grade === '0' ? (isAr ? 'غائب' : 'Absent') :
                   g.grade === '1+' ? (isAr ? 'كليل / خامل' : 'Hypoactive') :
                   g.grade === '2+' ? (isAr ? 'طبيعي معتاد' : 'Normal') :
                   g.grade === '3+' ? (isAr ? 'نشط بزيادة' : 'Brisk') :
                   (isAr ? 'مفرط + رمع' : 'Clonus')}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Active Grade Detailed Interpretation Box */}
        <div className={`p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
          activeGradeObj.grade === '0'
            ? 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800 text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100'
            : activeGradeObj.grade === '4+'
            ? 'bg-purple-50 dark:bg-purple-950/40 border-purple-200 dark:border-purple-800 text-purple-950 dark:text-purple-100'
            : 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100'
        }`}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
            <span className="font-bold text-sm">
              {isAr ? activeGradeObj.labelAr : activeGradeObj.labelEn}
            </span>
            <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
              activeGradeObj.isPhysiological
                ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300'
                : 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300'
            }`}>
              {activeGradeObj.isPhysiological
                ? (isAr ? 'فسيولوجي / محتمل طبيعياً' : 'Physiological / May be Normal')
                : (isAr ? 'مرضي يستلزم التقصي' : 'Pathological Finding')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium">
            {isAr ? activeGradeObj.clinicalInterpretationAr : activeGradeObj.clinicalInterpretationEn}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 italic">
            {isAr ? activeGradeObj.clinicalInterpretationEn : activeGradeObj.clinicalInterpretationAr}
          </p>
        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Myotomes Clinical Reference Tab Component (Phase 2C)
// Interactive Reference for Myotomal Levels, Testing, and Differentiation
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import {
  MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS,
  MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_AR,
  MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_EN,
  getMyotomeByRoot
} from '../../data/clinicalToolkit/myotomesData';
import type { SpinalLevel, SpinalRegionGroup, MyotomeDefinition } from '../../types/clinicalToolkit';
import { Search, Info, ShieldAlert, Activity, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, Stethoscope } from 'lucide-react';

const ASIA_KEY_ROOTS: SpinalLevel[] = ['C5', 'C6', 'C7', 'C8', 'T1', 'L2', 'L3', 'L4', 'L5', 'S1'];

export const MyotomesTab: React.FC = () => {
  const { language } = useLanguage();
  const isAr = language === 'ar';

  const [activeRoot, setActiveRoot] = useState<SpinalLevel>('L5');
  const [selectedRegion, setSelectedRegion] = useState<SpinalRegionGroup | 'all'>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const selectedMyotome: MyotomeDefinition = useMemo(() => {
    return getMyotomeByRoot(activeRoot) || MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS[0];
  }, [activeRoot]);

  // Filtered definitions based on region and search query
  const filteredMyotomes = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS.filter(m => {
      const matchRegion = selectedRegion === 'all' || m.regionGroup === selectedRegion;
      if (!matchRegion) return false;
      if (!q) return true;

      const matchRoot = m.rootLevel.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchMovEn = m.predominantMovementEn.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchMovAr = m.predominantMovementAr.includes(q);
      const matchMuscle = m.keyMuscles.some(k =>
        k.nameEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        k.nameAr.includes(q) ||
        k.peripheralNerveEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        k.peripheralNerveAr.includes(q)
      );
      const matchDiff = m.differentialClinicalNotesEn.toLowerCase().includes(q) || m.differentialClinicalNotesAr.includes(q);

      return matchRoot || matchMovEn || matchMovAr || matchMuscle || matchDiff;
    });
  }, [selectedRegion, searchQuery]);

  const regionTabs: Array<{ id: SpinalRegionGroup | 'all'; labelAr: string; labelEn: string }> = [
    { id: 'all', labelAr: 'الكل (C1–S3)', labelEn: 'All Roots (C1–S3)' },
    { id: 'cervical', labelAr: 'الرقبي (C1–C8)', labelEn: 'Cervical (C1–C8)' },
    { id: 'thoracic', labelAr: 'الصدري (T1)', labelEn: 'Thoracic (T1)' },
    { id: 'lumbar', labelAr: 'القطني (L1–L5)', labelEn: 'Lumbar (L1–L5)' },
    { id: 'sacral', labelAr: 'العجزي (S1–S3)', labelEn: 'Sacral (S1–S3)' }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Multisegmental Innervation Pearl Notice */}
      <div className="bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200 text-sm">
        <Info className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <p className="font-semibold text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
            <span>
              {isAr ? 'القاعدة السريرية للتعصيب المشترك متعدد المستويات (Multisegmental Innervation)' : 'Multisegmental Innervation Clinical Principle'}
            </span>
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 leading-relaxed">
            {isAr ? MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_AR : MULTISEGMENTAL_INNERVATION_GENERAL_NOTE_EN}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search and Filters */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 absolute top-3 start-3 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={
              isAr
                ? 'ابحث بالجذر (L5, C5..)، الحركة (Dorsiflexion..)، أو العضلة (Tibialis anterior, Biceps..)'
                : 'Search by root (L5, C5..), movement, muscle, or nerve...'
            }
            className="w-full ps-9 pe-4 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
              className="absolute top-2.5 end-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 px-1"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Region selector buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto pb-1 sm:pb-0">
          {regionTabs.map(tab => (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedRegion(tab.id)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-colors ${
                selectedRegion === tab.id
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              {isAr ? tab.labelAr : tab.labelEn}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Root Selector Pills */}
      <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
        <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>{isAr ? 'اختر الجذر الشوكي السريع:' : 'Quick Select Spinal Root:'}</span>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 inline-block"></span>
            {isAr ? 'عضلة مفتاحية معتمدة (ASIA Key Myotome)' : 'ASIA Key Myotome'}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS.map(m => {
            const isSelected = activeRoot === m.rootLevel;
            const isAsiaKey = ASIA_KEY_ROOTS.includes(m.rootLevel);
            return (
              <button
                key={m.rootLevel}
                onClick={() => setActiveRoot(m.rootLevel)}
                className={`relative px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all flex items-center gap-1 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-sm ring-2 ring-emerald-600 ring-offset-1 dark:ring-offset-slate-900'
                    : isAsiaKey
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{m.rootLevel}</span>
                {isAsiaKey && !isSelected && (
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"></span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Content Layout: Active Root Card & Quick Directory */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        
        {/* Left / Main Column: Selected Myotome Detailed Card (Span 2) */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm space-y-5">
            
            {/* Header: Root Badge, Region, and ASIA Status */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-700 pb-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold text-xl flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20">
                  {selectedMyotome.rootLevel}
                </span>
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                      {selectedMyotome.regionGroup}
                    </span>
                    {ASIA_KEY_ROOTS.includes(selectedMyotome.rootLevel) && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                        ASIA / ISNCSCI Standard
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-1">
                    {isAr ? selectedMyotome.predominantMovementAr : selectedMyotome.predominantMovementEn}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {isAr ? selectedMyotome.predominantMovementEn : selectedMyotome.predominantMovementAr}
                  </p>
                </div>

            <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/60 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
                <Stethoscope className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
                <span>{isAr ? 'وضعية الفحص السريري وطريقة المقاومة:' : 'Clinical Testing Position & Technique:'}</span>

                  <button
                    key={m.rootLevel}

                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'

                          ? 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300'

                        </div>

                        </div>

                  </button>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );

// =======================================================================
// PhysioRecord — Patient Neurological Screening Panel (NeuroScreeningPanel)
// In-Session Clinical Recording Tool: Myotomes, Dermatomes & Reflexes
// shadcn/ui Clinical Standard: Zero Dual-Language Clutter & Clean UX
// =======================================================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../context/LanguageContext';
import { CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type {
  NeuroScreeningBatteryData,
  MyotomeMotorEntry,
  DermatomeSensoryEntry,
  DtrAssessmentEntry,
  SensoryFindingStatus,
  ReflexGradeValue,
  MmtGradeValue,
} from '../../types/clinical';

export interface NeuroScreeningPanelProps {
  value?: NeuroScreeningBatteryData;
  onChange?: (battery: NeuroScreeningBatteryData) => void;
  readOnly?: boolean;
  compactPreview?: boolean;
}

// Key Myotomes Root Levels
const DEFAULT_MYOTOME_DEFINITIONS: Array<{
  rootLevel: string;
  movementEn: string;
  movementAr: string;
}> = [
  { rootLevel: 'C5', movementEn: 'Elbow Flexion', movementAr: 'ثني المرفق' },
